veḷiyīṭu
tamiḻmaṇ patippakam
2, ciṅkārāvēlar teru
tiyākarāyar nakar, ceṉṉi -600 017
to.pē: 2433 9030



moḻi mīṭpiṉ mīḷ varavu
taṉit  tamiḻ vitti ūṉṟiyavar kālṭuvelār. ati muḷikkac ceytavar paritimāl kaliñar; ceṭiyāka vaḷarttavar niṟimaliyām maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ; maramāka vaḷarttu varupavaṉ yāṉē eṉṟa vīṟuṭiyār pāvāṇar.
kiṟittu perumāṉ camaya mīṭpar; kāralmārkkacu poruḷiyal mīṭpar; moḻimīṭpar yāṉē eṉṉum perumita mikkār pāvāṇar.
āriyattiṉiṉṟu tamiḻi mīṭpataṟkāka yāṉ arumpāṭu paṭṭu ilakkiya ilakkaṇa muṟiyōṭu kaṟṟa moḻikaḷ muppatu  eṉṟu eḻutiya perumitat tōṉṟal pāvāṇar.
māntaṉ tōṉṟiyatu kumarik kaṇṭattilēyē; avaṉ pēciya moḻiyē ulaka mutaṉmoḻi; āriyattiṟku mūlamum, tiraviṭattukkut tāyum tamiḻē eṉṉum mummaṇik koḷkiḷi nili nāṭṭiya maliyaṉṉa māṇpar pāvāṇar.
avar colliyavi eḻutiyavi aṉittum meymmiyiṉ pāṟpaṭṭaṉavē eṉa iṉṟu ulaka āyvup perumakkaḷāl ovvoṉṟāka meyppikkappaṭṭu varutal kaṇkūṭu.
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭit tam āyvu matukiyāl tēvanēya ūḻi ākkiya pukaḻum vēṇṭāp pukaḻ māmaṇi tēvanēyap pāvaṇar.
avar moḻiyāyvuc ceytikaḷ oru nūlil, ōr itaḻil, oru malaril, orukaṭṭuriyil, oru kaṭitattil, oru poḻivil ōr uriyāṭalil aṭaṅkiyavi alla. koṅku tēr vāḻkki añciṟit tumpiyāka avaṟṟi yellām tokuttu akara niralil tokukkappaṭṭa ariya tokuppē tēvanēyam ākum.
neṭṭa neṭuṅkālamākat tēvanēyattil ūṉṟiya yāṉ ataṉi akara niral tokiyākki veḷiyiṭal tamiḻulakukkup perumpayaṉām eṉṟu eṇṇiya kāliyil, tamiḻ moḻiyiyum tamiḻ maṇṇiyum tamiḻ iṉattiyum tāṅkip piṭittu ūkkum - vaḷarkkum - vaṇmiyarāy - pāvāṇarkku aṇukkarāy - avarāl uriyum pāṭṭum oruṅku koṇṭa peruntoṇṭarāyt  tikaḻnta ciṅka purivāḻ tamiḻttiru ve. kōvalaṅkaṇṇaṉār avarkaḷ tamiḻ viḻā oṉṟaṟkākac ceṉṉi vanta pōtu yāṉum,  muṉivar ku. tirumāṟaṉārum cantittu aḷavaḷāviya pōtu ikkarutti yāṉ urikka uṭaṉē pāvāṇar aṟakkaṭṭaḷi tōṟṟuvippatākavum ataṉ vaḻiyē tēvanēyam veḷik koṇaralām eṉavum kūṟi appoḻutēyē aṟakkaṭṭaḷi amittār.
tēvanēyar paṭippukaḷ aṉittilum uḷḷa collāyvukaḷit tiraṭṭi akara niral paṭuttip patiṉmūṉṟu tokutikaḷ ākkiṉēṉ. patippuk kuḻuvum amikkappaṭṭatu; acciṭum poṟuppu, pāvāṇar pala kālattup palavakiyāl veḷiyiṭṭa nūlkaḷiyum kaṭṭurikaḷiyum oruṅkē tokuttu orē nērattil veḷiyiṭṭa tamiḻmaṇ patippaka urimiyāḷar nāṭu moḻi iṉap pōrāḷi kō. iḷavaḻakaṉāriṭam oppaṭikkappaṭṭu veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu. attēvanēyam tamiḻ āyvar, tamiḻ mīṭpar aṉivar kikaḷilum irukka vēṇṭum eṉṉum vēṇavāvāl mīḷpatippākat tamiḻmaṇ patippakam veḷiyiṭukiṟatu.
pāvāṇar aṟakkaṭṭaḷi niṟuviya kōvalaṅkaṇṇaṉār pukaḻ uṭal eytiya niliyil, avar eṉṟum iṟavā vāḻviṉar eṉpati nilippaṭuttum vakiyil avarkkup paṭiyalākki ippatippu veḷippaṭukiṉṟatu.
moḻi iṉa nāṭṭup paṟṟāḷar aṉivariṭamum irukka vēṇṭiya nūl, pal patippukaḷ kāṇa vēṇṭum. varuṅkāla iḷiñarkku vaḻikāṭṭum oḷiviḷakkākat tikaḻa vēṇṭum. ataṟkut tūṇṭalum tulakkalumāka irukka vēṇṭiyavarkaḷ tamiḻ mīṭṭeṭuppup paṟṟumiyarum toṇṭarumāvar.
veḷiyīṭṭāḷarkkum parappunarkkum perunaṉṟiyuṭiyēṉ.
vāḻiya nalaṉē!								iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ
vāḻiya nilaṉē!							       irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ



patippuri
20 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiya  iṇiyaṟṟat tamiḻp pēraṟiñar moḻiñāyiṟu tēvanēyap pāvāṇar. ivar vaṭamoḻi ātikkattiliruntu tamiḻi mīṭṭeṭuppataṟkākat tam vāḻviṉ muḻup poḻutiyum cela viṭṭavar.
tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkut tāymoḻit tamiḻ, intiya moḻikaḷukku mūlamoḻit tamiḻ, ulaka moḻikaḷukku mūtta moḻi tamiḻ eṉpatit tam paṉmoḻip pulamiyāl ulakukku aṟivittavar.
ivar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ, kaṭṭurikaḷ aṉittiyum tokuttu oru cēra tamiḻmaṇ patippakam veḷiyiṭṭatit tamiḻ ulakam aṟiyum. 
mutumuṉivar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār  pāvāṇar vaḻi nili aṟiñar. vāḻum tamiḻukku vaḷam pala cērppavar. pāvāṇariṉ aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷi yellām tēvanēyam eṉṉum talippil tamiḻ ulakam payaṉkoḷḷum vakiyil tokuttut tantuḷḷār. ittēvanēyat tokuppukaḷ tamiḻar kaḷukkuk kiṭitta viraccuraṅkam. it tokuppukaḷi veḷi yiṭuvatil perumip paṭukiṟōm.
aṟiñarulakamum, āyvulakamum ivvaruntamiḻk karuvūlatti vāṅkip payaṉ koḷvīr.
- patippāḷar
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ

tiru eṉṉum col teṉcollē
maruva larkoḷa maṉporu ḷaṟṟapaṉ
marapi ṉāruḷar mintilar vāyiru
tiruvi ḻantapiṉ ṟiruveṉuñ colliyu
moruva niṉṟavaṉ tamiḻa ṉoruvaṉē!
mikamikac ciṟupāṉmiyarāṉa vaṭamoḻiyāḷar paṉṉūṟṟuk kaṇakkāṉa teṉmoḻic coṟkaḷi vaṭamoḻiyil tirittuk koṇṭatu maṉṟi, collārāycciyum kalimuṟi yaṟivum mikka iv virupatām nūṟṟāṇṭilum avaṟṟi vaṭacoṟkaḷē yeṉṟu valippatum, vaṭacol vaṭivaṅkaḷiyē tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḻaṅka neñcaḻuttaṅ koḷvatum vintiyiṉum vintiyē!
tiruveṉṉuñ col āriyarāṉa vaṭamoḻiyāḷar nāvalan tēyattiṟku varumuṉṉarē tamiḻakattil vaḻaṅkivanta tamiḻc colleṉpataṟku, toṉmutu cārpu nūlāṉa tolkāppiyat tilēyē atu āṭci peṟṟiruppatum mēli yāriyamoḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟilēṉum atu ev vaṭiviṉum kāṇappaṭāmiyum, ataṉ vērpporuḷ vaṭamoḻiyilillātu tamiḻilēyē yiruppatum pōtiya cāṉṟukaḷām.
tiruveṉṉuñ col tamiḻil, 1. celvam, 2. tirumakaḷ, 3. ciṟappu, 4. aḻaku, 5. kānti, 6. polivu, 7. pēṟu(pākkiyam), 8. tūymi allatu teyvattaṉmi, 9. nalviṉi, 10.  kaṇiyaṉ (cōtiṭaṅ kūṟuvōṉ), 11. māṅkaliyam, 12. paḻiya taliyaṇi vaki, 13. makaḷir koṅkimēl tōṉṟum vīṟṟutteyvam, 14. tūymi allatu perumi kuṟikkum aṭimoḻi mutaliya pala poruḷkaḷil toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu: aḻaku eṉṉum poruḷil tirumi eṉap paṇpup peyar vikuti peṟṟum, celvam perumi tūymi mutaliya poruḷkaḷil tiruvam eṉa ammīṟu peṟṟum, ak kuṇaṅkaḷik kuṟittut tiruvaṉ, tiruvāḷaṉ, tiruvāṭṭi, tiruvattavar, tiruviṉāḷ eṉa uyartiṇip peyarākiyum vaḻaṅki yirukkiṉṟatu. srī eṉṉum ataṉ vaṭacol vaṭivamō kaṭikkaḻakattiṟkup piṟkālattatāy, atuvum urinūlkaḷil, 1. celvam, 2. tirumakaḷ, 3. pēṟu, 4. aḻaku, 5. tūymiyaṭimoḻi eṉṉum aimporuḷkaḷilē vaṭamoḻiyāḷar toṭaṅkiviṭa, avarālum moḻi nūlaṟivum tāymoḻip paṟṟum aṟṟa tamiḻarālum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu vantiruk kiṉṟatu. srī eṉpatiluḷḷa cakaram tamiḻc cakaratti yotta oliyiṉatā yiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
tiruveṉṉuñ collukku mēṟkūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa poruḷkaḷuḷ celvam, aḻaku, tūymi yeṉṉu mūṉṟē mukkiyamum piṟavaṟṟukku aṭippaṭiyumākum. am mūṉṟaṉuḷḷum, celvam eṉpatē mutalatākum.
celvattiṉāl oruvar ciṟakkavuṇṭu iṉpamāy vāḻvatāl uṭal moḻumoḻuveṉa aḻaku peṟukiṉṟatu: vaṟumiyiṉāl vayiṟu kāyntu maṉaṅkavaṉṟu veyilil vāṭi mēṉi keṭukiṉṟatu.  itaṉālēyē, celvamuḷḷa iṉattāri veḷḷokka leṉṟum vaṟumiyuṟṟa iṉattārik kārokka leṉṟuṅ kūṟiṉar nam muṉṉōr. celva mikutiyiṉāl oruvarkkup perumiyum teyvattaṉmiyum uṇṭākiṉṟatu: ulakiṟ celvamikka aracaṉ teyvat taṉmiyuṭiyavaṉākak karutappaṭu kiṉṟāṉ.  kaṭavuḷ ulakelāmuṭiya celvar. aḷaviṟanta matippē teyva vaṇakkamākum.  matippuc celvattāl varuvatu.  aṟivum oruvakic celvamē. teyvattaṉmiyuḷḷa viṭa mellām tūymi karutappaṭum.
āḻntu nōkkiṉ, tiruveṉṉuñ collukkuriya poruḷ kaḷellām mēṟkūṟiya mūṉṟaṉuḷ aṭaṅkum:  nōkkik kāṇka.
tiruveṉṉuñ col ippōtuḷḷapaṭi mutaṉmutalākat tolkāppiyattil,
piṟappē kuṭimi āṇmi āṇṭō
ṭuruvu niṟutta kāma vāyil
niṟiyē aruḷē uṇarvoṭu tiruveṉa
muṟiyaṟak kiḷanta oppiṉatu vakiyē  (tol.meyp.25)
eṉṉum nūṟpāvil, celvam eṉṉum poruḷilēyē vaḻaṅkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  piṉṉart tirukkuṟaḷil, 
aḻukkā ṟeṉavoru pāvi tirucceṟṟut
tīyuḻi yuyttu viṭum,  (kuṟaḷ.168)
aṟaṉaṟintu veḵkā vaṟivuṭiyārc cērun
tiṟaṉaṟin tāṅkē tiru,  (kuṟaḷ. 179)
ūruṇi nīr niṟin taṟṟē yulakavām
pēraṟi vāḷaṉ tiru,  (kuṟaḷ.215)
iruvē ṟulakat tiyaṟki tiruvēṟu
teḷḷiya rātalum vēṟu,  (kuṟaḷ.374)
nallārkaṭ paṭṭa vaṟumiyi ṉiṉṉātē
kallārkaṭ paṭṭa tiru,  (kuṟaḷ. 408)
viṉikkaṇ viṉiyuṭiyāṉ kēṇmivē ṟāka
niṉippāṉi nīṅkun tiru,  (kuṟaḷ.519)
iṉattāṟṟi yeṇṇāta vēntaṉ ciṉattāṟṟic
cīṟiṟ ciṟukun tiru,  (kuṟaḷ.568)
muyaṟci tiruviṉi yākku muyaṟṟiṉmi
yiṉmi pukutti viṭum,  (kuṟaḷ.616)
irumaṉap peṇṭiruṅ kaḷḷuṅ kavaṟun
tirunīkkap paṭṭār toṭarpu,  (kuṟaḷ.920)
karumaṇiyiṟ pāvāynī pōtāyām vīḻun
tirunutaṟ killi yiṭam  (kuṟaḷ.1123)
eṉac celvam, (celvattiṟkut taliviyāṉa) tirumakaḷ, aḻaku eṉṉum mupporuḷkaḷil vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷatu: piṟa poruḷkaḷil piṟa nūlkaḷil vaḻaṅki yuḷḷatu.
iṉi, iruvaki vaḻakkilum, tēviyal makaviyal eṉṉum iru vakic cārpil, tiru eṉṉum col tūymi, celvam, aḻaku, maṅkalam, kaṇṇiyam eṉṉum poruḷkaḷuḷ oṉṟil aṭimoḻiyākat toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅki varuvatip piṉvaruṅ kāṭṭukaḷā laṟika.
1. tēviyal
(1) teyvam: tirumakaṉ, tirumakaḷ, tirumāl, tirumaṭanti.
nūl		:	tirukkaṭikkāppu, tirukkuṟaḷ, tirukkuṟuntāṇ 
				ṭakam, tirukkōvi, tiruneṭuntāṇṭakam, tirup 
				patikam, tirup pallāṇṭu, tiruppāṭṭu, tiruppāvi, 
				tiruppukaḻ, tirumantiram, tirumuṟi, tirumoḻi, 
				tiruvakuppu, tiruvācakam, tiruvāymoḻi, tiru 
				veḻuttu.
ūṇ		:	tirukkaṉṉalamutu, tiruppaṭimāṟṟu, tirumaturam, 
				tirumālivaṭi, tiruvamutu.
aṇi		:	tiruccuṇṇam, tirunīṟu, tiruppaṭṭam, tirup 
				pāvāṭi, tirumaṇ, tiruvātti.
poruḷ:	tirucciṉṉam, tirumaram, tirumalar, tirumuṭṭu, tiru 
				muḷippāliki, tiruvalaku, tiruvāciki, 								tiruvāṭu taṇṭu, tiruviḷakku.
iṭam	:	tiruccentūr, tirunakar, tirunāṭu, tirunelvēli, 
				tiruppati, tirumali, tiruvaraṅkam, tiruvitāṅkūr, 
				tiruvēṅkaṭam, tirukkaṇ, tirukkaḷiṟṟuppaṭi, 
				tirukkaṟṟaḷi, tiruccuṟṟu, tiruppāṟkaṭal, tiru 
				maṭam, tirumāḷiki, tirumuṟṟam, tirumuṉ, tiru 
				vuṇṇāḻiki, tiruvōlakkam.
kālam	:	tirunāḷ, tiruvātiri, tiruvōṇam.
ciṉi		:	tirukkaṇ(cāttutal), tiruccevi, tirumuṭi, 							tirumēṉi,  tiruvaṭi, tiruvāy(malartal), 							tiruvuḷḷam.
kuṇam		: 	tirukkuṟippu, tiruvaruḷ, tirukkōlam.
toḻil	:	tirukkaliyāṇam, tirukkārttiki, tiruviḻā, 
				tiruvūṟal, tirukkāppu, tirukkūttu, tiruttoṇṭu, 
				tiruppaṇi, tiruppaḷḷi, tirumuḻukku, tiruviḷi 
				yāṭal.
(2) aṭiyār : tirukkūṭṭam, tiruccuṟṟu, tiruttakkatēvar, tirut toṇṭar, tirumūlar, tiruvaṭimār, tiruvaḷḷuvar, tiruvōṭu.

2. makaviyal
(1) vēttiyal : tirumaṟu, tirumukam, tirumuttu, tirumūppu, tirumantiram, tiruvāṇi, tiruvāykkēḷvi, tiruvōli.
(2)  potuviyal : tirunilikkāl, tirunilimakaḷir, tiruppūṭṭu, tirumakaṉ, tirumakaḷ, tirumaṅkaliyam, tirumaṇam, tiruvāṅkutal, tiruvil, tiruviṉi, tiruvuḻuttu (aṇi).
ivaṟṟuḷ, tirunelvēli, tiruvaraṅkam mutaliya iṭappeyarkaḷ toṉṟutoṭṭu ulakavaḻakkil tiruveṉṉum aṭipeṟṟē vaḻaṅkuvatiyum, srīraṅkam eṉṉum vaṭivam iruvaki vaḻakkilum piṟkālattatātaliyum nōkkuka.  itaṉāl, tiruvilliputtūr eṉpatē srīvilliputtūr eṉpataṉ paṇṭi vaṭivamum eṉa aṟika.
tirumakaṉ eṉṉum peyar tirumāṉ eṉa maruvic celvam allatu perumi kuṟikkum aṭimoḻiyākum.  perumakaṉ eṉṉum peyar perumāṉ eṉa maruvutal kāṇka. tiru eṉpati srī eṉṟu tirittataṉāl, tirumāṉ eṉṉum aṭiyi srīmāṉ eṉṟu tirippar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar. ataṉuḷ, māṉ eṉṉum īṟṟi mat eṉa maṟṟum tirittu, srīmat, srīmati eṉpavaṟṟi muṟiyē āṇpāl peṇpā laṭimoḻikaḷāka muḻu vaṭavaṭivil vaḻaṅkuvar.  tirumakaṉ, tirumāṉ eṉṉum āṇpāṟ coṟkaṭkut tirumakaḷ, tirumāṭṭi eṉpavi tamiḻil muṟiyē peṇpālākum. perumāṉ eṉṉum āṇpāṟ coṟkup perumāṭṭi eṉpatu peṇpālātal kāṇka.  srī eṉṉum vaṭacol vaṭivit tamiḻil cī eṉac citittut tirumāṉ tirumāṭṭi eṉpavaṟṟic cīmāṉ cīmāṭṭiyeṉa eḻutuvatu, kōḻikkōṭu eṉpati Calicut eṉṉum āṅkilavaṭivu vāyi lāyk kaḷḷikkōṭṭi yeṉṟeḻutuvatum, kāḷikkōṭṭam eṉpati Calcutta eṉṉum āṅkila vaṭivu vāyilāyk kalkattā eṉṟeḻutuvatum pōṉṟa aṟiyāmiyēyākum. itaṉāl, cīkāḷatti eṉṉum vaṭiviṉum tirukkāḷatti eṉṉum vaṭivē takkatākum eṉa aṟika.
iṉi, tiruveṉṉuñ colliyaṉṟic ciṟuveṉṉuñ colliyum srī eṉat tirittuḷḷaṉar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar. peṇṭirkkuc contamākavuḷḷa ciṟucelvattiṟku allatu celvattiṟkuc ciṟupāṭu eṉṟum ciṟutaṉam eṉṟum peyar.
ciṟutaṉan tēṭuvaḷ  (taṇṭali. 95)
vīṭṭuc celavil mīttuvikkum ciṟu tokikkuc cilvāṉam eṉṟu peyar. ciṟutaṉam eṉpatu piṟkālattil taṉi vēṟṟu (Private) niti eṉap potuppaṭavum poruḷ peṟṟatu.
uṭiyārsrī rājarāja tēvar ciṟutaṉattuk
koṭutta (S.I.I.ii,3)
ciṟutaṉam eṉpatu srītaṉam, cītaṉam eṉat tavaṟāy vaḻaṅkiṉṟatu.
srī eṉpatit tiruveṉṟum, srīlasrī eṉpatit tirupperuntiru allatu tirumikutiru eṉṟum, makārājarājasrī eṉpati makavaracavaracattiru allatu māvaraca varacattiru eṉṟum, rājasrī eṉpati aracattiru eṉṟum tamiḻil vaḻaṅkal vēṇṭum. k.(uh).(uh).srī eṉṉum mukavariyaṭiyil, srī eṉpatu tavira maṟṟa mūveḻuttukaḷum tamiḻc coṟkaḷiṉ mutaleḻuttukaḷāyē yiruttalik kāṇka.  itaṉāl, srī eṉpatu piṉṉi vaṭiva meṉpati yōrka.
cīrkāḻi eṉpataṉ mutal vaṭivam cīkāḻi eṉpar.  cīr eṉṉuñ col centamiḻāyiruttaliṉ, cīrkāḻi yeṉṟē vaḻaṅkuvatu nallatu.  itaṟkiciyātār tirukkāḻi yeṉṟē vaḻaṅkutal vēṇṭum.
iṉi, tiruveṉṉun tamiḻccoṟku vērpporuḷ:
tiruveṉpatu til eṉṉum vēraṭiyāyp piṟantu tiraṭṭappaṭuvatu allatu īṭṭappaṭuvatu eṉṉum poruḷatu.
o.nō: tēṭṭu (tēṭappaṭuvatu) = celvam.
til	>	tir	>	tiraḷ	>	tiraḷi	>	tiraṇi
til	> 	tir  	> 	tiru.
til 	> 	tir  	> 	tiram 	>	tiṟam 	> 	tiṟaṉ	>	tiṟal
til	>	tiḷ	>	tiṭṭu	>	tiṭṭi	> 	tiṟam	>	tiṟami.
	tiḷ 	> 	tiṇ 	> 	tiṇṇi	>	tiṇi.
					tiṇ	>	tiṇṇam.
					tiṇ	>	tiṇṭu.
					tiṭṭu	>	tiṭṭam	>	tiṭam.
					tiṭṭu	>	tiṭṭal	> 	tiṭal.
				tillumullu >  tiṇṭumuṇṭu.
til eṉṉum vēr muṟiyē tiraṭci, kaṭṭi, mēṭu eṉṉum poruḷkaḷiyum tiraṭcip poruḷiṉiṉṟu uṟuti, kuḻu, kuḷir, valimi, aṇi(metti) mutaliya poruḷkaḷiyum tarutali mēṟkāṭṭiya tiricoṟkaḷāṟ (Derivatives) kāṇka. 
itukāṟum kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, tiru eṉpatu teṉcollē yeṉṟum, atuvē vaṭamoḻiyil srī eṉat tirikkappaṭṭuḷḷa teṉṟum teḷḷatteḷiya aṟika.  srī eṉpatē cevik kiṉiya teṉṟu vaṭamoḻiyāḷariṉ aṭiyār cilar kūṟuvārāyiṉ,

eṉak kūṟi viṭukka.  (centamiḻc celvi viṭi 1943.)
tirukkuṟaṭ ciṟappu
ulakiyal iṉitu naṭipeṟutaṟku iṉṟiyamiyāta nāl nilimiyum illaṟamun tuṟavaṟamum araciyalum kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyar kāmaviṉpamum paṟṟi, uṇmiyākavum naṭunilimi yākavum ellārkkum oppa muṭinta vakiyilum, taliciṟanta pāvālum ciṟanta coṟkaḷālum ilakkaṇa vaḻuviṉṟi iruvaki yaṇikaḷuṭaṉ, curuṅkac colli viḷaṅka vikkum nūl tirukkuṟaḷ oṉṟē. irumikkum utavum viḻumiya poruḷi aṇimikka kuṟaḷ veṇpāvāṟ pāṭiyiruppatu, paṉmaṇipatitta ōviya vēlippāṭṭup poṟkalattil aracarkkuriya aṟucuviyuṇṭiyip paṭittāṟ pōlum.  kuṟaḷ veṇpāvāl āṉatiṉālum, vīṭupēṟṟu vaḻiyik kūṟi maṟittaṉmi peṟṟatiṉālum, tirukkuṟaḷ eṉa aṭiyaṭutta āku peyar peṟṟatu.
iruvaki moḻi naṭiyuṭ ciṟantatu ceyyuḷ. iruvakic ceṭyuḷuṭ ciṟantatu pā.  nālvakip pāvuṭ ciṟantatu veṇpā.  
kāciṉiyiṟ piḷḷik kavikkam pulipuliyām
pēcum ulāviṟ petumpi puli - ācu
valavarkku vaṇṇam puliyāmaṟ ṟellāp
pulavarkkum veṇpāp puli.  (taṉippāṭal)
aivaki veṇpāvuṭ kuṟukiyatu kuṟaḷ veṇpā. payaṉil col pakarvāṉip pataṭiyeṉṉum tiruvaḷḷuvar, coṟcurukkam paṟṟik kuṟaḷ veṇpāviyē tam nūṟkut terintu koṇṭār.
tiruvaḷḷuvariṉ pācciṟappi,
pūviṟkut tāmariyē poṉṉukkuc cāmpuṉatam
āviṟ karumuṉiyā yāṉik kamararumpal
tēviṟ ṟirumāl eṉacciṟanta teṉpavē
pāviṟku vaḷḷuvarveṇ pā.
eṉṉum tiruvaḷḷuva mālip pāvum; nūṟciṟappi,
niḻalarumi veyyililē niṉṟaṟimi ṉīcaṉ
kaḻalarumi vevviṉiyiṟ kāṇmiṉ - paḻakutamiḻc 
collarumi nāliraṇṭiṟ cōmaṉ koṭiyarumi
pullariṭat tēyaṟimiṉ pōy.  (auviyār taṉippāṭal)
pālellām nallāviṉ pālāmō pāriluḷḷa
nūlellām vaḷḷuvarcey nūlāmō.  (parimēlaḻakaruric ciṟappup pāyiram)
ālum vēlum palluk kuṟuti
nālum iraṇṭum colluk kuṟuti.  (paḻamoḻi)
eṉpaṉavum;pulamic ciṟappi,
pulavar tiruvaḷ ḷuvaraṉṟip pūmēl
cilavar pulavareṉac ceppal - nilavu
piṟaṅkoḷimā likkum peyarmāli maṟṟum
kaṟaṅkiruḷmā likkum peyar.
eṉṉum tiruvaḷḷuvamālippāvum; karuttup parappi,
māluṅ kuṟaḷāy vaḷarntiraṇṭu māṇaṭiyāṉ
ñāla muḻutum nayantaḷantāṉ - vālaṟiviṉ
vaḷḷuvarun taṅkuṟaḷveṇ pāvaṭiyāl viyattār
uḷḷuvavel lāmaḷantārorntu
eṉṉum tiruvaḷḷuvamālip pāvum; curuṅkac collali,
tiṉiyaḷavu pōtāc ciṟupuṉṉīr nīṇṭa
paṉiyaḷavu kāṭṭum paṭittāl - maṉiyaḷaku
vaḷḷik kuṟaṅkum vaḷanāṭa vaḷḷuvaṉār
veḷḷik kuṟaṭpā viri.
eṉṉum tiruvaḷḷuvamālip pāvum; viḷaṅkavittali,
paranta poruḷellām pāraṟiya vēṟu
terintu tiṟantoṟuñ cērac - curuṅkiya
collāl virittup poruḷviḷaṅkac collutal
vallārār vaḷḷuvaral lāl.
eṉṉum tiruvaḷḷuva mālip pāvum; uṇarttum.
iṅṅaṉam tirukkuṟaḷ ellā vakiyilum oppuyarvaṟṟa ulakat tamiḻ nūlām. ataṉāl, mupporuṇmi paṟṟi muppāl, muppāl nūl eṉṟum; āciriyaṉi nōkki vaḷḷuvam, vaḷḷuva nūl, vaḷḷuvappayaṉ eṉṟum; uṇmi yurittal paṟṟip poyyāmoḻi eṉṟum; mantirattaṉmipaṟṟi vaḷḷuvar vāy moḻiyeṉṟum maṟittaṉmi paṟṟit tamiḻmaṟi, potumaṟi eṉṟum, teyvattaṉmi paṟṟit teyvanūl eṉṟum;
vēmpuṅ kaṭuvum pōla veñcol
tāṅkutaliṉṟi vaḻinaṉi payakkumeṉ
ṟōmpaṭik kiḷaviyiṉ vāyuṟuttal
paṟṟi vāyuṟivāḻttu eṉṟum; peyar peṟṟuḷḷa teṉka.
mutaṉūṉmi: tirukkuṟaḷ ellāvakiyilum tūya mutaṉūlākum.  aṟam poruḷiṉpam eṉṉum mupporuḷiyum paṟṟit tirukkuṟaḷ muṟiyiṟ kūṟum vaṭanūl oṉṟu milli.  nāṉmukaṉ (piramaṉ) mutalil tirivarkkam eṉṉum perunūlic ceytā ṉeṉṟum, ati viyāḻaṉum (pirukaṟpati) veḷḷiyum (cukkiraṉ) curukki muṟiyē pārkaṟpattiyam cukkiranīti eṉṉum nūlkaḷi iyaṟṟiṉareṉṟum, tiruvaḷḷuvar tirivarkkam pōl aṟam poruḷiṉpam paṟṟi nūl ceytataṉālēyē nāṉmukaṉiṉ tōṟṟaravu (avatāram) eṉakkarutap peṟṟāreṉṟum, tamiḻp paṟṟillāta pirāmaṇat tamiḻp pulavar kūṟuvar.  oruvarō palarō kaṭṭippāṭiya tiruvaḷḷuvamāliyil,
nāṉmaṟiyiṉ meypporuḷi mupporuḷā nāṉmukattōṉ
tāṉmaṟintu vaḷḷuvaṉāyt tanturitta - nūṉmuṟi
eṉṟu ukkirapperuvaḻuti peyariluḷḷa pāvum,
meyyāya vētap poruḷviḷaṅkap - poyyātu
tantā ṉulakiṟkut tāṉvaḷ ḷuvaṉāki
yantā marimē layaṉ.
eṉṟu kāvirip pūmpaṭṭiṉattuk kārikkaṇṇaṉār peyariluḷḷa pāvum, ellā aṟanūlkaḷiyum maṟinūlkaḷiyum āriyavēta vaḻi nūlākak koḷḷum paṇṭi marapu paṟṟik kūṟiyatik koṇṭu, avar aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟukiṉṟār pōlum!
mēṟkāṭṭiya pāp pakutikaḷil nāṉmaṟiyeṉṟum vētameṉṟum kuṟittiruk kiṉṟatē yoḻiya, tirivarkkameṉṟu kuṟikkappaṭa villi. itaṟku muṟṟum māṟāka,
tāṉē muḻutuṇarntu taṇṭamiḻiṉ veṇkuṟaḷāl
āṉā vaṟamutalā vannāṉkum - ēṉōruk
kūḻi ṉurittāṟku moṇṇīr mukilukkum
vāḻiyula keṉṉāṟṟu maṟṟu.
eṉavuḷḷatu nakkīrar peyariluḷḷa pāṭṭu.
nāṉmukaṉ tēviyākac collappaṭum nāmakaḷ peyariluḷḷa pāṭṭu, 
nāṭā mutaṉāṉ maṟināṉ mukaṉāviṟ
pāṭāviṭippā ratampakarntēṉ - kūṭāri
yeḷḷiya veṉṟi yilaṅkilivēṉ māṟapiṉ
vaḷḷuvaṉ vāyateṉ vākku.
eṉpatu.  itilum tirivarkkak kuṟippilli.
mēlum, tiruvaḷḷuvar muppālāl nāṟporuḷuṅ kūṟiṉārē yaṉṟi, mutal mupporuḷi maṭṭum kūṟiṉārallar. makkaḷāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭa muppāṉūl vaṭamoḻiyil illātatiṉālēyē. tirivarkkam eṉpatu nāṉmukaṉāl iyaṟṟap peṟṟateṉṟum, ōṭippōṉa muyal periya muyal. eṉpataṟkoppa atu makācātiram eṉṟum, kūṟuvārāyiṉar eṉka.
iṉi, ki.mu. nāṉkām nūṟṟāṇṭil vāḻnta cāṇakkiyarum avar māṇava rākiya kāmantakarum eḻutiya poruḷnūṟ karuttukkaḷ tirukkuṟaḷiṟ payiṉṟu varuvatāl, ataṉ poruṭpālukku vaṭamoḻi arutta cāttiram mutaṉūleṉpar tamiḻp pakivarum pōlittamiḻarum.
toṉṟutoṭṭu varutal cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiya reṉṟāṟ pōlap paṭippuk kālan toṭaṅki mēmpaṭṭu varutal  eṉṟu parimēlaḻakarālum paḻimi kūṟappaṭum mūvēntarkuṭikaḷum, avaṟṟuḷḷum ciṟappākap 
paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟuṭaṉ paṉmali yaṭukkattuk
kumarik kōṭuṅ koṭuṅkaṭal koḷḷa
vaṭaticik kaṅkiyu mimayamuṅ koṇṭu
teṉṟici yāṇṭa teṉṉavaṉ kuṭi,
varalāṟṟiṟ keṭṭāta toṉṟutoṭṭut teṉṉāṭṭi yāṇṭu varuva tālum,
oḻukkattu nīttār perumi viḻuppattu
vēṇṭum paṉuvaṟ ṟuṇivu.
pakuttuṇṭu palluyi rōmputal nūlōr
tokuttavaṟṟu ḷellān tali
matinuṭpam nūlō ṭuṭiyārk katinuṭpam
yāvuḷa muṉṉiṟ pavi.
poccāppārk killi pukaḻmi yatuvulakat
teppāṉū lōrkkun tuṇivu.
eṉṟu tirukkuṟaḷ paltuṟi nūlkaḷiyuñ cuṭṭuvatālum,
iḻumeṉ moḻiyāṉ viḻumiyatu nuvaliṉum
paranta moḻiyāṉ aṭinimirn toḻukiṉum
tōleṉa moḻipa toṉmoḻip pulavar  (1945)
eṉṉun tolkāppiya nūṟpā ki.mu. ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkum muṟpaṭṭa aṟamporuḷiṉpa vīṭṭu nūlkaḷik kuṟippatālum, 
māraṇam poruḷ eṉṟiṉṉa māṉanūl yāvum vāri
vāraṇaṅkoṇṭa tantō vaḻivaḻip peyarum māḷa.
eṉṟu oru paḻantaṉic ceyyuḷ kūṟuvatālum,
tiruvaḷḷuvar vēntarkku uḷpaṭukarumat talivarāyiruntatākat teriva taṉālum, avar tirukkuṟaḷ iyaṟṟiyataṟku vaṭanūṟṟuṇi ettuṇiyum vēṇṭiya tāyiruntatilliyeṉa, avarnūli vaḻi nūleṉpār kūṟṟi maṟukka.
muḻuniṟivu:
aṟamporuḷiṉpam vīṭeṉṉum nāṟporuḷiyum paṟṟi viḷakkamākak kūṟuvataṉālum, ovvōr atikārattilum atataṟkuriya poruḷip paṟṟiya ellāk karuttukkaḷiyum koṇṭiruppataṉālum, tirukkuṟaḷ muḻuniṟivāṉa nūlākum.
akaramutala eṉṟu tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkiṉ mutaleḻuttil toṭaṅki muyaṅkap peṟiṉ eṉṟu ataṉ iṟutiyeḻuttil muṭintiruppatum, tirukkuṟaḷiṉ muḻuniṟi vik kāṭṭum.
āyirattu munnūṟṟu muppa taruṅkuṟaḷum
pāyirattiṉōṭu pakarntataṟpiṉ - pōyoruttar
vāykkēṭka nūluḷavō maṉṉu tamiḻppulava
rāykkēṭka vīṟṟirukka lām - tiruvaḷḷuvamāli.
ulakap potumi:
tirukkuṟaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭiṟku maṭṭumaṉṟi ulaka muḻumikkum ottateṉpatu ellārkkum oppa muṭintatē. itupaṟṟiyē vaḷḷuvaṉ taṉṉi ulakiṉukkē tantu vāṉpukaḻ koṇṭa tamiḻnāṭu eṉṟu pāṭiṉār pāvalar cuppiramaṇiya pāratiyārum. vāḻkkittuṟiyilum āṭcittuṟiyilum maṭṭumaṉṟic camayat tuṟiyilum potuvāyiruppatāl potumaṟi yeṉappaṭṭatu.
oṉṟē poruḷeṉiṉ vēṟeṉpa vēṟeṉiṉ
aṉṟeṉpa āṟu camayattār - naṉṟeṉa
eppā valarum iyipavē vaḷḷuvaṉār
muppāl moḻinta moḻi - tiruvaḷḷuvamāli.
kālavarampiṉmi:
tirukkuṟaḷ ittuṇikkālantāṉ allatu iṉṉa nūṟṟāṇṭu varitāṉ payaṉpaṭumeṉṟu evaruñ collutaṟkiṭamiṉṟi, ekkālattiṟkum ēṟṟatāyi ruppatum ataṉ ēṟṟaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟām.
kōvaracum (Monarchy) kuṭiyaracum (Democracy) makkaḷāṭciyum (Republic) kūṭṭuṭi miyum (Socialism) nīṅki ulakeṅkum potuvuṭimi (Communism) variṉum, ataṟkum,
pakuttuṇṭu palluyirōmputal nūlōr
tokuttavaṟṟu ḷellān tali.
pāttūṇ marīi yavaṉip paciyeṉṉun
tīppiṇi tīṇṭa laritu.
irantum uyirvāḻtal vēṇṭiṉ parantu
keṭuka vulakiyaṟṟi yāṉ.
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷkaḷ iṭantarum eṉka.
maṟiyiyal:
tirukkuṟaḷ immikkuriya oḻukka varampu kūṟum aṟanūlāka maṭṭumaṉṟi, maṟumiyil uyar piṟappō viṇṇiṉpamō vīṭṭiṉpamō peṟutaṟkuriya vaḻi kāṭṭum maṟinūlākavumirup patāl, tamiḻmaṟi, potumaṟi, vaḷḷuvar vāy moḻi, teyvanūl eṉap peyar peṟṟuḷḷatu.
immi maṟumi yiraṇṭu meḻumikkuñ
cemmi neṟiyiṟ ṟeḷivupeṟa - mummiyiṉ
vīṭavaṟṟi ṉāṉkiṉ vitivaḻaṅka vaḷḷuvaṉār
pāṭiṉa riṉkuṟaḷveṇ pā. - tiruvaḷḷuvamāli.
iyalvariyaṟic ciṟappu:
poruḷkaṭkum paṇpukaṭkum viṉikaṭkum iyalvariyaṟi (Definition) kūṟuvatil tirukkuṟaḷ taliciṟantatākum.
e-ṭu:
antaṇa reṉpō raṟavōrmaṟ ṟevvuyirkkuñ
centaṇmi pūṇṭoḻuka lāṉ
vāymi yeṉappaṭuva tiyāteṉiṉ yātoṉṟun
tīmi yilāta colal.
uṟṟanōy nōṉṟal uyirkkuṟukaṇ ceyyāmi
aṟṟē tavattiṟ kuru.
naviltoṟum nayamuṭimi:
ituvari pakutikkum muḻumikkum curukkamākavum perukka mākavum tirukkuṟaṭkut tōṉṟiyuḷḷa urikaḷ ēṟattāḻa nūṟum, atu moḻipeyarkkap peṟṟa moḻikaḷ irupatum, ākum.  āyiṉum, iṉṉum accuraṅkattiṉiṉṟu 
karuttumaṇikaḷ tōṇṭattōṇṭa mēṉmēlum vantu koṇṭēyirukkiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu:
nāṭeṉpa nāṭā vaḷattaṉa nāṭalla
nāṭa vaḷantaru nāṭu
nāṭu eṉpa - ciṟanta nāṭeṉṟu collappaṭuvaṉa; nāṭā vaḷattaṉa – piṟanāṭu kaḷiṉ utaviyi vēṇṭātu  tamakku vēṇṭiya poruḷ vaḷaṅkaḷi yellām tammakattē koṇṭaṉavākum; nāṭavaḷantaru nāṭu - piṟa nāṭṭutaviyi nāṭumāṟu kuṉṟiya vaḷaṅkoṇṭa nāṭukaḷ; nāṭu alla - ciṟanta nāṭākā.
maṇaṟkiḷikka nīrūṟu mintarkaḷ vāyvit
tuṇaccurakkun tāymuli yoṇpāl - piṇakkilā
vāymoḻi vaḷḷuvar muppāṉ matippulavōrk
kāytoṟu mūṟu maṟivu. - tiruvaḷḷuvamāli.
naṭuvu nilimi:
ellārum vāḻavēṇṭumeṉpatum, kuṟṟattiṟkēṟpa ellāriyum oppat taṇṭikka vēṇṭumeṉpatum, tirukkuṟaḷiṉ naṭunilik koḷkikaḷām.  ivi āriyak koḷkikaṭku nērmāṟāṉavi.
vaḷḷuvarcey tirukkuṟaḷi maṟuvaṟanaṉ kuṇarntōrkaḷ
uḷḷuvarō maṉuvāti yoru kulattiṟ korunīti.
eṉṟu maṉōṉmaṇīya āciriyar kūṟutal kāṇka.
uyarniliyaṟam:
ellā aṟanūlkaḷuḷḷum uyarnta oḻukka varampukaḷik kūṟuvatu tirukkuṟaḷē.
e-ṭu:
payaṉilcol pārāṭṭu vāṉi makaṉeṉal
makkaṭ pataṭi yeṉal.
iṉṉācey tāri yoṟutta lavarnāṇa
naṉṉayañ ceytu viṭal.
uḷḷattā luḷḷalun tītē piṟaṉporuḷik
kaḷḷattāṟ kaḷvē meṉal
īṉṟāḷ pacikāṇpā ṉāyiṉuñ ceyyaṟka
cāṉṟōr paḻikkum viṉi.
iṟanta mūppiṉarāya irumutu kuravarum kaṟpuṭi maṉiviyuṅ kuḻaviyum paciyāṉ varuntu mellikkaṇ, tīyaṉapalavuñ ceytāyiṉum puṟantaruka. eṉpatu āriya aṟanūl neṟiyīṭu.
naṭimuṟiyaṟivu:
tiruvaḷḷuvar makkaṭku uyarnta oḻukkatti vakuttārēṉum, ulakiyalaṟintu ataṉ oḻuṅkāṉa naṭappiṟkut tōtākavē cila vilakkukaḷiyum amit tirukkiṉṟār.
aruḷuṭimi, pulāṉmaṟuttal, kaḷḷāmi, vāymi, vekuḷāmi, iṉṉā ceyyāmi, kollāmi eṉṉum aṟa viṉikaḷi araciyalil muṟṟak kaṭip piṭippatu aritātaliṉ, avaṟṟi illaṟaviyaliṟ kūṟātu tuṟavaṟaviyalilēyē 
kūṟiyuḷḷār.
teyvan toḻāaḷ koḻunaṟ ṟoḻuteḻuvāḷ eṉṟu coṉṉa vaḷḷuvarē, pulattaliṟ puttēṇāṭuṇṭō eṉṟum, ūṭutal kāmattiṟ kiṉpam eṉṟum uṭaṉpaṭṭuḷḷār.
uṇmikkūṟṟu:
iṟivaṉē nālvaki varuṇattāriyum, muṟiyē taṉ mukattiṉiṉṟum mārpiṉiṉṟum toṭiyiṉiṉṟum pātat tiṉiṉṟum paṭittāṉeṉṟum, avaruṭ pirāmaṇaṉē uyarntava ṉeṉṟum, maṟṟa mūvarum avaṉukkut tāḻntavareṉṟum avaṉukkut toṇṭu ceyyavē paṭikkappaṭṭavareṉṟum, āriya nūlkaḷ tuṇintu poyyurippatu pōla, tirukkuṟaḷ ōriṭattum kaṭpulaṉāṉa uṇmikaḷit tirittuk kūṟuvatilli.  eṅkēṉum ōriṭattil uyarvu naviṟci aḷaviṟan tiruppiṉum, atu aṇitaḻuviyatum uṇmi ellārālum aṟiyappaṭattakkatu mākavē yirukkum. 
e-ṭu:
aṉiccamum aṉṉattiṉ tūviyum mātar
aṭikku neruñcip paḻam.
poyppāla poyyēyāyp pōyiṉapoy yallāta
meyppāla meyyāy viḷaṅkiṉavē  - tiruvaḷḷuvamāli.
atikāravoḻuṅku:
nūṉ muḻutum 133 atikāraṅkaḷāka vakukkappaṭṭiruppatum, ovvoṉṟum pappattuk kuṟaḷ koṇṭiruppatum, ovvōriya lilum atikāraṅkaḷ oṉṟō ṭoṉṟu kōviyākat toṭarpu koṇṭiruppatum, piṟanūlkaḷiṟ kāṇaṟkariya oḻuṅkākum.
coṟporuḷ tūymi:
akkālattil moḻiyārāycci yiṉmiyālum, vaṭacoṟkaḷ ov voṉṟākap pukuttappaṭṭamiyālum, tamiḻaruṭ perum pulavarkkum teṉcol vaṭacol vēṟupāṭu teriyātiruntatu.  ataṉāl tirukkuṟaḷilum cila vaṭacoṟkaḷ pukuntu viṭṭaṉa.  avi: antam, amarar, avi, ākulam, ācāram, āti, intiraṉ, kaṇam (kṣaṇam), kavari, kāraṇam, calam (vañcaṉi), nāmam (peyar), pākkiyam, pāvam, pāvi, vittakar eṉṉum patiṉāṟē, āriya veṟiyar kūṟuvatu pōl aimpatalla.
appatiṉāṟaṉuḷḷum, amarar, kāraṇam, pākkiyam, vittakar eṉṉum nāṉkum tamiḻ vēriṉavē.
al - a. maṭi - mari - marar.
karaṇam - kāraṇam (va.) karu + aṇam = karaṇam = ceyki, ceykik karuvi. karuttal ceytal.  itu oru vaḻakkaṟṟaviṉi.
paku - pakku-pākku=pakuti.  pākku- pākkiyam= naṟpakuti, naṟpāl, naṟpēṟu.
viḻittal = kaṇtiṟattal, pārttal, aṟital.  viḻi = aṟivu.  viḻi- viṭi- viti - vit (va.) - vittaka. kavari eṉpatu cavari eṉṉum vaṭanāṭṭuc colliṉ tiripu.
ati eṉpatu ati eṉṉum colliṟkiṉamāṉa vaḻakkaṟṟa tamiḻ viṉiccol.  āyam eṉṉuñ colliṉ mutaṉiliyāṉa ā eṉpatu vā eṉpataṉ tiripu.
kuṇam, niccam eṉṉum iraṇṭum teṉcollē.  koḷḷutal = koṇṭiruttal, uṭiyaṉāyiruttal.  koḷ - koḷki = iyalpu. koḷ - kōḷ = taṉmi. koḷ - koṇ - (koṇam) - kuṇam = koṇṭataṉmi, taṉmi.
iccoṟku vaṭavar kāṭṭum mūlam krah (paṟṟu) eṉpatē.
nil - niṟṟal = nilippu.
kuṇapattiraṉṟāḷ niṟṟalum vaṇaṅki  (cūṭā: 7: 76).
niṟṟal - niccal. o.nō: muṟam - muṟṟil - muccil.
niccalum viṇṇappañ ceyya  (tiv. tiruvāy.  (1:6:11)
niccal - nittal. nittal viḻāvaṇi  (cilap. uripeṟu kaṭṭuri). nil - niṟṟam. o.nō: vel -veṟṟam.
niṟṟam - niccam - nittam - nitya (va.)
tirukkuṟaḷiṟ collappaṭṭuḷḷa poruḷkaḷellām tūya tamiḻc ceytikaḷē. āriyattik kaṇṭittaṟkeṉṟē nūliyaṟ ṟiyavar eṅṅaṉam āriyac ceytikaḷit taḻuva muṭiyum? iyaṉṟa viṭamellām valintum nalintum āriya mūlaṅkāṭṭum parimēlaḻakarum, 
iṉṟi yamiyāc ciṟappiṉa vāyiṉum
kuṉṟa varupa viṭal
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaṭ ciṟappuriyil, iṟappa varuvaḻi iḷivantaṉa ceytāyiṉum uykaveṉṉum vaṭanūṉ muṟimiyi maṟuttu, uṭampiṉatu niliyiṉmi yiyum māṉattiṉatu niliyuṭimi yiyun tūkki avi ceyyaṟka veṉpatām. eṉṟu varintiruttal kāṇka.
ceyyuṭ ciṟappu:
tirukkuṟaḷpōṟ kuṟaḷveṇpāviṟkuc ciṟantanūl muṉṉu milli; piṉṉumilli.  ovvoru kuṟaḷum ovvoru vakiyil oḷiviṭum maṇipōlvatām.
e-ṭu:
teyvan toḻāaḷ koḻunaṟ ṟoḻuteḻuvāḷ
peyyeṉap peyyu maḻi.
maṅkala meṉpa maṉimāṭci maṟṟataṉ
naṉkala naṉmakkaṭ pēṟu.
eṉpaṉa iṉṉōci yuḷḷaṉa.
iyaṟṟalu mīṭṭaluṅ kāttaluṅ kātta
vakuttalum valla taracu
poruḷal lavarip poruḷākac ceyyum
poruḷalla tilli poruḷ
uḻuvā rulakattārk kāṇi yaḵtāṟṟā
teḻuvāri yellām poṟuttu
irantu muyirvāḻtal vēṇṭiṉ parantu
keṭuka vulakiyaṟṟi yāṉ
kaṇṭukēṭ ṭuṇṭuyirt tuṟṟaṟiyu mimpulaṉum
oṇṭoṭi kaṇṇē yuḷa.
eṉpaṉa poruṭ ciṟappuḷḷaṉa.
nattampōṟ kēṭu muḷatākuñ cākkāṭum
vittakark kallā laritu.
eṉpatu coṟcurukka muḷḷatu.
taliyi ṉiḻinta mayiraṉiyar māntar
niliyi ṉiḻintak kaṭi.  (uvami)
vēloṭu niṉṟā ṉiṭuveṉ ṟatupōluṅ
kōloṭu niṉṟā ṉiravu.  (uvami) 
uppamin taṟṟāṟ pulavi yatuciṟitu
mikkaṟṟāl nīḷa viṭal  (uvami)
neṭunīr maṟavi maṭituyil nāṉkuṅ
keṭunīrār kāmak kalaṉ.  (uruvakam)
nuṉikkompa rēṟiṉā raḵtiṟan tūkkiṉ
uyirkkiṟuti yāki viṭum  (piṟitumoḻital)
tīyiṉāṟ cuṭṭapu ṇuḷḷāṟum āṟātē
nāviṉāṟ cuṭṭa vaṭu  (vēṟṟumi)
naṉṟaṟi vāriṟ kayavar tiruvuṭiyar
neñcat tavala milar  (vañcakappukaḻcci)
kaṇṇiṉṟu kaṇṇaṟac colliṉuñ collaṟka
muṉṉiṉṟu piṉṉōkkāc col  (muraṇ)
neruna luḷaṉoruvaṉ iṉṟilli yeṉṉum
perumi yuṭittiv vulaku  (etirporuḷ)
kāṇātāṟ kāṭṭuvāṉ tāṉkāṇāṉ kāṇātāṉ
kaṇṭāṉān tāṉkaṇṭa vāṟu  (coṟporuṭ piṉvarunili)
eṉpaṉa palvēṟu aṇiyuṭiyaṉa.
kaṭikkaḻakac ceyyuṭkaḷ allatu nūlkaḷ eṉṟu mutalil vaḻaṅkiyavi pattup pāṭṭum eṭṭuttokiyumē. tiruvaḷḷuvar kaḻakappulavaraṉmiyālum, avar nūl kaḻakattiluḷḷa pirāmaṇap pulavarāl oppuk koḷḷap peṟāmiyāl araṅkēṟātirunta tiṉālum, atu kaḻaka nūlum kaḻaka maruviya nūlumallātu taṉiyākavēyiruntu vantatu. piṟkālattiṟ kaṭikkaḻakaṅ kalik kappaṭṭa piṉ, camaṇat tamiḻak kaḻakattit cērnta aṟanūlāci riyar cilar tam 
nūl kaṭkuk kaṭikkaḻaka nūl nilimi yūṭṭal vēṇṭi, kaḻakak kālattaṉavum piṟkālattaṉavumāṉa patiṉeṇ nūlkaḷi oru tokutiyākki, avaṟṟip patiṉeṇ kīḻkkaṇak keṉṟum pattup pāṭṭiyum eṭṭut tokiyiyum patiṉeṇ mēṟkaṇakkeṉṟum vaḻaṅkiṉatākat terikiṉṟatu.
tirukkuṟaḷ kaḻaka nūlaṉmiyālum, ataṟku nāl nūṟṟāṇ ṭiṟkup piṟpaṭṭa kārnāṟpatu, kaḷavaḻināṟpatu, iṉṉānāṟpatu, iṉiyavi nāṟpatu eṉṉum nāṉkē kaḻakakkālattaṉavāta lālum, ēṉiyavellām vaccirananti 5ām nūṟṟāṇṭil maturiyil camaṇat tamiḻkkaḻakam niṟuviya piṉṉarē eḻuntavi yātalālum, avaṟṟuḷ ācārakkōvi tirukkuṟaṭku nērmāṟāka vaṭacollum āriyak koḷkiyum mikuntiruppatuṭaṉ, iḻitaku niliyil tamiḻarip pirāmaṇarkku aṭimip paṭutti yiruppatālum, patiṉeṇkīḻkkaṇakku eṉṉum tokutimuṟi mikattavaṟāṉateṉṟum, tirukkuṟaḷi atiṟ cērttatu atiṉum mikat tavaṟāṉateṉṟum aṟintukoḷka.
tirukkuṟaḷik kaṭikkaḻakak kālattu ammi vaṉappu nūleṉṟu koḷvatē takkatām.
10. pāyiraviḷakkam
āyira mukattāṉ akaṉṟa tāyiṉum
pāyira millatu paṉuva laṉṟē
māṭakkuc cittiramum mānakarkkuk kōpuramum
āṭamittōḷ nallārk kaṇiyumpōl - nāṭimuṉ
aituriyā niṉṟa aṇinturiyi yennūṟkum
peyturiyā vittār peritu.
parupporuṭ ṭākiya pāyiraṅ kēṭṭārkku
nuṇporuṭ ṭākiya nūliṉitu viḷaṅkum.
pāyiram potuvum ciṟappum eṉa iruvakippaṭum.  ilakkaṇa nūṟku ivviraṇṭum vēṇṭum; ilakkiya nūṟkuc ciṟappoṉṟē pōtum. acciṟappum, piṟar āciriyaṉiyum nūliyum ciṟappittuk kūṟuvatum āciriyaṉ nūṟporuḷic curukkik kūṟuvatum eṉa iruvakiyām. āciriyaṉ kūṟuvatu taṟciṟappuppāyiram eṉappaṭum.  atu kaṭavuḷvaḻuttoṭu kūṭiyum kūṭātum irukkum.
makkaḷellārum immiyilum maṟumiyilum iṉpuṟṟu vāḻa vēṇṭumeṉpatē, tiruvaḷḷuvar tirukkuṟaḷiyaṟṟiyataṉ nōkkam. illaṟattil immikkum maṟumikkum ēṟṟavāṟu eṅṅaṉam oḻuka vēṇṭumeṉpatu illaṟaviyalilum, iṟantapiṉ vīṭu peṟuvataṟku evvevvaṟaṅkaḷiyum payiṟcikaḷiyum mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭumeṉpatu tuṟavaṟaviyalilum, ellārum tattam toḻilic ceytu poruḷīṭṭi vāḻvataṟku aracaṉ eṅṅaṉam pātu kāppaḷikka vēṇṭumeṉpatu poruṭpālilum, kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyum kāma viṉpatti eṅṅaṉam nukaravēṇṭu meṉpatu iṉpattup pālilum, kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
iṅṅaṉam makkaḷellārum iṭiyūṟiṉṟi iṉitu vāḻtaṟku, mutaṟpaṭiyāka vēṇṭiya ētukkaḷ nāṉkeṉat tiruvaḷḷuvar kaṇṭār. avi kaṭavuḷvaḻipāṭu, maḻipeyal, tuṟaviyar uṟivu, aṟavoḻukkam eṉpaṉa. ivaṟṟiyē ātipakavaṉ vaḻuttu, vāṉciṟappu, nīttārperumi, aṟaṉvaliyuṟuttal eṉṉum pāyira vatikāraṅkaḷ nāṉkum eṭuttōtukiṉṟaṉa. ātalāl, tirukkuṟaṭkup pāyiran tēviyilli yeṉpatum, atu iṭic cerukaleṉpatum, ārāycciyillār kūṟṟēyeṉa aṟika.
pāyiram eṉṉum col, mutaṟkaṇ pōrmaṟavar pōrkkaḷattiṟ pakivari viḷittukkūṟum neṭumoḻiyeṉṉum maṟaviyal mukavuriyik kuṟittup piṉpu nūṉ mukavuriyik kuṟittatu.
payirtal = (ka) ūri vilaṅku paṟavikaḷ oṉṟiyoṉṟu
aḻittal. ceṅkāṟ palli taṉṟuṇi payirum (kuṟun. 16).
kaṭuvaṉ... mantiyik kiyiṭū up payirum  (puṟam.158)
pulamputaru kurala puṟavuppeṭi payirum  (kuṟun. 79)
(2) makkaḷi aḻittal.
nāṭṭiṟi payiruṅkāli muracam  (cilap.26:52)
payir - payiram - pāyiram = aḻippu, pōrukkaḻippu, pōruk kaḻikkum mukavuri, mukavuri.
o.nō: akavutal = aḻittal. akavu -ākavam = pōr.
maṟumaṉatta ṉallāta mānalatta vēntaṉ
uṟumaṉatta ṉāki yoḻukiṉ - ceṟumaṉattār
pāyiraṅ kūṟip paṭitokkā leṉceypa
āyiraṅ kākkikkōr kal.  (paḻamoḻi.246)
pāyiraṅ kūṟi eṉpataṟku vīrattiṟku vēṇṭum mukavurikaḷ
colli eṉṟu paḻiya vuriyāciyar poruḷ varintiruppati nōkkuka.
11. nūṟpakuppu
tirukkuṟaḷ, pāl eṉṉum mupperum pirivukaḷiyum, iyal eṉṉum eṇ ciṟu pirivukaḷiyum, atikāram eṉṉum 133 uṭciṟu pirivukaḷiyum uṭiyatu.  ovvōr atikāramum pappattuk kuṟaḷkaḷik koṇṭatu. āka, mottam 1330 kuṟaḷām.  pāyiramum ūḻum nūl muḻumikkum potuvām.
atikārak kaṇakku
pāl 	iyal		atikārat toki

uṟuppiyal amiccu (10), nāṭu (1), araṇ (1) poruḷ (kūḻ,1), paṭi (2), naṭpu (17), kuṭi (13) eṉa ēḻu pakutikaḷiyum 45 ati kāraṅkaḷiyum uṭiyatu. naṭpiṟpaki yum (14) aṭaṅkiyuḷḷatu. 
araciya liyin tamicciya līrin
turinā ṭaraṇporu ḷovvoṉ -ṟuricāl
paṭiyiraṇṭu naṭpup patiṉēḻpaṉ mūṉṟu
kuṭiyeḻupāṉ ṟokkaporuṭ kūṟu.
eṉpatu tiruvaḷḷuvamāli.
12. parimēlaḻakar naccuk karuttukkaḷ
aṟamāvatu maṉu mutaliya nūlkaḷil vitittaṉa ceytalum vilakkiyaṉa oḻitalumām.
oḻukkamāvatu antaṇar mutaliya varuṇattār tattamakku vitikkappaṭṭa piramacariya mutaliya nilikaḷiṉiṉṟu, avvavaṟṟiṟ kōtiya aṟaṅkaḷiṉ vaḻuvātoḻukutal.
atutāṉ (aṟam) nālvaki nilittāy varuṇantōṟum vēṟu pāṭuṭimiyiṉ, ciṟupāṉmiyākiya acciṟappiyalpu kaḷoḻittu ...... kūṟappaṭṭatu  (urippāyiram)
ivvāḻttu ēṟpuṭik kaṭavuḷi yeṉavaṟika; eṉṉi? cattuva mutaliya kuṇaṅkaḷāṉ mūṉṟākiya uṟutipporuṭku, avaṟṟāṉ mūvarākiya mutaṟkaṭavuḷōṭu iyipuṇṭākalāṉ.  ammūṉṟu poruḷiyuṅ kūṟaluṟṟārkku ammūvariyum vāḻttutal muṟimiyākaliṉ, ivvāḻttu ammūvarkkum potuppaṭak kūṟiṉāreṉavuṇarka (kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu atikāra mukavuri).
tamiḻeḻuttiṟkēyaṉṟi vaṭaveḻuttiṟkum mutalātal nōkki eḻuttellā  meṉṟār. (1) tattuvamirupattintiṉiyun teritalāvatu,...... .cāṅkiya nūluḷōtiyavāṟṟāṉ ārāytal (27).
ēṉi mūvarāvār, ācāriyaṉiṭattiṉiṉ ṟōtutalum virataṅ kāttalumākiya piramacariya voḻukkattāṉum, illiviṭṭu vaṉattiṉ kaṭṭīyoṭu ceṉṟu maṉiyāḷ vaḻipaṭat tavañ ceyyumoḻuk kattāṉum, muṟṟattuṟanta yōkavoḻuk kattāṉumeṉa ivar. (41)
pitirarāvār paṭippukkālattu ayaṉāṟ paṭikkappaṭṭatōr
kaṭavuṭcāti: avarkkiṭam teṉṟiciyātaliṉ, teṉpulattā reṉṟār.  (43)
putalvarip peṟutal - aḵtāvatu, irupiṟappāḷar mūvarāṉum iyalpāka viṟukkappaṭūuṅ kaṭaṉ mūṉṟaṉuḷ, muṉivarkaṭaṉ kēḷviyāṉum, tēvarkaṭaṉ vēḷviyāṉum, teṉpulattār kaṭaṉ putalvarip peṟutalāṉu mallatu iṟukkap paṭāmiyiṉ, akkaṭaṉiṟuttaṟ poruṭṭu naṉmakkaḷip peṟutal.  (makkaṭ pēṟṟatikāra mukavuri)
makkaḷeṉṉum peyar peṇṇoḻittu niṉṟatu  (41)
peṇṇiyalpāṟ ṟāṉāka vaṟiyāmiyiṟ kēṭṭatā yeṉavuṅ kūṟiṉār  (46)

tīyacoṟkaḷāvaṉa.....................tUz¤â‰F uriyavallaṉavumām.  (139).
iṉi maṉu mutaliya aṟanūlkaḷāl potuvākak kūṟappaṭṭa illaṟaṅkaḷellām ivar tokuttukkūṟiya ivaṟṟuḷnḷa aṭaṅkum.  (240)
vētamum aṟamum anāti.  (543)
pacukkaḷ pālkuṉṟiyavaḻi aviyiṉmiyāṉum, atu koṭuttaṟ kuriyār mantiraṅ kaṟpameṉpaṉa ōtāmiyāṉum, vēḷvi naṭavātām; ākavē, vāṉam peyalollā teṉpatāyiṟṟu.  (560).
tēvarkkum acurarkkum amiccup pūṇṭa viyāḻa veḷḷi kaḷatu tuṇivu tokuttup piṉ nūluṭiyārkūṟiyavāṟukūṟukiṉṟamiyiṉ,īṇṭuviṉittūymiyumuṭaṉkūṟiṉār. (660).
vēntaṉ mēya tīmpuṉa lulakamum eṉṟār piṟarum.nakuṭaṉeṉ pāṉ intirapatam peṟṟuc celkiṉṟa kālattup peṟṟa kaḷippu mikutiyāṉ akattiyaṉ vekuḷvatōr piḻiceya, ataṉāṟ cāpa meyti appatam iṭiyē iḻantāṉeṉpataṉi yuṭkoṇṭu ivvāṟu kūṟiṉār.  (899).
peṇpālākkiyatu vaṭamoḻi muṟimi paṟṟi.  (624)
viṉivayattāṟ pañcapūta pariṇāmamākiya yākkiyip porunti niṉṟu atiṉ payaṉaṉupavittal ellā varuṇattārkku mottaliṟ piṟappokku meṉṟum ...... kūṟiṉār. (972).
vaṭanūlār aṅkameṉṟamiyiṉ uṟup peṉṟār. (993)
kāmattuppāl - itu puṇarcci piriveṉa viruvakippaṭum.  ēṉi iruttal, iraṅkal, ūṭa leṉpaṉavōveṉiṉ, ivar poruṭpākupāṭṭiṉi aṟam poruḷiṉpameṉa vaṭanūl vaḻakkup paṟṟi yōtutalāṉ, avvāṟē yavaṟṟip piriviṉ kaṇaṭakkiṉāreṉka. iṉi, avi tammiyē tamiḻ 
nūlkaḷōṭum poruntap puṇarcciyik kaḷaveṉṟum  pirivik kaṟpeṉṟum perumpāṉmi paṟṟi vakuttu, avaṟṟic cuvi mikuti payappa ulaka naṭiyōṭu oppu movvāmiyu muṭiyavākkik kūṟukiṉṟār.  (kāmattuppāl mukavuri).
īṇṭup piriviṉi vaṭanūṉmatam paṟṟic celavu āṟṟāmi vituppup pulaviyeṉa nālvakit tākkik kūṟiṉār.  avaṟṟuṭ celavu pirivāṟṟāmi yuḷḷum, āṟṟāmi paṭarmelintiraṅkaṉ mutal niṟiyaḻita līṟāyavaṟṟuḷḷum, vituppu avarvayiṉ vitumpaṉ mutaṟ puṇarcci vitumpa līṟāyavaṟṟuḷḷum, pulavi neñcoṭu pulattaṉ mutal ūṭaluvaki yīṟāyavaṟṟuḷḷuṅ kaṇṭu koḷka. aḵtēl, vaṭanūlār ivaṟṟuṭaṉē cāpattiṉāṉāya nīkkattiyuṅ 
kūṭṭip piriviṉi aivakit teṉṟārāleṉiṉ, aḵtu aṟamporuḷiṉpa meṉṉum payaṉkaḷuḷ oṉṟu paṟṟiya pirivaṉmiyāṉum, muṉivarāṇi yāṉ orukālat tōrkuṟṟat tuḷatāvatallatu ulakiyalpāy vārāmiyāṉum, īṇṭoḻikkap paṭṭateṉka. (kāmattuppāl muṭivuri).
ēṉik karuttukkaḷi āṅkāṅku uriyiṟ kāṇka.  (ti.ta.ma.)
tirukkuṟaṭ ciṟappuc coṟkaḷum collāṭciyum
ovvoru perunūlilum ōriru allatu orucila coṟkaḷiyum collāṭciyiyum ciṟappākak kāṇkiṉṟōm. tirukkuṟaḷi luḷḷa ciṟappuc coṟkaḷiyum collāṭciyiyum eṭuttuk kūṟuvatu itu.
nikaṇṭu eṉṉum vaṭacoṟ peyarāṟ kuṟikkappaṭum. uriccoṟ ṟokutikaḷum, uriyāciriyaṉmār urikaḷum, piṟa nūlkaḷi luḷḷa coṟkaḷi yellām tokuttum eṭuttum kūṟum iyalpuṭiyaṉa vātaliṉ, tirukkuṟaṭ ciṟappuc colvarātaṉa veṉṟu vilakkappaṭṭavi.  av viruvaki yoḻinta ēṉiya nūlkaḷum paṉuvalkaḷumē.
1. ciṟapput taṉiccoṟkaḷ.
kaḵcu = kāṟpalam
toṭippuḻuti kaḵcā vuṇakkiṟ piṭitteruvum
vēṇṭātu cālap paṭum  (1037)
kūḻ = celvam, poruḷ, poṉ.
paṭikuṭi kūḻamiccu naṭpara ṇāṟum
uṭiyā ṉaracaru ḷēṟu.  (381)
kūḻuṅ kuṭiyu moruṅkiḻakkuṅ kōlkōṭic
cūḻātu ceyyu maracu  (554)
uṇavik kuṟikkum kūḻ eṉṉuñ col kuḻi eṉṉum mutaṉilit tiripātalālum, uṇavu eṉṉuñ coṟkuc celvam eṉṉum poruḷiṉmi yālum,
palvaki yuṇavum payirum poṉṉum
koḷpa mātō kūḻeṉ kiḷavi
eṉṟu tivākaraṅ kūṟavatālum, celvattik kuṟikkum col uṇavup peyariṉ vēṟu eṉṟu koḷḷavum iṭamuṇṭu.  iṉi kuḻivāṉa (ductile) tātu (ulōkam) poṉ eṉṟumām: allatu iṉṟiyamiyāta aṭippaṭic celvam uṇavē eṉiṉum okkum.
koṭkutal = veḷippaṭutal.
kaṭikkoṭkac ceytakka tāṇmi yiṭikoṭkiṉ
eṟṟā viḻuman tarum  (663)
tavveṉal = tāḻtal, curuṅkutal.
kavviyāṟ kavvitu kāmam atuviṉṟēl
tavveṉṉun taṉmi yiḻantu  (1144)
ōci vakiyik kuṟikkum tav eṉṉum kuṟippuccol vēṟu.  ātalāl, tavveṉṉum eṉpatu kuṟippu moḻi:
 nūlkāl yātta māli veṇkuṭi
tavveṉ ṟaciit tāḻtuḷi maṟippa
eṉpuḻiyum atu,eṉṟu parimēlaḻakar kūṟuvatu poruntātu.  tavval eṉṉum peyar irutiṇiyilum iḷami kuṟittalālum, tavvutal eṉṉum viṉikkuk kuṟital, kuvital, tavaṟutal, keṭutal eṉṉum poruḷkaḷiruttalālum, nelli vaṭṭattil oru pāṭattil tāḻvāyirukkum māṇavaṉi ap pāṭattil tavvaleṉṟu kūṟum vaḻakkuṇmiyālum, tav eṉpati iṅku oru viṉip paṇpuk kuṟippeṉṟu koḷvatē poruttamām.
tōl = paḻamiyāṉa pukaḻ, perumi, tol - tōl, toṉmi = paḻimi. 
tolvaravun tōluṅ keṭukkun tokiyāka 
nalkura veṉṉum naci.  (1043)
iḻumeṉ moḻiyāṉ viḻumiyatu nuvaliṉum
paranta moḻiyāṉ aṭinimirn toḻukiṉum

eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpā oru vaki vaṉappiṉ  (kāviyattiṉ) ilakkaṇattik kūṟuvatāl, ataṉoṭu tirukkuṟaṭ collit toṭarpu paṭuttutal tavaṟām.
mukaṭi = mūtēvi (cēṭṭi).
maṭiyuḷāḷ māmukaṭi yeṉpa maṭiyilāṉ
tāḷuḷāḷ tāmariyi ṉāḷ  (617)
ati = miku, mika.
matinuṭpa nūlō ṭuṭiyārk katinuṭpam
yāvuḷa muṉṉiṟ pavi  (636)
atiyeṉpatu vaṭacolluḷ mikutip poruḷatōriṭic col: atu tirintu nuṭpameṉpataṉōṭu tokkatu eṉṟār parimēlaḻakar. mikutip poruḷ tarum vaṭamoḻi muṉṉoṭṭu (upacarkkam - (Prefix) ati, adhi eṉṟu iru vaṭivi liruppa taṉālum, mikutalik kuṟikkum atikarittal eṉṉum viṉiccol vaṭamoḻi yiliṉmiyālum, atikam eṉpataṟ kiṉamāṉa ataṉam (ati +aṉam) eṉṉum toḻiṟpeyar tamiḻilēyēyiruttalāṉum, atikaṉ eṉṉum tamiḻac ciṟṟaracar kuṭippeyar mikkōṉ allatu periyōṉ eṉṟu poruḷ koṇṭirukka lāmātalāṉum, ati eṉpatip perumpālum otta ati eṉṉum tamiḻ viṉiccol vīṅkutal (paruttal), mikutal eṉṉum poruḷ tarutalāṉum, ati eṉṉum col orukāl oru vaḻakkaṟṟa paḻantamiḻ viṉiyāyirukkumō eṉṉum aiyam nikaḻkiṉṟatu.
iṉi,atiṉuṭpam eṉṟu pāṭaṅ koṇṭu, mēṟkūṟiya nūṟ kalviyōṭu kūṭa nuṇṇitākiya matiyiṉiyum uṭiyārkku, ataṉiṉum nuṇṇiyavāy māṟṟārā leṇṇappaṭṭu etirniṟkum viṉikaḷ yāvuḷa? eṉṉum maṇakkuṭavar uri, colvaki yilum poruḷ vakiyilum parimēḻakaruriyiṉuñ cālac ciṟantatām.  ip poruḷil ati eṉṉum muṉṉoṭṭiṟkē iṭamilli.  parimēlaḻakar oru teṉcolli vaṭacollākku vāṉ vēṇṭi, iṉi, atiṉuṭpameṉṟu pāṭamōti, ataṉi ṉuṭpam yāveṉṟurippārumuḷar. avar cūḻccikkiṉamāy muṉ cuṭṭappaṭuva toṉṟillāmiyum, cuṭṭup peyar aintā murupēṟṟavaḻi avvāṟu nillāmiyu maṟintilar. eṉṟu maṇakkuṭava ruriyi maṟuttiruppatu, avariyun tākkutal kāṇka.

paṇṭik kālattil ulakameṅkum kālnaṭiyē celvamākak karutappaṭṭatiṉāl, māṭu eṉṉum vilaṅkup peyarē celvam eṉṉum poruḷit taḻuviṟṟeṉṟu koḷvatē mikap poruttamām.

miyāttal = mayaṅkutal.

nākarikam = paṇpāṭu.
ic colli ip poruḷilēyē tiruvaḷḷuvar āṇṭiruk kiṉṟār. kaṇṇōṭṭam oru paṇpāṭṭiyal yeṉpati yaṟika.  nākarikam eṉṉuñ coṟkuk kaṇṇōṭṭam eṉpatu nērp poruḷaṉṟu.  (tiruvaḷḷuvar niṉivu malar.)
tirukkuṟaṭ potukkūṟukaḷ
pāyirattil vāṉciṟappu, aṟattuppālil illaṟaviyal, poruṭpāvil araciyaloḻinta pakutikaḷ, iṉpattuppāliṟ cila kaṟpiyalatikāraṅkaḷ ākiyavi ellārkkum potuvām.
tirukkuṟaḷ araciyal
ikkālattaraciyal aracuḷḷa oru nāṭṭi State mutalākavum āḷnilam Territory, kuṭikaḷ Population aracu Government,nfh‹ik Sovereignty, oṟṟumi Unity eṉṉum aintiyum ciṉiyākavum koṇṭuḷḷatu. akkālat taraciyalō aracaṉiyē mutalākavum, kuṭi poruḷ (kūḻ) paṭi, araṇ, amiccu, naṭpu eṉṉum āṟiyum ciṉiyākavuṅ koṇṭiruntatu. āḷnilam kuṭiyuḷ aṭakkappaṭṭatu. ceṅkōlō koṭuṅkōlō ōccum amiccuk kōvaracē aṟṟiyaracu vakiyām.
ceṅkōlaracaṉ, amiccaṉ paṭittalivaṉ tūtaṉ oṟṟaṉ ākiyōri aṟamporuḷiṉpa vuyiraccameṉṉum nālvakit tērtiṟaṅkaḷāl tērnteṭuttu, nāṭṭuvaḷattiyum varuvāyiyum perukki vanta poruḷkaḷi aṟam poruḷiṉ paṅkaṭkuc celaviṭṭu, ciṟanta paṭiyaraṇ amittu, kāṭcik keḷiyaṉāyk kaṭuñcollaṉallaṉāy naṭuniliyākak kuṟṟavāḷiyit taṇṭittu muṟiceytu, ciṟṟiṉañcērātu periyārit tuṇikkoṇṭu cuṟṟantaḻuvi naṭpiṟ piḻipoṟuttu, nālvaki āmpuṭikaḷik kiyāṇṭu piṟavaracaroṭu paḻaki, pētip peṇvaḻic cellātu potumakaḷiroṭu kūṭātu, kaḷḷuṇṇātu, cūtāṭātu, aḷavaṟintuṇṭu nōyvarāmaṟ kāttu, taḷarnta kuṭikaḷik kitūkkic ciṟanta amiccarkkum paṭittalivarkkum peruñciṟappuc ceytu, aivakit tuṉpamum varāmal aṉpākak kuṭikaḷik kāttal vēṇṭumeṉpatē tirukkuṟaḷ araciyalām.
iṟṟi yaracu elvevvakiyāyiruppiṉum, ceṅkōṉmi yip poṟutta maṭṭil tirukkuṟaḷmuṟiyē ellāvaṟṟiṟkum ēṟpatām.
iṉi, naṭpu eṉpatu puṟattuṟuppāyiṉum, īrāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉ tiruvaḷḷuvar vaṟpuṟuttiyavāṟē iṉṟum atu vallaracu kaṭkum iṉṟiyami yātatāyiruppati, amerikka oṉṟiya nāṭukaḷum iraciyāvum ēṉi nāṭukaḷit tamvayappaṭuttu vataṟku ellā muyaṟcikaḷum iṭiviṭātu ceytuvarutalāl aṟika.
tirukkuṟaḷiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭa tol (purāṇak) katikaḷ
arukaṉ pūmēl naṭantatu.
vēntaṉ (intiraṉ) muṉivaṉatu āṟṟalukkuc cāṉṟāṉatu.
tirumāl mūvulakum īraṭiyā laḷantatu(?)
tirukkuṟaḷiṉ taṉṉēriṉmi
ellā nāṭṭārkkum ekkālattiṟkum ēṟṟavāṟu, illaṟa vāḻkkiyum ceṅkōlāṭciyum eṭuttuk kūṟavatu tirukkuṟaḷ oṉṟē. nālvētamum pāratamum maṉutaruma cāttiramum tirukkuṟaṭku oppākumeṉṟu tiruvaḷḷuva mālic ceyyuṭkaḷ kūṟuvatu avaṟṟip pāṭiyavariṉ aṟiyāmi, aṭimittaṉam, oppu nōkkun tiṟamiṉmi ākiyavaṟṟiyē oruṅkē kāṭṭum.
iṉi, ikkālattum, āriyarun tamiḻarumāṉa palar pakavaṟkītiyit tirukkuṟaṭku oppākak kūṟat tuṇikiṉṟaṉar.
kaṇṇapirāṉ aruccuṉaṉukku aṟivuṟuttiyatellām, pōrk kaḷattiṟ porumaṟavaṉ pakippaṭiyiluḷḷa uṟaviṉari nōkkātu ūkkamākap porutu taṉ kaṭamiyic  cevvaṉē niṟivēṟṟavēṇṭumeṉpatē. itip piṟkālattu āriya iṉa veṟiyaṉoruvaṉ peritum payaṉpaṭuttikkoṇṭu, pirāmaṇaṉ cattiriyaṉ viciyaṉ cūttiraṉ eṉṉum nāl varaṇattiyum iṟivaṉē paṭittāṉeṉṉum naccuk karutti, atiṟ cūḻcciyākap pukuttiyirukkiṉṟāṉ.
nālvaruṇam iṟivaṉ paṭippaṉmikkuc cāṉṟukaḷ
(1)	mutaṟkāla anākarika māntar vakuppu vēṟupāṭiṉṟiyē yiruntami.
(2)	iyaṟkiyāṉa vakuppuvēṟupāṭu niṟattālaṉṟit toḻilā lēyē oru nāṭṭil ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷami.
(3) 	toḻilukkup piṟappoṭu toṭarpiṉmi.
(4)	toḻil viruppappaṭiyum tiṟamippaṭiyum māṟṟak kūṭiyatā yiruttal.
(5)	kalvi orē kulattiṟkuc ciṟappurimiyākātu ellā vakup pārkkum potuvāyiruttal.
(6)	āriya nālvaraṇap pakuppu ulakil pirāmaṇaruḷḷa intiyāvil maṭṭu miruttal.
(7)	āriya nālvaraṇattiṟku mūlamāṉa (antaṇar aracar vaṇikar vēḷāḷar eṉṉum) tamiḻa nāṟpāṟ pakupput toḻiliyē aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭiruntami.
(8)	aṟivu vaḷarcciyiṉāl nālvaraṇap pakuppup paṭippaṭiyākac citiyuṇṭu varutal.
iṅṅaṉamiruppatāl, kaṇṇapirāṉ kaṭavuḷiṉ tōṟṟaravāyiṉ (avatāra māyiṉ), nāṉē nālvaraṇattiyum paṭittēṉeṉṟu kūṟiyirukka muṭiyātu; kūṟiyiruppiṉ kaṭavuḷiṉ tōṟṟaravā yiruntirukka muṭiyātu.
pakavaṟkīti patiṉeṇṇatikāraṅkaḷiyum eḻunūṟu colavaṅ kaḷiyum (culōkaṅkaḷiyum) uṭiyatu. itilaṭaṅkiyuḷḷa virivāṉa ceytiyi oru meypporuḷiyal mānāṭṭuttalivar kūṭac colliyirukka muṭiyātu.  oru māperum  pōrkkaḷattil irutiṟattum nālvakip paṭikaḷum aṇiyamākip pōrukku muṉintu niṟkum vēḷiyil, ittakiya ōtuvam (upatēcam) orupakkap paṭiyiṉ muṉṉaṇiyil naṭipeṟṟateṉiṉ ati nampupavaṉ paramārttakuru māṇavaṉākavē yiruttal vēṇṭum.
mēlum, aruccuṉaṉukkum kaṇṇapirāṉukkum iṭiyē naṭi peṟṟa uri yāṭṭic cañcayaṉ kēṭṭut tirutarāṭṭiraṉukku avvappōtu aṟivittā ṉeṉṟiruppatu, ikkālattu oli perukkiyum (Microphone) urattolippāṉkaḷum 
(Loudspeakers) niṟinta mānāṭṭuc coṟpoḻivoṉṟi, avikkōṭi yiluḷḷa oruvaṉ kēṭṭu arukiluḷḷa kuṭuṭṭuc ceviṭaṉukku eṭuttuc colvatu pōṉṟē irukkiṉṟatu.
tamiḻakattu iruperumataṅkaḷuḷ mūttatāṉa civaṉiyam (civam) kaṇṇapirāṉiṉ tōṟṟaravut taṉmiyi oppukkoḷḷavu milli.
pakavaṟkītiyiṉ ceyti amippu naṭi mutaliyavaṟṟi nōkkiṉ, kārpu (Garbe) kūṟiyuḷḷavāṟu, annūl ki.mu. 2ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟik ki.pi.2ām nūṟṟāṇṭil 
virivupaṭuttap peṟṟateṉṟu koḷvatē poruttamām. aṉṟi, ki.mu.5ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiyateṉak koḷvārum, ataṉpiṉ mēṉmēlum virivum tiruttamum peṟṟu vantuḷḷamiyi maṟukka muṭiyātu.  ki.mu.10ām nūṟṟāṇṭil nikaḻnta oru pōriṉ ciṟu nikaḻcciyip piṟkālattup pirāmaṇaṉoruvaṉ payaṉpaṭuttikkoṇṭu, nālvaraṇatti nilināṭṭa oru perumuyaṟci ceytuḷḷāṉ eṉpatē uṇmiyāṉa ceytiyām.  pakavaṟkītiyiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa oḻukkaneṟikaḷum meypporuḷ viḷakkaṅkaḷum, iṟantupaṭṭa tamiḻ ōttukkaḷiṉiṉṟu eṭut tāṇṭaviyē eṉa aṟika.   (ti.ta.ma.)

tirukkōvi
tirucciṟampalak kōvi piṟa kōvikaḷ pōṉṟē akapporuṭ kōviyākat tōṉṟiṉum akanōkkil paḻutta ātaṉiyum (āṉmāviyum) ataṟkup pēriṉpam tarum iṟivaṉiyum muṟiyē kiḷavittalivaṉ taliviyarākak koṇṭa uruvaka nāṭakamē (Allegory).  (ta. i.va:77)
tiruttakkal
teṉṉāṭṭil yārēṉum oruvar ilakkaṇa vaḻuppaṭap pēciyati vēṟoruvar tiruttiṉāl tiruttappaṭṭavar tiruttiyavari ivar tiruttak kalliṟkut teṟkiṭṭup piṟantavar eṉṟu nakiyāṭuvatu vaḻakkam. itaṉāl, paṇṭit tamiḻakattil centamiḻ vaḻaṅkum elli kuṟikkak kalnaṭṭappaṭṭirunta teṉpatum teṟkē cellac cellat tamiḻ ciṟantirunta teṉpatum veḷiyākum.  (col.27).
tirunāṉ maṟi ārāycci
civaṉiyam māliyam eṉṉum iru tamiḻa mataṅkaḷiṉiṉṟum āriyak kūṟukaḷi nīkkum muyaṟci, tiruvaḷḷuvar kālattiṉiṉṟu toṭarntu varukiṉṟatu.
pallavapuram tavattiru. maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ, tiruvācakap pōṟṟit tiru vakavalukkut tāṅkaḷ varinta virivuriyil, mūvā nāṉmaṟi mutalvā pōṟṟi eṉṉum aṭiyiluḷḷa mūvā nāṉmaṟi eṉṉun toṭarccol, tolkāppiyam, iṟiyaṉārakap poruḷ, tirukkuṟaḷ, tēvāram, civañāṉa pōtam eṉṉum nūlkaḷik kuṟikkumeṉṟum, vaṭamoḻi nāṉku vētaṅkaḷik kuṟikkāteṉṟum, kūṟiṉārkaḷ. atit tirucci mā. cāmpacivap piḷḷi etirttu oru maṟuppuri veḷiyiṭṭār.
ataṉpiṉ, pērā. kā. cuppiramaṇiyap piḷḷi centamiḻc celvi mutaṟ cilampil,tiru nāṉmaṟi viḷakkam eṉṉum talippil, nāṉmaṟi eṉpaṉa tamiḻ nūlkaḷē yeṉṟu oru toṭar kaṭṭuri veḷiyiṭu vittār.  ataṉ karuttu:
civaperumāṉ, nam tamiḻnāṭṭil, oru kallāla marattiṉ kīḻt tamiḻmakkaḷ nālvarkku aṟamporuḷiṉpam vīṭeṉṉum uṟutip poruḷ nāṉkiṉiyum uṇarttiṉār.  at tamiḻaṟiñar, tirukkuṟaḷ pōṉṟa nāṉmaṟi nūlkaḷiyum, avaṟṟiṟkut tuṇiyāka aṟuvaki uṟuppu nūlkaḷiyum, iyaṟṟiṉar.  avi talikkaḻakak kālattuk kaṭal kōḷil, kaḻaka nūlkaḷuṭaṉ aḻintu pōyiṉa.  nam civaṉiyak kuravar karuttum ituvē. - eṉpatēyām.
itiyum cāmpacivap piḷḷi maṟuttu, tiru nāṉmaṟi viḷakka ārāyccieṉṉum 236 pakka etir nūloṉṟu veḷiyiṭṭār.  annūṟku, avar karuttiṟkiṇaṅkip pala pirāmaṇa aṟiñarum, nellic citampara rāmaliṅkam piḷḷi, taccanallur ilakkumaṇap pōṟṟi aiyā, ampācamuttiram kāntimati nātappiḷḷi, tirucci ā. cuppiramaṇiyap piḷḷi, uṟantip pulavar periyacāmip piḷḷi, kōvi (C.K)R¥ãukÂa mutaliyār, tañci arāva āṇṭaki (rāv pahatūr) (K.S) cīnivācap piḷḷi, cīkāḻip pulavar muttut tāṇṭa varāyap piḷḷi, uyir nūlāciriyar pa.mu. cōmacuntarampiḷḷi, ca.catācivamutaliyār, yāḻppāṇam civapātacuntaram piḷḷi, kilāca piḷḷi, vittiya liṅkam piḷḷi, aruḷampalam, āṟumukam piḷḷi, kaṇēca paṇṭitar, cuvāmināta paṇṭitar ākiya tamiḻa aṟiñarum, naṉ matippuriyum pārāṭṭuriyum vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷaṉar.
nālvētam allatu nāṉmaṟi, āṟaṅkam, ākamam eṉpaṉa āriya nūlkaḷē eṉpatum, tirukkuṟaḷ tavira itupōtuḷḷa maṟṟip paṇṭi nūlkaḷilellām antaṇar eṉpatu pirāmaṇariyē kuṟikkum eṉpatum, cariyē.  āyiṉ, āriyac cār pāṉa iṟṟip paṇṭi nūlka ḷellām, āriyar teṉṉāṭu vantu tammi nilat tēvareṉṟum tam moḻiyit tēva moḻi yeṉṟum colli ēmāṟṟi, tam mēpāṭṭi nāṭṭi matatti yum varalāṟṟiyum tiritta piṉṉarē iyaṟṟap paṭṭaviyāta lāl, avaṟṟi mutaṉūlkaḷeṉa mayaṅki, avaṟṟiṟkumuṉ tamiḻ nūlkaḷ iruntatilli yeṉṟu koḷvatu, varalāṟ ṟaṟivum moḻi yārāycciyum iṉmiyāl nērnta viḷivām.  ivviraṇṭum pōtiya aḷavu iṉmiyālēyē, āriya ēmāṟṟi yum ataṉāl tamiḻar aṭintu varum peruṅ kēṭṭiyum kaṇṭa aṭikaḷum pērāciriyarum, iṉanala naṉṉōkkamākavē, nāṉmaṟi tamiḻark kuriyaviyallaveṉṟum, iṟantupaṭṭa tamiḻ nūlkaḷeṉṟum,  kūṟalāyiṉar.
tirumantiram āriyam tamiḻakattil vērūṉṟiya 6-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu nūlātalāl,
māriyuṅ kōṭiyum vārpaṉi tūṅkaniṉ
ṟēriyu niṉṟaṅ kiḷikkiṉṟa kālattu
vāriya muntami ḻummuṭa ṉēcollik
kāriki yārkkuṅ karuṇicey tāṉē
aviḻkkiṉṟa vāṟum atukiṭṭu māṟum
cimiṭṭalip paṭṭuyir pōkiṉṟa vāṟum
tamiḻccol vaṭacol leṉumiv viraṇṭum 
uṇarttu mavaṉi yuṇaralu māmē
antaṇa rāvōr aṟutoḻil pūṇṭuḷōr
centaḻa lōmpimup pōtu niyamañcey
tantava naṟkaru mattuniṉ ṟāṅkiṭṭuc
cantiyu mōtic caṭaṅkaṟup pārkaḷē
peruneṟi yāṉa piraṇava mōrntu
kuruneṟi yāluri kūṭināl vētat 
tiruneṟi yāṉa tiruki yiruttic
corūpama tāṉōr tukaḷilpārp pārē.
eṉpavaṟṟik kaṇēca paṇṭitarum,
civamām parattiṉiṟ catti catācivam
uvamā makēca ruruttira tēvar
tavamāl piramīcar tammiṟṟām peṟṟa
navavā kamameṅkaḷ nantipeṟ ṟāṉē.
eṉpatic cuvāmināta paṇṭitarum, tiruvācakam tēvāram mutaliya piṟa paṉuvaṟ ceyyuṭkaḷip piṟarum, tam koḷkikkuc cāṉṟu kāṭṭip payaṉilli.
mūlaṉ uri ceyta mūvā yirantamiḻ eṉṟu ciṟappuppāyiram kūṟuvatāl, tirumantira maṇṭilaṅkaḷ (viruttaṅkaḷ) mottam mūvāyiramē.  vē.vicuvanātap piḷḷi patippil 3047 mantiraṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ci.ci.nū.pa.kaḻakap patippil mūvāyiramē uḷḷaṉa.  avaṟṟuḷ, aviḻkkiṉṟa vāṟum, antaṇa rāvōr eṉṉumiraṇṭum iṭam peṟavilli. avi iṭic cerukal pōlum!
vaṭamoḻiyip pāṇiṉikku vakuttaruḷi yataṟkiṇiyāt
toṭarpuṭiya teṉmoḻiyi yulakamelān toḻutēttum
kuṭamuṉikku valiyuṟuttār kollēṟṟup pākar ... ...
irumoḻikkuṅ kaṇṇutalār mutaṟkuravar... ... ... ...
eṉṉum civañāṉa muṉivar kūṟṟukkaḷ ikkālattiṟ kēṟkā.
maṉṉu māmali makēntira mataṉiṟ 
coṉṉa vākaman tōṟṟuvit taruḷiyum
eṉṟu pāṭiṉār, ki.pi. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟutiyiṟ pāṇṭi nāṭṭiṟ piṟantu vaḷarntu, tamiḻi muṟṟak kaṟṟu, varakuṇa pāṇṭiyaṉiṉ talimi mantiriyārā yiruntu, ciṟanta civaṉaṭiyārāka māṟiya māṇikka vācakar.
makēntiramali teṟkē mūḻkippōṉa kumarimalit toṭariṉ vaṭapakuti yeṉpatu,
tuṅkamali potiyatteṉpāṟ ṟoṭarntavaṭi vārattiṉ
aṅkaṉaka ilaṅkiyumēḻ variccāra laṭittēcam
eṉṉum civa tarumōttira (kōpura. 48) aṭikaḷāl aṟiyappaṭum. mikavum uyarn tirukkiṟa potiya malikkut teṟkākiya mayēntirakiriyiṉ aṭivārattil, aḻakiya poṉmaya mākiya ilaṅki yeṉṉun tēcamum poruntum. eṉṉum avvaṭi kaḷil uriyi nōkkuka.
ki.pi.2-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṭi vari iruntatākat terikiṉṟa kumariyāṟu, makēntiram eṉa vaṭavar vaḻaṅkum kumarimalip pakutiyi ṉiṉṟē eḻuntōṭi, ilaṅkit tīvu piriyātu peru nilattōṭu cērntirunta kālattil, ataṉūṭu pāyntu kaṭaliṟ kalantiruttal vēṇṭum.
kumari yāṟṟiṉ oru pakuti ōṭik koṇṭirunta kālattilēyē āriyar oru cilar teṉṉāṭṭiṟku vantu viṭṭārēṉum, makēntiram eṉṉum kumarimaliyiṟ civa perumāṉiṭam allatu avararuḷ peṟṟa nanti pōṉṟa kuravaṉiṭam, koḷki maṟiyum toḻuki maṟiyum paṟṟip pāṭaṅ kēṭṭavar taṉit tamiḻp peyar tāṅkiya tamiḻarākavē yiruntiruttal vēṇṭum.
iṉṟuḷḷa vaṭamoḻic civākamaṅkaḷ ki.pi.5-ām nūṟṟāṇ ṭiṟkup piṉṉarē tōṉṟiṉa.  avaṟṟuḷ mūlamāṉavi toṇṭu (oṉpatu) eṉpatum, ēṉip patiṉ ṟoṇṭum (pattoṉpatum) avaṟṟiṉ vaḻip piṉṉart tōṉṟiṉa eṉpatum,
peṟṟanal ākamam kāraṇam kāmikam
uṟṟanal vīram uyarcintam vātuḷam
maṟṟav viyāmaḷam ākumkā lōttaram
tuṟṟanaṟ cuppiram collum makuṭamē  (73)
ākamam oṉpāṉ atilāṉa nālēḻu
mōkamil nālēḻu muppēta muṟṟuṭaṉ
vēkamil vētānta cittānta meymmiyoṉ
ṟāka muṭinta aruñcutta civamē  (74)
eṉṉun tiruntiraṅkaḷāṉ aṟiyappaṭum.
muṉpētam - mū vēṟupāṭu.  avi karumak kāṇṭam, vaḻipāṭṭuk kāṇṭam, aṟivak kāṇṭam eṉpaṉa.
civaṉiyam āriyar intiyāviṟkuṭ pukumuṉṉarē kumarināṭṭil tōṉṟiya matamātalāl, kumarimalit toṭariṉ vaṭamuṭi yoṉṟākiya makēntira maliyil, iṟiva ṉaruḷ peṟṟa kuravaṉiṭam, tolkāppiyar kālamākiya ki.mu. ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṉ pāṭaṅkēṭṭavar tamiḻarākavē yiruntatu maṭṭumaṉṟi, avar pāṭaṅ kēṭṭatum ati nūlāka varintatum taṉit tamiḻ vāyilākavē nikaḻn tiruttal vēṇṭum. annūl allatu nūlkaḷē, piṟkālattu vaṭamoḻi yākamaṅ kaṭku mūlamāyirun tiruttal vēṇṭum. ammūla nūlkaḷ yāvum āriyarāl aḻikkappaṭṭu viṭṭaṉa.
pirāmaṇarē ōtavum ōtuvikkavum, iru vakic caṭaṅkum kōvil vaḻipāṭum naṭattavum, ivvakiyil tamiḻari eṉṟum aṭimip paṭuttavum, vēṇṭumeṉṉuṅ kuṟikkōḷuṭaṉēyē, vaṭamoḻi yākamaṅkaḷ tōṟṟuvikkap paṭṭaṉa.
ākamam eṉṉum vaṭacoṟkut tōṉṟiyatu eṉṟē poruḷ.  tōṉṟaṟ puṇarcciyi vaṭamoḻiyāḷar ākama canti eṉṟu kūṟutal kāṇka.  kuṟiyīṭṭu muṟiyil ākamattit tōṉṟiyam eṉṉalām. tōṉṟiyatu eṉṉum poruḷālēyē, ākamaṅkaḷ vaṭanāṭṭu āriyar teṉṉāṭu vantapiṉ putitākat tōṉṟiyavi eṉpati, ākamam eṉṉuñ col uṇarttutal kāṇka.
iṉi, ākamam eṉṉum vaṭacollum teṉcol aṭi vēri ṉiṉṟē tōṉṟiya teṉpati, kīḻ varuñ col varalāṟṟāl tērntu teḷika.
uytal = cellutal. uyttal = celuttutal. uy - iy-iya-iyavu = (1) celavu. iṭineṟik kiṭanta iyavukkoṇ maruṅ kil (cilap.11:168). (2) vaḻi. iyaviṭi varuvōṉ (maṇi. 13:16).
iya-iyal. iyalutal = (1) cellutal. (2) naṭattal. arivi yoṭu mela viyali  (aiṅ.175. (3).  kūṭiyatātal.
iyavu - (iyaku) - iyaṅku. iyaṅkutal = cellutal. iyaṅku - iyakku - iyakkam = celavu.
iyavu - iyavuḷ = (1) vaḻi. (2) naṭattuvōṉ, talivaṉ, iṟivaṉ. periyō rēttum perumpeya riyavuḷ (tirumuru. 274)
iyavu - ey - eytu. eytutal = naṭattal, nikaḻtal, ceṉṟaṭital, aṭital.
ey-ē. ētal = pōtal. iv viṉi iṉṟu vaḻakkaṟṟatu.
ē- ēvu. ēvutal= celuttutal, tūṇṭutal, tūṇṭikkaṭṭaḷi yiṭutal. 
ē- ēvu - ēku. ēkutal = pōtal
o.nō: pō - pōvu - pōku.
ē-yā-yāttiri = celavu. takaṭūr yāttiri = takaṭūrc celavu. o.nō: mā- māttiri. māttal = aḷattal. ituvum oru vaḻakkaṟṟa viṉi.
ēkāram yāvākat tiritali, ēṉ - yāṉ, ē-yā, ētu-yātu, ēṉi - yāṉi, ēmi - yāmi, ēmam - yāmam - cāmam mutaliya coṟṟiripuka ḷāṉ aṟika.
yā-jā (marāṭṭi, kucarātti, inti, vaṅkam.)
yāttiri -yātrā- jātrā
marāṭṭiyum kucarāttiyum paḻam pañca tiraviṭam; intiyum vaṅkamum vaṭatiraviṭamākiya muṉ vaṭamoḻi (pirākirutam).
ya - ja, pōlit tiripu. e-ṭu: yōki (va.) -n#h» (ï.), yuvaṉ (va.) - #th‹ (ï.), yuvaṉam (va.) - juvenis L.
jā - ga#. O.E, OS. ga#n, OHG. ga#n,ge#n, E.go, Skt. ga# (to go), ga#tu (going).
cakaram kakaramākat tiritalāl, jakaram kakarattiṉ eṭuppoliyāka pa eṉṟu tiriyum.
vaṭa tiraviṭa vaḻippaṭṭa intiyilēyē, pōtalik kuṟikkum viṉic colliṉ jakaram, iṟanta kālattiṟ kakara eṭuppoli (pa)āki viṭukiṉṟatu.
e-ṭu:
jā = pō, jāttā hi = pōkiṉṟāṉ,
jāvēkā (ja#ve#ga#) = pōvāṉ.
kayā (gaya#) = pōṉāṉ.
vaṭamoḻi eṉṉum camaṟ kirutattil, ga eṉṉum mutaṉili ga eṉṟu kuṟukiyum gam eṉṟu tirintum, gati,gama, gamana eṉṉum viṉip peyarkaḷip piṟappikkum.
kam (gam) eṉṉum mutaṉili āeṉṉum etirmaṟi muṉṉoṭṭoṭu cērntu, celkikku etirāṉa varukiyik kuṟikkum ākama (aÝgama) eṉṉum viṉip peyariyum viṉiyāku peyariyum tōṟṟuvikkum. ākama eṉṉum vaṭacol, tamiḻil ākamam eṉṟu makaramey yīṟu koṭuttuk kuṟikkappaṭum.
ā eṉṉum etirmaṟi muṉṉoṭṭu, akara etirmaṟi muṉṉoṭṭiṉ nīṭṭamē.
al - a - ā. o.nō  nal - na.
iṅṅaṉam, ākāra etirmaṟi muṉṉoṭṭuc cērkkiyāl etirviṉic coṟkaḷip paṭippatu, camaṟkiruta iyalpē.
kam = pō.  ākam = vā
tā = koṭu ātā = koḷ, vāṅku
camaṟkirutam, taṉṉil aintiliru pakutiyi muḻuc coṟ kaṭaṉākavum, vēṟōr aintiliru pakutiyi vērccoṟ kaṭaṉākavum, tamiḻiṉiṉṟu koṇ ṭuḷḷatu.
tiru nāṉmaṟi viḷakka ārāyccikku matippuri vaḻaṅkiya varuḷ oruvarāṉa, tañcic cīṉivācap piḷḷi, 
srīmāṉ kā. cuppiramaṇiya piḷḷi yavarkaḷ, nalla kulattil piṟantavarā yiruntum inta nāṉmaṟi viḷakkam eḻutiyiruppatu, kaliyukak kūttukkaḷil oṉṟeṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu.  acañcala civa paktiyil ciṟanta kuṭumpattil tōṉṟiya srīmāṉ cāmpacivam piḷḷi yavarkaḷ, mēlē kaṇṭa ārāycci eṉṉum nūli eḻutiyiruppatu, civarkaḷ aṉivarukkum cantōṣattik koṭukkak kūṭiyatu. eṉṟu varintiruppatu, āriyarkku aṭimiyarā yiruppavarē nalla kulattāreṉṟum nalla civaṉiya reṉṟum poruḷpaṭuvatā yuḷḷatu.  iti avartam tamiḻ varalāṟu maṟu patippil veḷiyiṭātu pōyiṉar.
ittakiya nalla kulattārum nalla civaṉiyarum iruntataṉāl tāṉ, ciṉṉamaṉūrt tēvāra ūrvalam pirāmaṇat teruvil taṭukkap paṭṭatum, atu paṟṟit toṭarnta vaḻakkil pirāmaṇa muṟiyāḷi pirāmaṇac cārpākat tīrppuk kūṟiyatum, ākum.
makēntiram eṉṉum muṭiyik koṇṭa kumarimalit toṭariṉ vaṭapakuti mūḻkiya piṉṉarē, kañcam māvaṭṭattiṟ civaṉ kōvil koṇṭuḷḷa mali makēntiram eṉṟu peyar peṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum.  mantira māmali makēntira veṟpaṉ eṉṉum tiruvācakat toṭaril (2:100) māṇikkavācakar kuṟittuḷḷa mali, kañcam māvaṭṭa maliyē yeṉṟu, ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakat tamiḻ akaramutali taṉ iyalpiṟkēṟpak kuṟittuḷḷatu.
tiruppaṇikaḷ
immi maṟumi vīṭeṉṉum mummiyiṉpattiṟkum iṟivaḻipāṭu iṉṟiya miyātatākak karutappaṭṭamiyiṉ, paṇṭit tamiḻakattil iṅkilāntiṟpōl matattuṟiyum ōr araciyal tiṇikkaḷamāka iruntu vantatu. piṟa matak kōyilkaṭkum iṭiyiṭi māṉiyam viṭappaṭṭatēṉum, civaviṇavak kōyilkaḷē perumpālum aracariṉ āṭcikkuṭpaṭṭiruntaṉa.  pēraracarellārum oṉṟum palavumākap peruṅkōyilkaḷ kaṭṭi vittaṉar. ki.pi. 5 ām nūṟṟāṇṭi lirunta kōcceṅkaṭ cōḻaṉ eḻupatu civaṉkōyilkaḷ kaṭṭiṉāṉ.  ki.pi.10 ām 11m nūṟṟāṇṭilirunta mutalām irācarācaṉ tañcip peruvuṭi yār kōyilik kaṭṭi viliyēṟappeṟṟa aṇikalaṅkaḷ ūrtikaḷ taṭṭumuṭṭuk kaḷuṭaṉ, 35 ūrkaḷit tēvatāṉamāka viṭṭāṉ. kōyil kaṭṭāta aracar iṟiyili yum aṇikalaṅkaḷum piṟavum aḷittaṉar.
kōyiṟku, kōṭṭam, kuṭiki, tēvakulam, taḷi, nakaram, paḷḷi, mantiram, māḷiki, ampalam mutaliya pala peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉa.  kōyilkaḷ ciṟukōyil peruṅkōyil eṉa irutiṟattaṉa.  ciṟukōyilkaḷi ūraviyārum peruṅ kōyilkaḷit taṉik kuḻuvārum kavaṉittu vantaṉar. peruṅkōyili mēṟpārp pavarkkuk kōyil vāriyattār eṉṟum srīkāriyam pārppār eṉṟum, avaruḷ talivaṉukku srīkāriyak kaṇkāṇi nāyakam eṉṟum, peyar. civiṉkōyili mēṟpārppavar makēcuvarar eṉṟum, tirumāl kōyili mēṟpārppavar makā viṇavar allatu tāṉattār eṉṟum, potuvākak kūṟappeṟuvar.kōyiṟ kāriyaṅkaḷi naṭattivippataṟku āḷvāṉ eṉṟōr araciyal viṉiñaṉum iruntāṉ.
kōyiṟ pūcārikkut tiruvaṭi piṭippāṉ eṉṟum, pōṟṟi eṉṟum, nampiyāṉ eṉṟum, avaṉ tuṇivaṉukku eṭuttuk kinīṭṭi eṉṟum, peyaruṇṭu. uvaccaṉ (occaṉ) eṉpatu kāḷikōyiṟ pūcāriyiṉ peyar.  kurukkaḷ paṇṭāram pulavaṉ eṉṟum pūcārikkup peyaruṇṭu. ivar aṇukkat toṇṭarāvar.  ivarallātu tirukkikkōṭṭi yōtuvār, tēvaraṭiyār (patiyilār, cākkiyaṉ, paṇṭāri, aṟikāraṉ, tēvakaṉmikaḷ, meḻukkaṭikaḷ, tiruviḷakkiṭuvār, tirumeyk kāppu (tirumeyk kāppōṉ, tirumēṉi kāval) mutaliya ettuṇiyō viṉiñar kōyilic cērntiruntaṉar. perunāṭu muḻutumirunta tirukkik kōṭṭi yōtuvārkkut tēvāranāyakam eṉṟoru talivaṉi runtatākat terikiṉṟatu.
iṟiyili(tēvatāṉam,) naṉkoṭi, nērttikkaṭaṉ, kāṇikki, vari, taṇṭam, paṟimutal ākiya eḻuvakiyil kōyiṟku varumāṉam vantu koṇṭiruntatu.
kōyiṟ celaviṟkellām potuppaṭa viṭappaṭṭirunta iṟiyiliyuṭaṉ, vevvēṟu celaviṟkut taṉittaṉi avvap pōtu aracarālum perumakkaḷālum viṭappaṭṭu vanta iṟiyilikaḷum uḷa. avi tiruviḻāppuṟam, kaṟṟaḷippuṟam, nanta vaṉappuṟam, aṭukkaḷippuṟam, avalamutuppuṟam, akkāravaṭici lamutuppuṟam, meḻukkaṭippuṟam, tiruvaṉantaṟkaṭṭaḷi mutaliyaṉa. kōyiṟ paṇiyāḷarkkup pakirappaṭum tiruccōṟṟiṟ kāka viṭappaṭum kaṭṭaḷi aṭṭiṟpēṟu eṉappaṭṭatu.  oruvar ciṟitukālam nukarntapiṉ kōyiṟku viṭumāṟu aḷikkappaṭum nilattiṟku iṟikkaṭṭaḷi eṉṟu peyar.
kōyiyil viḷakkēṟṟutaṟkum piṟa tiruppaṇikaṭkum pārppaṉari uṇpippataṟkum, vēṟu vēṟāka, aracarālum perumakkaḷālum potumakkaḷālum kōyilil nitikaḷum tokikaḷum oppaṭikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa.  avaṟṟiṉ vaṭṭiyikkoṇṭē appaṇikaḷ ceyyappaṭṭu vantamiyiṉ, ammutalkaḷ aḻiyātiruntaṉa.  vaṭṭi polici eṉappaṭṭatu.  tām kuṟitta tiruppaṇikaḷum aṟaṅkaḷum nāḷtōṟum allatu niliyāka naṭipeṟaṟ poruṭṭu, avaṟṟip policiyik koṇṭē naṭatta vēṇṭumeṉṟu, koṭiyāḷarē kuṟittuviṭuvatu marapu.
paṟimutalāṉa kuṟṟavāḷikaḷiṉ cottukkaḷ kōyiṟku viṟkappaṭṭu, araciṟi niluvi pōka eñciyatellām, kōyilōṭu cērkkappaṭṭatu.
kōyil varumāṉattil celavupōka eccamellām aṟamuṅ kalviyumākiya potunalap paṇikkuc celaviṭappaṭṭatu.  akkālattuk kōyilkaḷ piṉ varumāṟu pala tuṟiyil potunalat toṇṭāṟṟi vantaṉa.
(1) toḻiṟ ṟuṟi: kōyil nilaṅkaḷ ēḻiyuḻavariṭattum, kōyiṟ kaṉṟukālikaḷ ēḻiyiṭiyariṭattum, vārac cākupaṭikkum vaḷarppiṟkum viṭap paṭṭaṉa.
cuntarapāṇṭiyaṉ kālattil, cūralūriyaṭuttirunta kōyiṟ kuḷamum vāykkālum aṇiyum, piḷḷiyāṉ eṉṉumcem paṭavaṉ pārviyilum, avaṟṟiṉ paḻutupārppu cūralūr veṭṭiyāḷiṭattum, viṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa.  piḷḷi yāṉukkuk kōyiṟkuṭikaḷ celuttum vāykkālpāṭṭamum pācip pāṭṭamum, veṭṭiyāḷukkuc campaḷamum umpaḷamum, kimmāṟāka aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa. P.K., pp. 222-223.
kaṇkāṇippu, vaḻipāṭu, maṭittoḻil, tuppuravākkam, alaṅkarippu, kaṇakku, kāval mutaliya paltuṟi paṟṟiya aluvalāḷarōṭu; icivāṇarum kūttarum kammiyarum paṇimakkaḷum kuṭimakkaḷumāka; ettuṇiyō pēr kōyilil niliyāka amarttappeṟṟut takka campaḷam peṟṟu vantaṉar.
tañcip peruvuṭiyār kōyilil, tēvāra ōtuvār nāṟpat teṇmarum patiyilār nāṉūṟṟuvarum, icikkaruvi yiyakkuvār paṟpalarum, cākkiyarum, kaṇiyarum, aivakik kollarum, kuyavarum, tiyaṟkārarum, vaṇṇārum, muṭiyalaṅ kāra viṉiñarum, uṭpaṭa; ēṟattāḻa āyiravar niliyāṉa viṉiñarāka amarttap peṟṟiruntatākat terikiṉṟatu. pañcakālattum piṟakālattum, uṇaviṟku vaḻiyaṟṟa āṭavarum peṇṭirum kōyiṟkaṭimi pukkuk kōyiṟpaṇiyāṟ piḻittu vantaṉar.  avarukku maṇakkavum kuṭumpavāḻkki naṭattavum urimiyiruntatu.
(2) paṇattuṟi: paṇam vēṇṭiyavarkku, vaṭṭik kīṭākak kōyilil kuṟippiṭṭa viḷakēṟṟumāṟu, kōyiṟ paṇṭārat tiṉiṉṟu kaṭaṉ koṭukkappaṭṭatu.
varakuṇapāṇṭiyaṉāl, tiruccentiṟ kōyil nitta vaḻipāṭṭiṟ kāka 1400 poṟkācu paṉṉīrūrāriṭip pakirntu koṭukkap paṭṭatu.  vaṭṭi āṇṭiṟku oru kāciṟku irukalam nel eṉṟum, vaṭṭiyikkoṇṭu vaḻipāṭṭi naṭappikka vēṇṭumeṉṟum, vaṭṭi niluviyāyiṉ iraṭṭiyum 25 kācu taṇṭamum iṟukka vēṇṭumeṉṟum, vitikkappaṭṭatu. P.K. pp 90-91.
aṟattiṟkum kaṭṭaḷikkum kōyiṟkuk koṭukkappaṭum paṇat tokikaḷ, kōyilatikārikaḷiṭamāvatu, ūraviyāriṭamāvatu, nakarattāriṭamāvatu, taṉip paṭṭavariṭamāvatu oppaṭikkap paṭuvatuṇṭu.
(3) kalvittuṟi:  eṇṇāyiram eṉṟa iṭattil oru maṟi nūl (vēta) viṭutik kallūriyum, tirumukkūṭal eṉṉum iṭattil oru vaṭamoḻi viṭutik kallūriyum; tiruvoṟṟiyūril viyākaraṇa tāṉa maṇṭapam eṉṉum vaṭamoḻip pāṇiṉiyilakkaṇak kallūriyum, kōyilōṭu cērkkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. eṇṇā yirattil 300 māṇavarum 10 āciriyarum iruntaṉar.  cila iṭaṅkaḷil vāṉa nūlum 
takukkamum pirapākaram eṉṉum mīmāñci nūlum vaṭamoḻiyiṟ kaṟpikkappaṭṭaṉa. māṇavarkkuk kalviyum ūṇuṭi yuṟiyuḷum ilavacamāy aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa.  ataṟkup pōtiya iṟiyilikaḷ viṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa.
pala iṭaṅkaḷil itikāca purāṇaṅkaḷ potu makkaṭkut tamiḻil viritturikkappaṭṭaṉa. ataṟkum iṟiyili viṭap paṭṭiruntatu.
(4) kalittuṟi: iṟi kūttu ōviyam ciṟpam vaṇṇam mutaliya pala kalikaḷ, kōyil vāyilāy vaḷarkkappeṟṟaṉa.
(5) varalāṟṟut tuṟi: kaṭveṭṭukkaḷ, kōyil, maṇṭapam, pāṟi, veṟṟittūṇ, malikkuki, paṭimi, naṭukal mutaliya palaviṭaṅkaḷil veṭṭap paṭṭaṉavēṉum,  perumpāṉmiyākak kōyilkaḷilēyē avi iṭam peṟṟaṉa. 
ki.pi. 6 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkup piṟpaṭṭa tamiḻaracar varalāṟṟi aṟitaṟku uṟutu ṇiyāṉavi kalveṭṭukkaḷē. aracaruṭiya pōrc ceyalkaḷiyum veṟṟiyi yuṅ kūṟum meykkīrtti kaḷum, ūraviyārukkum nāṭṭatikārikaṭkum piṟarkkum viṭukkappaṭṭa aracavāṇikaḷum, ūraviyārāl aṉṟaṉṟu ceyyappaṭṭa putut tīrmāṉaṅkaḷum, aracarālum atikāri kaḷālum perumakkaḷālum koṭukkappaṭṭa tāṉap paṭṭayaṅkaḷum, kallil veṭṭappaṭṭuk kōyilil ellāruṅ kāṇu miṭattiṟ patikkappeṟuvatu, tavirā vaḻakkamāyiruntatu.
oru citinta kōyilip putuppikkumpōtu, ūraviyār nāṭṭatikāri vāyilāy aracaṉukkaṟivittu avaṉatu icivu peṟṟapiṉ, kōyililuḷḷa kal veṭṭukkaḷi icciri vimāṉamiṭattuk kaṭṭuṅkāl kaṇṭa cilā lēkippaṭi eṉṟu toṭaṅki ōlippaṭi yeṭuttu, putuk kalveṭṭup poṟittu, kōyil kaṭṭi muṭintapiṉ, apputuk kalveṭṭukkaḷi atikārikaḷ kāṭṭumiṭattil patippatu vaḻakkam.  kalveṭṭup paṭiyeṭuppu kaṟpaṭimāṟṟu eṉappaṭṭatu. citintupōṉa kalveṭṭukkaḷip putuppikkumpōtum immuṟiyē kiyāḷappeṟum.
(6) aṟattuṟi: pārppaṉari yuṇpikkum akkiracāliyum, aṭiyāri yuṇpikkum maṭamum, irappōriyuṇpikkum aṭiciṟcāliyum,ākiya mūṉṟaṉuḷ oṉṟō palavō, palavūrkaḷiṟ kōyilōṭiṇikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa.  perumpaṟṟap puliyūril irunta aṟaccāli aṟapperuñ celvi eṉṟum, tirumukkūṭalil cāttirar eṉṉum pārppaṉa vaṭamoḻi māṇavarkku maruttakamākavum maruttuvak kallūriyākavumirunta āyurvēta maruttuvacāli (ātula cāli) vīracōḻaṉ eṉṟum peyar peṟṟiruntaṉa.
(7) araciyaṟ ṟuṟi: araciṟi yiṟukkāta nilaṅkaḷum ūrkaḷum kōyiṟku viṟkappaṭṭu, avvilit tokiyiṉiṉṟu avaṟṟiṟku araciṟi yiṟukkappaṭṭatu.
ūr maṇṭapamillāta vūrkaḷilellām, araciyaṟ kūṭṭaṅkaḷ kōyil maṇṭapattilēyē kūṭṭappaṭṭaṉa.
cila aracar kōyiṟpaṇiyōṭu tiru nūṟpaṇiyum ceytu vantaṉar.  mutalām irācarācaṉ tirumuṟi kaṇṭatum, mūṉṟām kulōttuṅkaṉ periya purāṇam iyaṟṟuvittatum, nūṟ paṇiyām.
kōyiṟ kaṇakkukaḷ āṇṭutōṟum nāṭṭatikārikaḷāl taṇikki ceyyappaṭṭaṉa.  kuṟṟavāḷikaḷ taṇṭikkap paṭṭaṉar.  (ta.ma.)
tiruppukaḻ:
patiṉintām nūṟṟāṇṭil vēṟu emmoḻiyilum eṉṟum evarālum pāṭa iyalāta nūṟṟuk kaṇakkāṉa vaṇṇakkuḻippu kaḷil pāṭappaṭṭa oppuyarvaṟṟa tamiḻppaṉuval aruṇakiri nātariṉ tiruppukaḻt tiraṭṭē. atu tēvāram pōṉṟu icittamiḻ ilakkiya mākavum uḷḷatu.  (ta.i.va.46)

tirum. - E. turn, move round from a centre or axis, change from one side to another, take new direction, more to other side of, cuse to go, change in nature, translate.
OE. tyrnan, turnian, OF. turner, torner. f L. tornare f. Gk. tornos
E. attorney, n. ME. f. OF. atorne p.p. of atorner (a) to, torner turn.)
E. return, ME. retorner, returner f. Rom. re (tornare, turn).
m -ṉ, pōli. x.neh.: kaṭam - kaṭaṉ. irum (irumpu) -iron.
E. tornado, n. Violent storm of small extent with whirling winds Sp. tornada, thunder, storm, tornar, to turn.
E. tourney, ME f. OF. tornei, torneier f. Rom. tornidiare f. L. tornus, a turn.
E. tournament, ME. f. OF. torneiement, torneier, tourney.
E. tourniquet. n. Bandage etc.  for stopping flow of blood through artery by compression effected with screw of twisted bar,(?)
âU¥ò - âU¥ã -E. trope, n. figurative use of a word. L.F. Gk. tropos, turn, way, trope, trepo, turn.
E.  trophy, 1. Arms etc. of vanquished enemy set up on field of battle or elsewhere to commemorate victory. F. trophee f. L. trop(h)aeum f. Gk. tropion (trope, rout f. trepo, turn)
veṟṟiyaracaṉ tōṟṟa aracaṉip pōrkkaḷattiṉiṉṟu tiruppiyaṭittal allatu turattutal eṉṉum aṭippaṭik karuttuk koṇṭatē trophy eṉṉuñ col.  itē karutti viḷiyāṭṭup pōrukkum ēṟṟik kūṟuvaṉavē tourney tournament eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷum eṉa aṟika.
E. Trophy. 2. Thing made or kept or awarded as memorial of victory in tournament.
E. tropic, n. “Parallel of iatitude 23 27’ north (Tropic of Cancer) or south (Tropic of Capricorn) of the equator (the Tropics, region between these);; each of the two corresponding circles on celestial sphere where sun appears to turn after reaching greatest destination.” M.E. f. L. f. Gk. tropikos (trope, turning f. trepo, turn.)
E. tropical, a. OF, peculiar to, suggestive of the tropics.
E. tropism, n. (Biol,) Turning of (part of) organism in particular direction in response to external stimulus. (f. second element of heliotropism)
E. heliotrope n. Herb or shrub of genus Heliotropium with fragrant purple flowers;; f. L.f. Gk. heliotropion, plant turning flowers to the sun; helios, sun, tropos f. trepo, turn.
o.nō : ñāyiṟu tirumpi = cūriyakānti.
E. tropology, n. Figurative use of words; figurative interpretation esp. of the Scriptures; f. LL. f. Gk. tropologia, tropos f. trepo turn, logy, suffix forming nouns denoting sciences.
aṇiyiyalil, coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷkaḷ palvēṟu vakiyil tirikkavum tiruppavum paṭutal kāṇka.
E. tropopuse n. Interface between troposphere and stratosphere.
E. troposphere, n. Layer of atmospheric air extending about seven miles upwards from earth’s surface, in which temperature falls with increasing height.  Gk. tropos,turning + sphere.
E. strophanthin. n. White crystaline poisonous glucoside extracted from various species of the genus Strophanthus of tropical plants and used as a heart - tonic; f. mod. L. strophanthus f. Gk. strophos, twisted cord + anthos, flower.
E. strophe, n. (Lines recited during) turn made in dancing by ancient Greek chorus; strophe, lit. turning, strepho, turn.
E. antistrophe, f. Gk. antistropho, turn aginst.
E. apostrophe, n.f. Gk. apo, away from, strepho, to turn away.
E catastrophe, n. Sudden or widespread or noteworthy disaster; even subverting system of things; disastrous end, ruin; denouement of drama; f. L. catastropha f. Gk. cata, down, strophe, turning, strepho, turn. para tort (twist), tortus (crooked), tortum (wrong)/ tortor (twister) mutaliya ilattīṉac coṟkaḷum, ilattīṉattiṉiṉṟu koṇṭa kaṭaṉ collākak kūṟappaṭum vaṭicava  eṉṉum āṅkilac collum, atiṉiṉṟu tirinta tortion, tortuous, torture, tortrix mutaliya taṉic coṟkaḷum, torticollis eṉṉum kūṭṭuc collum, contort, distort, extort, retort mutaliya muṉṉoṭṭup peṟṟa tiricoṟkaḷum, tirittal allatu tirukaṟ poruḷiyē aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭuḷḷamiyiṉ, tiri eṉṉum vērc colliṉiṉṟu tirintaṉavākak koḷḷat takkaṉavē. tirumaṇam
āyiraṅ kālattup payir ākiyālum, vāḻkkit tuṇivar iruvarum teyvattiṉ muṉ allatu teyvattiṉpēril palaraṟiya āṇiyiṭṭuk kūṭutalālum maṇam teyvat taṉmi peṟṟut tirumaṇam eṉappeṟṟatu. ippeyar piṉṉar tirumaṇat toṭarpāṉa viḻāviyuṅ kuṟittatu.  (ta.ti.muṉ ē)
tirumaṇa vaki:
karaṇac caṭaṅku avaravar poruḷāṭci nilimikkēṟpa viḻāvoṭu kūṭiyatum kūṭātatumāka irukkum. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉatu koṭṭuttirumaṇam eṉṟum piṉṉatu kaṭṭuttāli eṉṟum collap peṟum.  (ti.ma. ati.116).
tirumāliṉ āmit tiruviṟakkam
pāṇṭi nāṭṭic cūḻnta kaṭalkaḷil, mikap periya āmikaḷ vāḻkkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ ōṭukaḷ vīṭukaḷukku mukaṭu pōṭa utavukiṉṟaṉa.  ōr oṭṭiṉ nīḷam 15 muḻam. ataṉaṭiyil palar niṉṟu veyilukkut tappamuṭiyum eṉṟu mekataṉi kūṟakiṟār. Foreign Notices of South India. p. 42 itu muṉṉōr kūṟṟik koṇṭu kūṟiyatē.
teṉkaṭalil nilanaṭukkattāl tattaḷitta oru malit tīviyē, āmiyuṭaṉ iṇittu, tiruppāṟkaṭal kaṭinta katiyik kaṭṭiyiruppatākat terikiṉṟatu.  irāmāyaṇattil mukkiyamāṉa pakuti teṉṉāṭṭuc ceytiyē.
ciṟanta allatu periya aracarit teyvamākki vaṇaṅkuvatu paṇṭi vaḻakkam.  tirumali nāykkar iṟantapiṉpu  avarkkuk kōyil eṭuttu vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. Historyof the Navaks of Madura. p.146. tirumālippōla aracarum kāpput toḻili yuṭimiyiṉ, avarit tirumālākak kūṟuvatu tolkāppiyar kālattilēyē vaḻakkam eṉpati, pūvi nili eṉpatālaṟi yalām. muccakkaramum eṉṉum veṇpā karikālaṉit tirumālākak kūṟuvatiyum, aracar nāṭum veṟṟiyum aṭi vati nilamakaḷiyum vayamakaḷiyum maṇappatāka meykkīrtti mālikaḷ kūṟuvatiyum, tiruvuṭi yaracarik kāṇiṉ tirumālik kaṇṭēṉ eṉṉum tivviyap paṉuvaṟ kūṟṟiyum nōkkuka.
irāmaṉ tavañ ceytukoṇṭirunta oru cūttiraṉik koṉṟum, kaṇṇaṉ nālvakik kulamum piramāviṉ paṭippēyeṉṟu kūṟiyum, pirāmaṇīyatti vaḷarttatiṉāl mikap pōṟṟappaṭṭatākat terikiṟatu. ikkālattil varaṇācira mattit tāṅkum tiruvāḷar kāntiyi, makātmāveṉṟum muṉivar eṉṟuṅ kūṟutal kāṇka.
kantapurāṇattil, kantaṉ piṟapput tavira, maṟṟiyavellām (perumpālum) teṉṉāṭṭuc ceytikaḷē.
pañcantirak katikaḷiṟ pala teṉṉāṭṭil vaḻaṅkiyavi.
hālāya māṉmiyam, upamaṉyu pakta vilācam mutaliyaviyum pala talap paḻimikaḷum teṉṉāṭṭuc ceytikaḷik kūṟupavi.
aṭimuṭi tēṭiya kati tiruvaṇṇāmaliyil tōṉṟiyatu.
muppuram eritta kati vaṭavilaṅkiyil tōṉṟiyatu. māntiyi lirunta mukkōṭṭikaḷ nila naṭukkattālō erimaliyālō paṉmuṟi aḻintupōyiṉa.  oraḻivu puttamatam ilaṅkiyiṟ  pukuntapiṉ nikaḻntatu.  atuvari civamē aṅku vaḻaṅkivantatu.  ataṉāl tirumāl puttavaṭivukoṇṭu tārakāṭcaṉ mutaliya mūvarip puttarākki, piṉpu civaperumāṉāl avarkku aḻivu nērvittatākak katikaṭṭappaṭṭatu.  (katirimalip paḷḷik kāṇka.)
jaṉamējayaṉ kati micūr nāṭṭil tōṉṟiyatu. Illustrated Weekly of India Oct. 33, 1938. p.22
paṇṭikkālattil, tirumāliyarkkut tiruvaraṅkamum civarkkut tilliyumē ciṟanta tiruttalaṅkaḷāka viruntaṉa.
ataṉāl avaṟṟiṟkuk kōyil eṉṟē peyar.
kaṅkināṭṭilirunta pārppaṉar teṉṉāṭṭiṟkuk kumariyāṭa vantaṉar.
irāvaṇaṉ peyarkka muyaṉṟatākak kūṟum kayili yāḻppāṇatti luḷḷatē.
patiṉeṇkaṇattāruḷ, perumpālār teṉṉāṭṭiṟkē yuriyavar.
cōḻamarapiṉ muṉṉōruḷ oruvaṉāṉa cipi ayōttiyil āṇṭatākak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟāṉ.  itaṉāl, vaṭanāṭṭuc cūriya tiṅkaḷ marapiṉariṉ muṉṉōr teṉṉāṭṭārē eṉṟu terikiṉṟatu.
tiruvaḷḷuvar em matattār?
āriyat teyvamākiya nāṉmukaṉi yuṭkoṇṭa muttirumēṉik koḷkiyit taḻuvāmiyiṉ, tiruvaḷḷuvar vitikarallar. 
kaṭavuḷilli yeṉṟum, pūtaṅkaḷ nāṉkēyeṉṟum, oruvar koṉṟa vuyiriyiṉ ūṉi iṉṉoruvar uṇṇiṉ karicaṉ ṟeṉṟum, kūṟum pavuttak koḷkikaḷi oppukkoḷḷā miyāl tiruvaḷḷuvar pavuttarallar.
ulakiṟku oru mutalvaṉ vēṇṭuvatilliyeṉṟum, illaṟat tiṉum tuṟavaṟam ciṟantateṉṟum, makaḷirkku vīṭupēṟilli yeṉṟum, meyyuṇarviṟku iṉṟiyamiyāta cukkilat tiyāṉam eṉṉum paramavūḻkam ikkālattil nikaḻvatilliyeṉṟum, kūṟum camaṇak koḷkikaḷi oppuk koḷḷāmiyāl tiruvaḷḷuvar ciṉarallar.
kaṭavuḷilliyeṉṟum, meypporuḷ irupattintē yeṉṟum, kūṟum cāṅkiyak koḷkikaḷi oppukkoḷḷā miyāl tiruvaḷḷuvar cāṅkiyarallar.
kaṭavuḷ tōṟṟaravu koḷvāreṉpatiyum uruva vaḻipāṭṭiyum oppuk koḷḷāmiyāl, tiruvaḷḷuvar māliyar (viṇavar) allar.
meypporuḷ muppatāṟu eṉpatiyum uruva vaḻipāṭṭiyum āriyac cārpiyum oppukkoḷḷāmiyāl, tiruvaḷḷuvar civaṉiyar (civar) allar.
ōtu meḻuttō ṭuyirkkali mūvañcu
māti yeḻuttavi yimpattō ṭoṉṟeṉpar.  (tiruman.963)
eṉṉum oṉṟē civaṉiya āriyac cārpik kāṭṭap pōtiya cāṉṟām.
iṟṟic civaṉiyam māliyattiṉum āriyam mikuntuḷḷami.
iṉi, tiruvaḷḷuvar emmatattārtāṉeṉiṉ, tāyumāṉavar aṅkiṅ keṉātapaṭi yeṉṉum kaṭavuḷ vaṇakkac ceyyuḷiṟ kūṟiyuḷḷavāṟu, kaṭavuḷ matattiṉar eṉṟu kūṟiviṭukka.  (ti.ta.ma)
tiruvaḷḷuvar kālak kumukāya (camutāya) nili
tiruvaḷḷuvar kālattil āriyam vērūṉṟiviṭṭatu.niṟam  paṟṟiya nālvaki varaṇappākupāṭum pukuttappaṭṭuviṭṭatu.  tamiḻa rellārum pirāmaṇarukkut tāḻntavarāyiṉar. pārppāreṉṟu collappaṭṭilvāḻkkip pirāmaṇarum tammi antaṇa reṉṟu kūṟikkoṇṭaṉar. toḻilpaṟṟi yēṟpaṭṭa kulaṅkaḷum piṟappup paṟṟip peyarpeṟalāyiṉa.  pulavaruḷḷum pirāmaṇar uyarntavar eṉṉuṅ karutteḻuntatu, aṟaṅkaḷellām potuvākap pirāmaṇarkkē ceyyap paṭṭaṉa. tamiḻarukku uṟavukkalappum oṟṟumiyum varavarak kuṟintuvantaṉa.  mūvēntarum maṉṉar cilarum āriyaccārpā yiruntatiṉāl, pirāmaṇiyattik kaṇṭikka evarukkum vāyillāmaṟ pōyiṟṟu. ataṉāṟ pulavarum aṭimiyarāyiṉar.  pirāmaṇar teyvap piṟappiṉar eṉṉuṅ karuttu tamiḻarukkuḷ vaḷarntu vantatu.  ataṉāl vaṭacoṟkaḷ tamiḻil tārāḷamākak kalakkavum tamiḻccoṟkaḷ maṟiyavum putut tamiḻc coṟkaḷ tōṉṟātirukkavum, vaḻivakukkappaṭṭuviṭṭatu.  (ti.ta.ma.)
tiruvaḷḷuvar kālam
mutaliru kaḻaka ilakkiyamum aḻikkappaṭṭapiṉ, tamiḻ nākarikattiyum paṇpāṭṭiyum tāṅkikkoṇṭirukkum iruperu nūlkaḷuḷ oṉṟāṉa tirukkuṟaḷiṉ kālam, ati iyaṟṟiya tiruvaḷḷuvariṉ kālam.  iṉṉa nūṟṟāṇṭil iṉṉa vāṇṭiliruntu iṉṉa vāṇṭu vari eṉṟu tiṭṭavaṭṭamāyk kūṟutaṟ kiyalāviṭiṉum, tolkāppiyar kālamākiya ki.mu. ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkum kaṭikkaḻaka muṭivākiya ki.pi. 3ām nūṟṟāṇ ṭiṟkum iṭippaṭṭatāyk kiṟittuviṟku muntiyateṉṟu koḷvatu, perumpālum kuṟṟattiṟ kiṭamillātatum ēṟattāḻa uṇmiyi oṭṭiyatumākum.

6. 	malarmici yēkiṉāṉ (pūmēl naṭantāṉ), poṟivāyi lintavittāṉ mutaliya arukaṉ peyarkaḷ, kaṭavuṭ peyar kaḷākat tirukkuṟaḷ mutalatikārattil āḷappaṭṭuḷḷami.
putta matattiṉ piṉṉarē ārukatam tamiḻakam vantatākat terikiṉṟatu.
tiruvaḷḷuvar kālam kaṭikkaḻaka muṭiviṟku muntiyateṉ pataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ:
