iccoṟkaḷellām tolkāppiyar kālattiṟkup piṉ ṟōṉṟiyavi eṉpar cilar. avar tamiḻiyalpiyum tamiḻ varalāṟṟiyum aṟiyār. tol kāppiyarkkup piṉ tamiḻ taḷarntatē yaṉṟi vaḷarnta tiṉṟu.
cakkaṇi (kūttu vaki), caṭaṅkaṭi (vañcaṉi),caṭari (citivu), caṭali (vīṇceyal), cakkattu (muttu vaki), cakkaṭṭam (cakkantam), caṭṭaṟam (aṟavuri), cappaṭi (vayirakkuṟṟam), carāy (kāṟ caṭṭi), callaṭam (arikkāṟ caṭṭi), calla vaṭṭam (kēṭakavaki), camaṉi (ōkavaki), cavatari (tēṭu) eṉpaṉavum, ivi pōṉṟa piṟavumē, tolkāppiyarukkup piṉ tōṉṟi yirukkalām.
tolkāppiyar ulakavaḻakkic cevviyāka ārāyavilli eṉpataṟkup pala cāṉṟukaḷ uḷa.
1.	māṟṟaruñ ciṟappiṉ marapiyal kiḷappiṉ
		pārppum paṟaḻum kuṭṭiyum kuruḷiyum
		kaṉṟum piḷḷiyum makavum maṟiyumeṉ(ṟu)
		oṉpatum kuḻaviyō ṭiḷamip peyarē  (mara.ka)
	eṉṟatō ṭamiyātu,
		colliya marapiṉ iḷami tāṉē
		colluṅ kāli aviyala tilavē.  (. 29)
	eṉṟu variyaṟiyum iṭṭār tolkāppiyar.
kacaḷi, kayantali, karu, karuntu, kuñcu, kurumaṉ, ceḷ, cēy, nāku, putalvu (putalvaṉ, putalvi), poṭi, muṉi (yāṉik kaṉṟu) mutaliya iḷamip peyarkaḷ viṭappaṭṭup pōyiṉa. iṅṅaṉamē cila āṇpāṟ peyarkaḷum peṇpāṟ peyarkaḷum.
(2) nīṉ nīm eṉpaṉa muṉṉili yorumi paṉmip peyarkaḷ. ivaṟṟiṉ muntiṉa nili nūṉ nūm. eṉpataṉ vēṟṟumiyaṭi num. nīm eṉpataṉ kaṭikkuṟi nī, nī eṉpatu ir eṉṉum palar pālīṟṟoṭu cērntu nīyir nīvir eṉṟākum. āyiṉ, tolkāp piyarō, num eṉpati ṉiṉṟu nīyir eṉpatit tirittār.
allataṉ maruṅkiṟ colluṅ kāli
ukkeṭa niṉṟa meyvayiṉ ivara
iyyiṭi nilii īṟukeṭa rakaram
niṟṟal vēṇṭum puḷḷiyoṭu puṇarntē
appāl moḻivayiṉ iyaṟki yākum  (tol. 326)
(3) oṉpatu + pattu eṉa niṟuttit toṇṇūṟu eṉṟum, oṉpatu + nūṟu eṉa niṟuttit toḷḷāyiram eṉṟum, puṇarttār tolkāppiyar. (tol. eḻut.kuṟ.40, 58).
itu nāḷikēram eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu kēraḷam eṉṉum collit tirikkum vaṭamoḻi vaḻakkottatē.
ivaṟṟi iṭiccerukal eṉpār iṭiccerukaliṉ iyalpi aṟiyār.  ivi iṭiccerukalāyiṉ naṉṉūlār ivaṟṟip piṉpaṟṟiyirār.  ivi pōṉṟa tavaṟṟup puṇarppum pirippum iṉṉum palavuḷa. 
(4) uriyiyalil, tīrtal, tīrttal, paḻutu, muḻutu eṉṉum peru vaḻakkāṉa ulakavaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum, aruñ ceyyuṭcoṟkaḷ pōl eṭuttuk kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
(5) puṇariyal 17-ām nūṟpāviṟ cāriyi eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa vaṟṟuḷ, āṉ akku ikku eṉpaṉa cāriyi yalla.  iṟṟuc cāriyi kuṟikkappeṟavilli.  aṟṟuccāriyi vaṟṟuccāriyi eṉak kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  
piḷḷiyāṭṭu
tolkāppiyattiṟ collappaṭṭuḷḷa pala puṟattuṟikaḷ, paṇṭit tamiḻariṉ maṟatti maṭṭumaṉṟi, akkālattuk kōvaraciluḷḷa kuṭiyāṭci yurimi yiyuṅ kāṭṭuvaṉavāyuḷa.
vāṇmalin teḻuntōṉi makiḻntupaṟi tūṅka
nāṭavaṟ karuḷiya piḷḷi yāṭṭum (puṟat. 60)
eṉṉum karantittuṟiyum, ataṉ uriyil nacciṉārkkiṉiyar eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya
vaṉkaṇ maṟamaṉṉaṉ vāṇmalintu mēmpaṭṭa
puṉṟali yoḷvāṭ putalvaṟkaṇ - ṭaṉpuṟṟuk
kāṉkeḻu nāṭu koṭuttār karutārkku
vāṉkeḻu nāṭu vara.
eṉṉum veṇpāvum, pārāṭṭi makiḻattakkaṉa. itu puṟapporuḷ veṇpā māliyil muṟṟum māṟṟappaṭṭuc ciṟappiḻantuḷḷatu.
tolkāppiya nālvakuppu
nūlē karakam mukkōl maṇiyē
āyuṅ kāli antaṇark kuriya  (tol. 1570).
anta ṇāḷark kuriyavum aracark(ku)
oṉṟiya varūum poruḷumā ruḷavē  (tol. 1572).
anta ṇāḷark karacuvari viṉṟē  (tol. 1574)
paṭiyuṅ koṭiyuṅ kuṭiyum muracum
naṭinavil puraviyuṅ kaḷiṟun tērum
tārum muṭiyum nērvaṉa piṟavum
terivukoḷ ceṅkōl aracark kuriya  (tol. 1571)
vicikaṉ peṟumē vāṇika vāḻkki  (tol. 1578)
vēḷāṇ māntarkku uḻutūṇ allatu
illeṉa moḻipa piṟavaki nikaḻcci  (tol. 1581)
āriya nālvaraṇap pākupāṭu tamiḻakattil meḷḷameḷḷap pukuttappaṭṭatu. tolkāppiyar kālattil pirāmaṇarē antaṇa rāyiṉar.  avarkku, aracurimiyum koḷḷappaṭṭatu.  maṟṟa mūvakuppum muṉpōliruntaṉa. vāṇikarkku viciyar eṉṉum vaṭacol vaḻaṅkat toṭaṅkiṟṟu.
vēḷāṇ talivarāṉa vēḷirkku allatu kuṟunila maṉṉarkku vēntark kuṟṟuḻip pirivuṇṭu.  ataṉāl,
vēntuviṭu toḻiliṉ paṭiyuṅ kaṇṇiyum
vāyntaṉar eṉpa avarpeṟum poruḷē  ( 1582)
villum vēluṅ kaḻaluṅ kaṇṇiyum
tārum āramun tērum vāḷum
maṉpeṟu marapiṉ ēṉōrkku muriya  ( 1583)
eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭatu. icciṟappu potumakkaḷāṉa uḻutūṇ vēḷāḷark killi yeṉpatu,
aṉṉa rāyiṉum iḻintōrk killi  ( 1584)
eṉpataṉāṟ peṟappaṭum.
tolkāppiya araṅkēṟṟam
tolkāppiyar akattiyariṉ māṇavarallar.  māṇavarāyiṉ akattiyari allatu akattiyattit tam nūliṟ kuṟittiruppār.  akattiyar talimiyilēyē tolkāppiyamum araṅkēṟṟap paṭṭirukkum.  muntunūl kaṇṭu eṉṟataṟkēṟpa, eṉmaṉār pulavar eṉṟu palarpālilēyē muṉṉūlāciriyarit tam nūlmuḻutum kuṟittirukkiṉṟār. akattiyarkkum tolkāp piyarkkum iṭippaṭṭa kālam ēṟattāḻa ainnūṟāṇṭākum.  muṉṉavariṉ māṇavarē piṉṉavar eṉṟu koṇṭavar, akattiyarip paṟṟit tolkāppiyattil oru kuṟippumiṉmi yāl, ataṟkuṅ karaṇiyaṅ kāṭṭuvār pōl oru katiyik kaṭṭiviṭṭaṉar.
tolkāppiyar kālattil kaḻakamum ēṟpaṭavilli.  pāṇṭiyaṉ perum pālum maṇavūril vatintiruttal vēṇṭum.  aṟṟip pāṇṭiyaṉ peyar nilantaru tiruviṟ pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉṟu, paṉam pāraṉār kuṟittuḷḷār.  avaṉ iraṇṭām kaṭalkōṭkut tappiṉa iṭikkaḻakap pāṇṭiyaṉ allaṉ.  nilamillāta taṉ kuṭikaḷ cilarkku nilam otukkiyataṉāl, appeyar peṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum.
aṟaṅkari nāviṉ nāṉmaṟi muṟṟiya
ataṅkōṭ ṭācāṟ kariltapat terintu
eṉṟu ciṟappup pāyiraṅ kūṟuvatāl, araṅkēṟṟa aviyil talimi tāṅkiṉavaṉ nālvētamum valla oru pirāmaṇat tamiḻppulavaṉ eṉpatu teḷivākiṉṟatu.
tolkāppiyattiluḷḷa āriyak karuttukkaḷum oru cila vaṭa coṟkaḷum paṟṟi nām varunta vēṇṭiyatilli. atu akkālattu nilimi. āriyak karuttum collum koṇṭiruntataṉā lēyē, atu ituvari aḻivuṟāmal tappi vantuḷḷateṉṟu nām uṇarntu makiḻavēṇṭum.
kiṟittuviṟku muṟpaṭṭa kaṭikkaḻakak kālattu nūlkaḷuḷ, namakkuk kiṭittuḷḷatu tirukkuṟaḷ oṉṟē.  atuvum, āti pakavaṉ katiyālum tirivarkka vaḻinūleṉṉum icippālum tiruvaḷḷuva veṇpāmāliyālumē, tappi yiruttal vēṇṭum. tirukkuṟaṭku ainnūṟāṇṭu muṟpaṭṭatum, iyaṟṟa miḻilakkaṇam muḻuvatum kumarināṭṭu muṟippaṭi eṭuttiyam puvatumāṉa tolkāppiyam, nam muṉṉōriṉ perumiyi aṟiyavum, mīṇṭum tamiḻi ariyaṇi yēṟṟavum nāmum muṉṉēṟi nam urimiyi muṟṟum peṟavum, iṉṟu nam kiyil uḷḷateṉiṉ, itu nam naṟkālattiṟ kētuvāṉa iṟivaṉ tiruvaruḷē eṉṟu terintu koḷka.
tolkāppiyat toḻiṟkulaṅkaḷum toḻilkaḷum
akavar (va.cūtar.) - aracaṉ paḷḷiyeḻucci pāṭuvōr.
cūta rēttiya tuyileṭi niliyum  (tol. po. 91)
nāḷīṇṭiya nallakavar  (maturik. 223)
tolkāppiyattil vaṭacollē āḷappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
aṭiyōr - aṭittoḻil ceypavar, aṭimiyar.
antaṇar - tuṟaviyar
aracar - nāṭāḷvār
aṟivar - mukkāla aṟiviṉar, mutaṟkāṇiyar (Prophets)
āyar - āniri mēyppōr, āṭumāṭu mēykkum iṭiyar.
iḷiyar - vēḷikkārar
ērōr - uḻavar
aiyar - muṉivar
cevili - aracar celvar mutaliya perumakkaḷ piḷḷikaḷiṉ vaḷarpput tāy.
kūttar - kūttāṭupavar, naṭañceypavar, nāṭaka naṭikar.
tuṭiyar - uṭukki yaṭippavar
tērōr - tērp pākar
paṭiñar - paṭimaṟavar
parattiyar - vilimakaḷir, potumakaḷir.
pākar - kutirippākar (vātuvar), yāṉip pākar.
pāṅkaṉ - aracariṉ akapporuḷoḻukkat tōḻaṉ.
pāṅki - araciyariṉ akapporuḷoḻukkat tōḻi.
pāṇar - icippāṇar, kuḻaṟpāṇar, yāḻppāṇar, maṇṭippāṇar ākiya icit toḻilār.
pārppār - āciriyar pulavar uvaccar kurukkaḷ mutaliya illaṟattārāṉa kalvittoḻilār.
porunar - ērkkaḷam pāṭunarum pōrkkaḷam pāṭunarum
maṟavar - pōruṅ koḷḷiyumākiya maṟattoḻil puriyum pālivāṇar, paṭimaṟavar.
vāṇikar - cillaṟiyākavum mottamākavum paṇṭamāṟṟuc ceyyum nilavāṇikarum, kaṭalkaṭantu veḷināṭṭoṭu vaṇikañceyyum nīrvāṇikarum.
viricci - viricci (Oracle) kūṟupavar. viḷ - viṭu - viṭicci - viricci = teyvattiṉiṭamiruntu maṟivāṉa ceytikaḷi aṟintu veḷippaṭuttutal.
viṉivalar - ēviya toḻil ceyvatil vallavar.
viṟali - viṟal (cattuvam ) paṭa āṭum pāṇmakaḷ.
vēṭṭuvar - vēṭṭit toḻil ceyyum kuṟiñci mulli pāli vāṇar.
vēntar - cēracōḻapāṇṭiyar
vēyar - oṟṟar
vēlaṉ - murukaṉ eṉṉum teyvamēṟi yāṭupavaṉ.
vēḷāḷar - uḻutuṇpōrum uḻuvittuṇpōrum.
iṉi, palvakik kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷum karuvikaḷum kalaṅkaḷum ceyporuṭkaḷum tolkāppiyattiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭirup patāl, avaṟṟi uruvākkiya kollar, taccar, kaṟṟaccar, taṭṭār, kaṉṉār eṉṉum aivakik kammiyarum, kottar necavar kuyavar mutaliya toḻilāḷarum, akkālattil iruntiruttal vēṇṭum.
tolkāppiyar kālat tamiḻaka elli
vaṇpukaḻ mūvar taṇpoḻil varippiṉ
nāṟpeyar elli yakattavar vaḻaṅkum
yāppiṉ vaḻiya teṉmaṉār pulavar  (tol. 1336)
eṉṟu, tolkāppiyam tam kālat tamiḻyāppiṟkuk kūṟiya elliyē, avar kālat tamiḻaka elliyumākum.  oru nāṭṭiṟku  nāṟṟiciyiruppiṉ, nālelliyum irukkattāṉ ceyyum.  atic colla vēṇṭuvatilli.  āyiṉ, ellip peyarik kuṟiyātu nāṟpeyar elli eṉṟu maṭṭum colli yiruttalāl, an nālelliyum vēṟupaṭṭa vakiyiṉavā yiruttal vēṇṭum.  vaṭakkil vēṅkaṭamaliyum teṟkil kumariyāṟum kiḻakkil vaṅkak kaṭalum teḷivāyt terinta ellikaḷām.  mēṟkil etu elliyeṉiṉ, atu kuṭamalit toṭarē.  akkālattil cēranāṭu tirucci māvaṭṭak karuvūrit talinakarākak koṇṭu, perumpālum kuṭamalikkuk kiḻakkēyē iruntatu.  ammalit toṭarukku mēṟkil irunta nilam, mika voṭuṅkit tuṟi nakarkaḷukkaṉṟi oru nāṭṭu makkaḷ aṉivarum vatiyattakka parappuḷḷatā yiruntatilli.  piṟkālat tilēyē mēlkari nilappakuti virivaṭintatākat terikiṉṟatu.  itiyē, paracurāmar tām tavañceytaṟku iṭam vēṇṭik kaṭalmītu ampeyya, atu caṟṟup piṉvāṅki iṭantantatu eṉṉuṅ kati kuṟikkum.  paracurāmar aṅkuc ceṉṟapiṉ, iyaṟkiyākak kaṭal otuṅki nilam virivaṭintatu eṉpatē uṇmi.
vaṇpukaḻ mūvar taṇpoḻil varippiṉ eṉṉun toṭar, tolkāppiyar kālattil mūvēntarum oṟṟumippaṭṭu uyarvā yiruntatiyum, kuṭikaḷ koḷḷiyum pōrumiṉṟi iṉpamāka vāḻntatiyum, kuṟippāka vuṇarttum.
tolkāppiyarukkup piṉ āriyac cūḻcciyālum taṉṉalap paṟṟālum mūvēntariṭiyum aṭikkaṭi pōr mūṇṭataṉālum,  tuṇiyaracarum kuṟunila maṉṉarum tam vēntark kaṭaṅkātu muṭicūṭi muḻuvurimi yaracarāṉataṉālum, cōḻa nāṭṭiṉ vaṭapakuti toṇṭināṭeṉṟum, cēranāṭṭiṉ kīḻppakuti koṅku nāṭeṉṟum pirintu pōyiṉa.
toṇṭināṭu
toṇṭaṭināṭu ārkkāṭu pōl nilittiṇiyāṟ peyar peṟṟatākat terikiṉṟatu.  toṇṭi eṉpatu kōvik koṭiyiyum ātoṇṭiyiyum kuṟikkum.  ātoṇṭi eṉpatu kāṟṟōṭṭic ceṭikkum kāṟṟōṭṭik koṭikkum potuppeyar.  immūṉṟaṉuḷ oṉṟu mikuntirunta nilappakuti toṇṭi nāṭeṉap peyar peṟṟatu.
tiriyaṉ eṉpatu neytal nilat talivaṉ peyar. ārkkāṭu ceṅkaḻunīrppaṭṭu nellur ākiya mum māvaṭṭappakutikaḷik koṇṭatu toṇṭi nāṭu. orukāl neytal nilac ciṟappuppaṟṟi, annāṭṭaracaṉ tiriyaṉ eṉap peyar koṇṭirukkalām. nākappaṭṭiṉattuc cōḻaṉ nāka kaṉṉiyip puṇarntu oru putalvaṉip peṟṟāṉeṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟuṅ katiyum, kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ pīlivaḷi eṉṉum nāka kaṉṉiyip puṇarntu oru putalvaṉip peṟṟāṉeṉṟu cīttalic cāttaṉār kūṟuṅ katiyum, pala ceytikaḷil vēṟupaṭṭaṉavum vevvēṟu kālattaṉavum ākum.
cōḻaṉ karikāṟ peruvaḷattāṉmēṟ paṭṭiṉappāli pāṭiya kaṭiyalur uruttiraṅ kaṇṇaṉārē, perumpāṇāṟṟup paṭiyil,
koṇṭi yuṇṭit toṇṭiyōr maruka
eṉṟu iḷantiriyaṉip pala talimuṟippaṭṭa ōr āḷkuṭi yiṉaṉākak kūṟiyiruppatāl, mēṟkūṟiya katiyiraṇṭum piṟkālattaṉaveṉṟum perumpāluṅ kaṭṭucceyti yeṉṟum aṟika.
aḵtāyiṉ, annīrt tiritaru marapiṉ uravō ṉumpaṉ eṉṟu pāṭiyati iḷantiriyaṉ eṅṅaṉam ottuk koṇṭāṉeṉiṉ, āriyac cārpāl akkālat tamiḻar pakuttaṟivi yiḻantu viṭṭataṉāl,
nīyē, vaṭapāṉ muṉivaṉ taṭaviṉuḷ tōṉṟi
....................................................................................................................................
nāṟpat toṉpatu vaḻimuṟi vanta
vēḷiruḷ vēḷē  (puṟam. 201)
eṉṟu kapilar pāṭiyati iruṅkōvēḷ ēṟṟukkoṇṭatu pōṉṟē, iḷantiriyaṉum uruttiraṅ kaṇṇaṉār pāṭiyati ēṟṟukkoṇṭāṉ eṉṟu kūṟi viṭukka.
koṅku nāṭu
koṅku eṉpatu, maṇam, pūntātu, tēṉ, kaḷ, karuñcuri, coṅku (umi allatu puṟattōl), kuvivu eṉa eṇporuḷ paṭum palaporu ḷorucol. koṅku nāṭṭil tēṉ mikutiyāka allatu ciṟantatākak kiṭippatāl, annāṭu koṅku nāṭeṉap paṭṭatu eṉṟu palar kūṟuvar. tēṉ koṅku nāṭṭiṟkē ciṟappāka vuriya taṉṟu. cōlikaḷ, ciṟappāka iyaṟkic cōlikaḷ, uḷḷa iṭamellām tēṉkūṭu kaṭṭappaṭum. attakiya cōlikaḷāṟ pōrkkap paṭṭiruppavi peru malikaḷ allatu uyarmalittoṭarkaḷ.  koṅkunāṭṭu maṟikaḷiṟ pōṉṟē piṟanāṭṭu malikaḷilum tēṉkūṭu kaṭṭum.
aṇi niṟa vōri pāytaliṉ mītaḻintu
tiṇineṭuṅ kuṉṟan tēṉcoriyum
paṟampumali, muṟkālac cōḻa nāṭṭi allatu piṟkālap pāṇṭināṭṭic cērntatākum. paṟampumalit tēṉ ev vakiyilum tiṟakkēṭuḷḷa taṉṟu. koṅku nāṭṭu āṉi maliyi oru kūṟākakkoṇṭa kuṭamalittoṭar, nelli māvaṭṭat teṉkōṭivari celkiṉṟatu.  aṅkum tēṉkūṭu kaṭṭattāṉ ceykiṉṟatu.
koṅkunāṭṭiṉ paṇṭikkālap parappip palar cariyāka aṟiyavilli.  kuṭakamum erumiyūr (micūr) nāṭum cēlam māvaṭṭaṅkaḷum cērntatē mutaṟkālak koṅku nāṭām.  atil uṭalpōl mikuntiruntatu erumiyūr nāṭē.  mūvēntar nāṭukaḷuḷḷum uyarntu kuvintirukkum nilappakuti erumiyūr nāṭeṉpati, puṟaṇip paṭattiyum (relief map) maṭṭak kōṭṭup paṭattiyum (contour map) pārttut teḷika.  cēranāṭṭiṉ kīḻippakuti, malikaḷālum uyarnila maṭṭat tālum kuvintiruppataṉālēyē koṅku nāṭeṉap paṭṭatu.
kum - kummal = kuviyal.  kummi = ki kuvittaṭittu viḷiyāṭum viḷiyāṭṭu.  kummi - kommi.
kum  - kom - kommi = 1. mēṭu. 2. aṭuppuk kumiḻ. 3. katavuk kuṭumi. 4. kommaṭṭikkāy (kummaṭṭikkāy). 5. ki kuvittuk koṭṭuki. 6. iḷamuli. vāraṇi kommi (paripā.22:30).
kum - kumpu. kumputal = kuvital, kūṭutal.
kumpu - kumpam = 1. kuviyal. 2. kuṭam. 3. yāṉi mattakam. 4. kumpa vōri. 5. kumpa mātam (māci). 
kumpam - va. kumpa (kumbha)
kumpu - kumpaḷam = pūcaṇikkāy.
kumpu - kūmpu - kūppu. kūpputal kuvittal.
kumpu - kumpiṭu. kumpiṭutal ki kuvittal.
kumpu - kuppu - kuppal. kuppu - kuppi - koppi = kummi.
kuppu - kuppi = kuviyal, mēṭu, kaḻivupporuṭ kuviyal.
kum - kumi - kumiḻ - kumiḻi.  kumi - kuvi - kuviyal, kuviyam, kuvāl, kuvi.
kum - kuṅku. kuṅkutal = 1. kuvital. 2. curuṅkutal. 3. kuṉṟutal.
kuṅku - koṅku = kuvinta poruḷ allatu iṭam.
koṅku - koṅki = 1. kuvinta muli. 2. marattiṉ puṭippu (protuberances or knobs of a tree).
koṅka - kōṅku = koṅki pōṟ kuvinta moṭṭu, ataṉ malar, atu pūkkum maram.
vēṅki veṟpiṉ virinta kōṅkiṉ
mukivaṉap pēntiya muṟṟā viḷamuli
eṉpatu, (puṟam. 336:9 - 10)  kōṅkamoṭṭu koṅki pōṟ kuvintateṉpatit terivittal kāṇka.
koṅku nāṭu piṟkālattiṟ kuṭakoṅku kuṇakoṅku eṉa iraṇṭākap pirintatu.  piṉṉark kuṇakoṅkum vaṭakoṅku teṉkoṅku eṉa iraṇṭākap pirintatu.  erumiyūr nāṭṭiṉ mēṟpakuti kuṭakoṅku; kuṭakak koṅkarum (30:159) eṉṟu cilappatikāram kūṟuvatāl, kuṭakap pakutiyē mutaṟ kālak kuṭakoṅkākum. ataṉ kīḻppakutiyum cēlam, kōvi māvaṭṭaṅkaḷum kuṇa koṅku.  atu iraṇṭākap pirintapiṉ, erumiyūr nāṭṭiṉ kīḻppakuti vaṭakoṅku; cēlaṅ kōvi māvaṭṭaṅkaḷ teṉkoṅku.  vaṭakoṅkiṉ teṉpakuti kaṅkanāṭeṉa māṟiṟṟu.
iṉṟu koṅku nāṭeṉap paṭuvatu cēlaṅ kōvi māvaṭṭa nilappakutiyē. atuvē teṉkoṅku vaṭakoṅku mēl koṅku (mīkoṅku) eṉa mūṉṟākap pirintumuḷḷatu.
koṅku nāṭu toṭarnta pōr nikaḻcciyāṟ ceṅkaḷamāki amiti kulintataṉāl, cēraṉ taṉ talinakarik karuvūri ṉiṉṟu mēlkaṭaṟkariyiluḷḷa koṭuṅkōḷūrkku māṟṟik koṇṭāṉ.atu piṉṉark karuvūr vañci eṉṉum peyarkaḷum peṟṟatu.  paḻiya karuvūr cōḻar vayappaṭṭatu.
cēranāṭṭu makkaḷ kuṭamalikkuk kiḻakkiluḷḷa koṅku nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiyataṉālēyē, avar vaḻiyiṉar moḻiyilum, katiravaṉeḻuntici kiḻakku eṉṟum viḻuntici mēṟku eṉṟum, cōḻa pāṇṭi nāṭukaḷiṟ pōṉṟē collap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. kuṭamalikkuk kiḻakkiluḷḷa nilappakutiyil, kaṭalaṭutta kīḻppākam tāḻntum maliyaṭutta mēṟpākam uyarntum iruppatāl, katiravaṉ eḻunticiyum viḻunticiyum kiḻakku mēṟku eṉappaṭṭaṉa.  kīḻ - kīḻkku - kiḻakku.  mēl - mēṟku. kuṭamalikku mēṟkiluḷḷa nilappakuti kiḻakkil uyarntum mēṟkil tāḻntum iruppatāl, kiḻakki mēṟkeṉ ṟum mēṟkik kiḻakkeṉṟum collavēṇṭum.  avvaḻakkiṉ miyāl, cēranāṭṭāriṉ muṉṉōr tamiḻakattiṉ kīḻip 
pakuti yiṉiṉṟē mēlippakutikkuc ceṉṟaṉar eṉpatu teḷivām.
mēṟṟiciyik kuṟikka paṭuñāyiṟu (paṭuñāyiṟṟut tici) eṉṉum colvaḻakkum cēranāṭṭil allatu maliyāḷa nāṭṭil uṇṭēṉum, atuvum cōḻa pāṇṭi nāṭṭiṟ poḻutaṭivik kuṟikkum kīḻit tamiḻccollē yeṉṟum, mēṟku eṉṉum col ataṉāl aṅkut taṉ poruḷi 
iḻantu viṭavilli yeṉṟum, kīḻtticiyik kuṟikkak kiḻakku eṉpatu tavira vēṟoru collumilli yeṉṟum, aṟintu koḷka.
kiḻakkintiyat tīviṉiṉṟu karumpik koṇṭuvantu payirākkiya mutu paḻaṅkālac cēraṉi, atikamāṉiṉ muṉṉōṉeṉṟu auviyār kūṟiyiruppatum, cēranāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ muṉṉōr koṅku nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu ceṉṟavar eṉpati, valiyuṟuttal kāṇka.
kumarimalittoṭar mūḻkiyapiṉ, nilamaṭṭattil vaṭanāṭu uyarntum teṉṉāṭu tāḻntum pōṉataṉāl, vaṭateṉ ticikaṭku uttaram takkaṇam eṉṉum peyarkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷamiyiyum nōkkuka. u = uyarvu. takku = tāḻvu. uttaram - va. uttara. takkaṇam - va. takṣiṇa. eḻuṅkatiravaṉi nōkkum pōtu teṉṟici valappuṟatti liruppataṉāl, katirava vaṇakkaṅ koṇṭa āriyar takṣiṇa eṉṉuñ colliṟku valam (right) eṉṟum poruḷ koṇṭaṉar. pratakṣiṇa = valamākac cuṟṟivarutal. L. dexter = right.
tolkāppiyarkālat tamiḻnūlkaḷum kalikaḷum
tolkāppiyar kālattil, tamiḻiṟ pala kalikaḷum nūlkaḷu miruntaṉa vēṉum, avaṟṟuḷ, tolkāppiyan tavira vēṟoṉṟum akappaṭāmiyāṉ, paṇṭit tamiḻak kalikaḷippaṟṟi virivā yeṭutturikka iyalavilli. āyiṉum, āṉi yaṭiccuvaṭu kaṇṭu āṉi ceṉṟati yaṟital pōla, tolkāppiyattil āṅkāṅkuḷḷa cila kuṟippukaḷiṉiṉṟu, tolkāppiyar kālat til evvek kalikaḷ tamiḻiliruntaṉa veṉṟu aṟiyalām.
(1) ilakkaṇam
tolkāppiyar kālattil tamiḻil ilakkaṇamiruntamikkut tolkāppiyam oṉṟē pōtiya cāṉṟām.  tolkāppiyariṉ uṭaṉ māṇavarāṉa avinayaṉār, kākki pāṭiṉiyār mutaliya piṟarum tamiḻilakkaṇa miyaṟṟiṉati yāpparuṅkala viruttiyuriyā laṟiyalām.
piṟamoḻi yilakkaṇaṅkaḷellām eḻuttu, col, yāppu, aṇi eṉa nāṉku pirivē yuṭiyaṉa.  āṉāl, tamiḻō avaṟ ṟuṭaṉ poruḷ eṉṉum ciṟanta piriviyumuṭiyatu.  yāppu, aṇi eṉpavaṟṟip poruḷil aṭakkit tamiḻilak kaṇatti mūṉṟākap pakuppatē paṇṭi vaḻakkam.  yāppum aṇiyum oru ceyyuḷiṉ poruḷi yuṇartaṟkuk karuvikaḷātaliṉ, avaṟṟip poruḷilakkaṇattiṉiṉṟum pirikkak kūṭāteṉpatu muṉṉōr karuttu.  yāpparuṅkalam, yāpparuṅkalak kāriki eṉpati, tolkāppiyattiṟ kūṟappaṭāta, ceyyuḷilakkaṇaṅkaḷik kūṟuvataṟkut taṉi nūlkaḷāyeḻuntaṉavēyaṉṟi, mūṉṟāyirunta tamiḻilakkaṇap pirivi nāṉkākkuvataṟkaṉṟu.
tamiḻiluḷḷa poruḷilakkaṇatti iṟṟit tamiḻar cariyā yuṇaravilli. pārppaṉarō avariṉum uṇaravilli.  paṇṭit tamiḻar matinuṭpamellām paḻuttukkiṭappatu poruḷilakkaṇam oṉṟiltāṉ.  oru makkaḷiṉ nākarikattik kāṭṭac ciṟanta ciṉṉam moḻiyē.  pala karuttukaḷiyum teri vippataṟkuriya coṟkaḷum col vaṭivaṅkaḷum, virivāṉa ilak kiyamum oru moḻiyiṉ ciṟappik kāṭṭum. ilakkiyattiṉum ilakkaṇam ciṟantatu.  ilakkaṇattiṟ ciṟantatu tamiḻmoḻi oṉṟē. ākiyāl ulakamoḻikaḷ ellāvaṟṟiṉum tamiḻē mikat tiruntiya teṉpataṟku eḷḷaḷavum aiyamilli.
eḻuttāṟ collum collāṟ poruḷum uṇarappaṭum. eḻuttu, col eṉpavaṟṟiṉ kūṟupāṭukaḷum iyalpukaḷum, avaṟṟic cevviyā yuṇartaṟku eṅṅaṉam payaṉpaṭumō, aṅṅaṉamē poruḷiṉ kūṟupāṭum ataṉ iyalpukaḷum poruḷic cevviyā yuṇartaṟkup payaṉpaṭum.
poruḷilakkaṇam oṉṟiyē ilakkaṇamākavum, ēṉi eḻuttum collum ataṟkuk karuvikaḷākavum koṇṭaṉar muṉṉōr.  iti,
pāṇṭiya aracaṉum puṭipaṭak kavaṉṟu, eṉṉi? eḻuttuñ
collum ārāyvatu poruḷatikārattiṉ poruṭṭaṉṟē! poruḷatikāram peṟēmeṉiṉ, ivi peṟṟum peṟṟilēm... (iṟi(pa),pa,8,9) eṉak kūṟiyatāl aṟiyalām.
poruḷilakkaṇam vīṭum, eḻuttilakkaṇamum collilak kaṇamum, muṟiyē ataṟkuṭ cella vaḻiyāyiruttalāl vāyilum naṭiyum pōlvaṉa.
poruḷilakkaṇattil, poruḷkaḷ ellām akam puṟam eṉa iraṇṭākavum, avaṟṟuḷ, akam kikkiḷi aintiṇi peruntiṇi eṉa ēḻākavum, puṟam veṭci, vañci, uḻiñi, tumpi, vāki, kāñci, pāṭāṇ eṉa ēḻākavum vakukkappaṭum.
oḻukkanūṟ pākupāṭāṉa aṟam, poruḷ, iṉpam, vīṭu  eṉṉum nāṟporuḷkaḷ iṉpam iṉṉavāṟiruntateṉṟu piṟarkkup pulaṉākumpaṭi eṭuttukkūṟa iyalātavāṟu, akattāṉāyē uṇarntu nukarappaṭutaliṉ akameṉappaṭṭa teṉṟum, aṅṅaṉamaṉṟip piṟarkkup pulappaṭak kūṟuvatāy iṉpattiṟkup puṟamāṉatellām puṟameṉappaṭṭateṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
cilar akapporuḷilakkaṇatti aṉpunūl (Science of Love) eṉṟum, puṟapporuḷilakkaṇattip pōrnūl (Science of War) eṉṟum koṇṭaṉar.  aṉpu ciṟappatu perumpālum kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyiruvariṭiyē yātalālum, aṉpiṟketirāṉa 
pakiyā lēyē pōr nikaḻvatālum ivvurikōḷum oruvāṟu poruntuvatē.
māntaṉ ulakil aṭiyakkūṭiya aṟam poruḷ iṉpam eṉṉum mūṉṟaṉuḷ, aṟam taṉittirātu ēṉi yiraṇṭiyum paṟṟiyēyirukkum. aṟavaḻiyāl īṭṭuvatē poruḷ.  aṟavaḻiyāl nukarvatē iṉpam. aṟavaḻiyāl īṭṭāta poruḷālum, allaṟa vaḻiyāl iṉpa nukarntukoṇṭum, ceyyappaṭum aṟam aṟamākātu.  iṉpatti nukarvataṟkup poruḷ karuvi. ākavē, poruḷīṭṭuvatum ataṉāl iṉpam nukarvatumākiya iraṇṭē ellā makkaḷiṉ toḻilkaḷum. ulaka iṉpattiṟ ciṟantatu peṇṇiṉpam allatu maṉiviyiṉpam. itu perumpālum vīṭṭilēyē nukarappaṭuvatu. poruḷ muyaṟci perumpālum vīṭṭiṟku veḷiyē nikaḻvatu, makkaḷ talivaṉāṉa aracaṉatu poruḷ muyaṟci pōr. akam vīṭu, puṟam vīṭṭiṟku veḷi.  puṟappaṭṭāṉ eṉṉuñ col, vīṭṭiṟku veḷippaṭṭāṉ eṉṟa karuttuppaṟṟiyē, vaḻippōkkut toṭaṅkiṉāṉ eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvatākum.  ākavē, akattil nikaḻvati akam eṉṟum, puṟattil nikaḻvatip puṟameṉṟum muṉṉōr koṇṭaṉar eṉiṉum poruntum.  akam eṉpatu centamiḻc collē.  pārppaṉar tam vīṭukaḷip piṟa kulattār vīṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu pirittuk kāṭṭuvataṟku akam eṉṉuñ collit terintu koṇṭaṉar.
iṉi, makkaḷ āṇ peṇ eṉa irupāṟpaṭutaliṉ, av virupālpaṟṟiya toḻilkaḷ akam puṟam eṉa vakuttuk kūṟiṉar 
eṉṟu koḷḷiṉum poruntum.  uyartiṇiyil peṇpālē aḻakuṭiyatu.  peṇ eṉṉum col virumpattakkatu allatu virumpattakkavaḷ eṉṉum poruḷiyuṭiyatu.  virumpat takuvatu iṉpamum aḻakumpaṟṟi. iṉpattiṟku aḻaku tuṇi yāvatu. itaṉāl, kāmar eṉṉum viruppampaṟṟiya col aḻaku eṉṉum poruḷpaṭuvatāyiṟṟu.
peḷ - peṇ. peṭpu = viruppam. peṇ - pēṇ. pēṇutal, virumputal allatu virumpip pātukāttal, peṇ- piṇā. piṇavu - piṇaval. peḷ + ti = peṭṭi - peṭi - pēṭi - pēṭu, peṇ vīṭṭiliruppavaḷ.  itaṉālēyē, il, maṉi, kuṭi eṉṉum vīṭṭuppeyarkaḷ maṉiviyiyuṅ kuṟikkalāyiṉa.  akattiluḷḷa maṉivipaṟṟiyatu akapporuḷ.  āṇpāl maṟattiṟkuc ciṟantatu.  āḷ - āṇ. āṇ eṉpatu āḷviṉit tiṟamiyuṭiyatu allatu maṟamuṭiyatu eṉṉum poruḷatu.  āṭci, āḷal, āṇmi eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷi nōkkuka. āṇ - (āṭu) - āṭavaṉ -. (āṭu) - āṭūu. āṇmittoḻil pōrttoḻilum poruḷīṭṭalum.  akattukku (vīṭṭukku)p puṟampē nikaḻum toḻil puṟapporuḷ.
poruḷilakkaṇattippaṟṟic curuṅkac coṉṉāl, ellā karuttukaḷum toḻilum paṟṟiya makkaḷ vāḻkkiyiyē, akam puṟam eṉṉum iruvakiyāl nuṭpamāy ārāyntaṉar muṉṉōr eṉalām.  paṇṭit tamiḻar kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭṭilum vīṭupēṟṟu muyaṟciyilum ciṟantiruntamiyiṉ, akapporu ḷilakkaṇattik kaṭavuṭkum āṉmāviṟkum uḷḷa toṭarpik kuṟikkum piṟitumoḻi yuvamiyākavuṅ koṇṭaṉar.
puṟapporuṭkuriya eḻutiṇikaḷuḷ, aintu tiṇikaḷil aracarkkum maṟavarkku muriya pōrttoḻilic ciṟappāyk kūṟi, vākittiṇiyil ellāt toḻilkaḷiyum kali kaḷiyum pāṭāṇṭiṇiyil pukaḻnūlkaḷiṉ ellā vaki kaḷiyum aṭakkiṉar muṉṉi yilakkaṇikaḷ.
vākittiṇiyiṉ ilakkaṇamāvatu:
vāki tāṉē pāliyatu puṟaṉē
tāvil koḷḷit tattam kūṟṟip
pākupaṭa mikutip paṭuttal eṉpa  (tol.puṟat.15)
eṉpatu.
vākittiṇiyiṉ pākupāṭāvatu:
aṟuvakip paṭṭa pārppaṉap pakkamum
aivaki marapiṉ aracar pakkamum
irumūṉṟu marapiṉ ēṉōr pakkamum
maṟuvil ceyti mūvakik kālamum
neṟiyiṉ āṟṟiya aṟivaṉ tēyamum
nāliru vaḻakkiṟ ṟāpatap pakkamum
pālaṟi marapiṟ porunar kaṇṇum
aṉinili vakiyō ṭāṅkeḻu vakiyāṉ
tokinili peṟṟa teṉmaṉār pulavar  (tol.puṟat.16)
eṉpatu.
paṇṭi yulakil maṟam ciṟanta kuṇamākak karutappaṭṭatālum, pakiyaracarip pōrālaṭakkiṉālaṉṟik kuṭikaṭkup pātukāppum toḻil naṭipēṟu miṉmiyāṉum, kalikaḷi vaḷarkkum aracar nūlvāyilāyp pukaḻappaṭaṟ kuriyarātalāṉum, aracar toḻilit talimipaṟṟic collavē kuṭikaḷ toḻilum oruvāṟu ataṉuḷ aṭaṅkutalāṉum, pōrttoḻil poruḷilak kaṇattiṟ ciṟappāyk kūṟappaṭṭateṉka.
cāṉṟōṉ eṉṉum tamiḻnāṭṭu maṟavar peyariyum Knight eṉṉum mēṉāṭṭu maṟavar peyariyum oppunōkkuka.
itil mukkiyamāṉavaṟṟik kūṟi ēṉiyavaṟṟi aṉinili vaki yeṉpati laṭakkiṉār tolkāppiyar.  paṇṭit tamiḻāciriyar palar aṟiva(citta) rāyiruntamiyiṉ, avariyaṟṟiya kalinūlkaḷellām aṟivaṉ tēyamāy aṭaṅkum.
ceyyuḷilakkaṇam: ceyyuḷuṟuppukaḷil aṭippaṭiyāṉatu aci.  mēliyāriya moḻikaḷilellām, aci vakuppu aciyaḻuttattip (Accentuation of syllables) poṟuttatu. aṅku acikaḷ (Thesis - unaccented syllable) iraṇṭāka vakukkap peṟum. vaṭamoḻiyil laku kuru eṉṉum pākupāṭum im muṟiyoṭṭiyatē.  tamiḻilō, āriya moḻikaḷiṟpōla eḷiya muṟiyil ovvō ruyireḻuttāyp piriyāmal, eḻuttukaḷ taṉittolippatum iṇintolippatumpaṟṟi, nēraci niriyaciyeṉṟum, avi kuṟṟiya lukarattoṭu kūṭivarutal paṟṟi, nērpu aci niripu aci eṉṟum nāṉkāka  vakuttaṉar. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉavi iyalaci; piṉṉavi uriyaci.
ceyyuḷkaḷil veṇpā, kalippā eṉṉum pākkaḷum avaṟṟiṉ vakikaḷum piṟamoḻikaṭkilli.
ceppalōcikku veṇpāvum, akavalōcikku āciriyamum tuḷḷa lōcikkuk kalippāvum, tūṅkalōcikku vañciyum ciṟantaṉaveṉṟu kaṇṭupiṭittatum, akavali nūṟpāviṟkum, kaliyiyum ataṉ vēṟupāṭākiya paripāṭaliyum akap poruṭkum, ampōtaraṅka vottāḻicik kali, koccaka vorupōku mutaliya kalivakikaḷik kaṭavuḷ vāḻttiṟkum maruṭpāvik kikkiḷikkum urimiyākkiṉatum, tamiḻariṉ ciṟanta matinuṭpattiyum nuṇṇiya cevippulaṉiyuṅ kuṟippaṉa vākum. ceppal - viḷiyiṟuttal. akaval - oruvari viḷittuk kūṟal.
epporuḷukkum ēṟṟa ōciyiṟ ceyyuḷ ceyvatum, palavaki vaṇṇaṅ kaḷiṟ pāṭuvatum, coṟceṟivum, tamiḻukkē ciṟap pāyuriyaṉa.  tamiḻ yāppumuṟi mikamika virivāṉatu.
tamiḻc ceyyuḷkaḷil mikac ciṟantatu kalippāvākum. 
tamiḻcceyyuḷkaḷ pā, pāviṉam eṉa iruvakippaṭum.  avaṟṟuḷ, pā veṇpā, āciriyappā, kalippā, vañcippā eṉa nāṉku.  paripāṭal kaliyiṉ tiripu.  maruṭpā mutaliru pākkaḷiṉ kalavi. pāviṉam, tuṟi, tāḻici, maṇṭilam eṉa mūṉṟu.  ivi ovvoru pāviṟku muriyaṉa. maṇṭilattip piṟ 
kālattār viruttam eṉṉum vaṭacollāṟ kuṟittatuṭaṉ, vaṭamoḻi yāppākavuṅ kāṭṭiṉar. 
oru moḻiyiṉ vaṭivam urinaṭi. oyyuḷ eṉa iraṇṭu.  avaṟṟuḷ ceyyuḷ ciṟantatu. ceyyuḷiṟ ciṟantavar tamiḻar. muṟkālattil tamiḻppulavar uriyāṭṭellām ceyyuḷāka vēyiruntaṉa.  kalikaḷellām perumpālum ceyyuḷilēyē eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. akarāti (nikaṇṭu) kūṭac ceyyuḷi leḻutap paṭṭatu. palvakit toḻilāḷarum ceyyuḷiyaṟṟun tiṟami yuṭiyavarāyiruntaṉar. muṉṉiya payiṟciyum ūkkalumiruppiṉ, ikkālattum kaṭumpāvalar (ācukaviñar) tōṉṟalām.
ceyyuḷip piṟamoḻiyārellām eḻuttu, aci, cīr, taḷi, aṭi, toṭi eṉa aṟuvakiyāyē yārāyntaṉar; tamiḻarō irupattāṟu vakiyā yārāyntār. avvakiyāvaṉa:
māttiri yeḻuttiya lacivaki yeṉāa
......................................................................
āṟutali yiṭṭa vannā lintum
............................................................................
nallicip pulavar ceyyu ḷuṟuppeṉa
vallitiṟ kūṟi vakutturit taṉarē  (tol.cey.1)
1)	māttiri yeṉpatu, eḻuttiṟkōtiya māttirikaḷic ceyyuḷ virāyk kiṭakkum aḷaviyeṉṟavāṟu....
2)	eḻuttiyalvaki yeṉpatu, mēṟkūṟiya eḻuttukaḷi iyaṟṟik koḷḷuṅ kūṟupāṭu.
3)	acivaki yeṉpatu acik kūṟupāṭu;avi iyalaciyum uriyaciyumeṉa iraṇṭām.
4)	yāttacīr eṉpatu, poruḷpeṟat toṭarntu niṟkuñ cīreṉṟavāṟu.
5)	aṭiyeṉpatu, accīr iraṇṭum palavun toṭarntāvatōr uṟuppu.
6)	yāppu eṉpatu, av vaṭitoṟum poruḷpeṟac ceyva toru ceyki.
7)	marapu eṉpatu kālamu miṭaṉumpaṟṟi vaḻakkut tirintakkālun tirintavaṟṟukku ēṟpa vaḻuppaṭāmic ceyvatōr muṟimi.
8)	tūkku eṉpatu, pākkaḷit tuṇintu niṟuttal
9)	toṭivaki yeṉpatu, toṭippakuti palavumeṉṟavāṟu
10)	nōkku eṉpatu, māttiri mutalākiya uṟuppukaḷik kēṭṭōrkku nōkkuppaṭac ceytal.
11)	pā eṉpatu, cēṭpulattirunta kālattum oruvaṉ eḻuttuñ collun teriyāmaṟ pāṭamōtuṅkāl, avaṉ collu kiṉṟa ceyyuḷi vikaṟpittu iṉṉa ceyyuḷeṉṟu uṇartaṟ kētuvākip parantupaṭṭuc celvatōr ōci.
12)	aḷaviyal eṉpatu, appā variyaṟi.
13)	tiṇi yeṉpatu, akam puṟameṉṟu aṟiyacceytal
14)	kikōḷ eṉpatu, avvat tiṇi yoḻukka vikaṟpam aṟiyac ceytal
15)	kūṟṟuvaki yeṉpatu, ac ceyyuḷ kēṭṭāri itu collukiṉṟār iṉṉāreṉa uṇarvittal.....
16)	kēṭpōr eṉpatu, iṉṉārkkuc collukiṉṟatu ituveṉat terittal.
17)	kaḷaṉ eṉpatu, mulli kuṟiñci mutalāyiṉavum iravukkuṟi pakaṟkuṟi mutalāyiṉavum uṇarac ceytal...
18)	kālavaki yeṉpatu, ciṟupoḻutu perumpoḻuteṉṉuṅ kālappakuti mutalāyiṉa.
19)	payaṉ eṉpatu, colliya poruḷāṟ piṟitoṉṟu payappac ceytal.
20)	meyppāṭu eṉpatu, coṟkēṭṭōrkkup poruḷ kaṇ kūṭātal.
21)	eccavaki yeṉpatu, collappaṭāta moḻikaḷik kuṟittukkoḷḷac ceytal.
22)	muṉṉam eṉpatu, uyarntōrum iḻintōrum ottōrum tattam vakiyāṉ oppac collutaṟkuk karuttuppaṭac ceytal.
23)	poruḷ eṉpatu, pulavaṉ tāṉ tōṟṟikkoṇṭu ceyyap paṭuvatōr poruṇmi.
24)	tuṟivaki eṉpatu, mutalum karuvum muṟipiṟaḻa vantālum iḵtu itaṉpāṟpaṭu meṉṟu oru tuṟippaṭuttaṟku ētuvākiyatōr karuvi ac ceyyuṭkuḷatākac ceytal.
25)	māṭṭu eṉpatu, palvēṟu poruṭ parappiṟṟāyiṉum aṉṟāyiṉum niṉṟataṉoṭu vantataṉi oru toṭar koḷīi muṭittuk koḷḷac ceytal.
(26)	vaṇṇam eṉpatu. oru pāviṉ kaṇṇē nikaḻum ōci vikaṟpam (tol.cē.1 pērāciriyar uri)
ivaṟṟuḷ kūṟṟu, kēṭpōr, kaḷaṉ mutaliya cila vuṟuppukaḷ ellā moḻikaṭkum potuvēṉum, avi ceyyuḷuṟuppu kaḷiṟ cērkkappaṭṭatum, nōkku, tiṇi, tuṟi mutaliya piṟavuṟuppukaḷil cērkkappaṭṭatum tamiḻukkuc ciṟappākum.
mēṟkūṟiya 26 uṟuppukaḷiyum tolkāppiyattiṟ kaṇṭu koḷka. iṅku nōkku eṉpataṟku maṭṭum oru kāṭṭut tarukiṉṟēṉ.
mulli vinnuṉi tōṉṟa villamoṭu
piṅkāṟ koṉṟi meṉpiṇi yaviḻa
irumpu tirittaṉṉa māyiru maruppiṟ
paralava laṭiya virali teṟippa
malarnta ñālam pulampupuṟak koṭuppak
karuvi vāṉaṅ kataḻuṟi citaṟik
kārcey taṉṟē kaviṉperu kāṉaṅ
kuraṅkuḷip polinta koycuvaṟ puravi
narampārp paṉṉa vāṅkuvaḷ pariyap
pūtta poṅkart tuṇiyoṭu vatinta
tātuṇ paṟavi pētuṟa lañci
maṇinā vārtta māṇviṉit tēra
ṉuvakkāṇ ṭōṉṟuṅ kuṟumpoṟi nāṭaṉ
kaṟaṅkici viḻavi ṉuṟantik kuṇātu
neṭumperuṅ kuṉṟat tamaṉṟa kāntaṭ
pōtavi ḻalari ṉāṟu
māytoṭi yariviniṉ māṇalam paṭarntē  (akam.4)
mulliyeṉṉātu vinnuṉi eṉṟataṉāṉ, avi arumpiyatu aṇitteṉpatu peṟappaṭṭatu; ciṉṉāḷ kaḻiyiṉ vinnuṉiyākātu melleṉṉu mākaliṉ. illamum koṉ ṟiyum melleṉṟa piṇiyaviḻntaṉa veṉṟāṉ, mullik koṭi karinta tuṇi avi mutal keṭātu mulli yarumpiya kālattu malarntamiyiṉ. irali maruppiṉi, irumpu tirittaṉṉa maruppu eṉṟatūum, nīr tōyntum veyiluḻanta veppantaṇintila, irumpu muṟukkiviṭṭa vaḻi veppam māṟāviṭṭavāṟupōla vempā niṉṟaṉa viṉṉumeṉṟavāṟu. paralava laṭiya virali teṟippa eṉavē, paralpaṭu kuḻitōṟun teḷintu niṉṟa nīrkku viruntiṉavākalāṟ palakālum  nīrparukiyum ap paral avaliṉatu aṭikari viṭātu tuḷḷukiṉṟaṉaveṉa atuvum paruvan toṭaṅkiṉami kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu. karuvi vāṉaṅ kataḻuṟi citaṟi eṉpatūum palavuṟuppuṅ kuṟipaṭātu tokku niṉṟaṉa mēkan taṉatu vīkkattiṭik kāṟṟeṟiyappaṭuta liṉ virintu tuḷi citaṟiṉa veṉṟavaṟṟiyum putumi kūṟi ṉāḷ; eṉavē, iviyellām paruvan toṭaṅki yaṇitteṉṟa miyiṉ vaṟpuṟuttutaṟku ilēcāyiṟṟu. kuraṅkuḷip polinta koycuvaṟ puravi eṉpatu, koyyāta uḷi palkiyum koyta uḷi palakāluṅ koyya vēṇṭutalumuṭiya kutiri yeṉ vāṟu; eṉavē, taṉatu maṉappukaḻcci kūṟiyavāṟu, attuṇi mikutiyuṭiya kutiri pūṭṭiṉa vāroli vilakkātu maṇiyoli vilakki vārāniṉṟāṉ aṅṅaṉam māṭcimippaṭṭa māṉṟō ṉātalāṉeṉṟavāṟu. ataṟkeṉṉi kāraṇa meṉiṉ, tuṇi yōṭu vatiyun tātuṇ paṟavi eṉavē, pirivañci yeṉṟavāṟu. maṇi nāvoli kēṭpiṉ vaṇṭu veruvumākaliṉ atu kēḷāmimaṇināviṉi iyaṅkāmi yāppitta māṇviṉit tēraṉāki vārāniṉṟā ṉeṉa iviyellān talimakaḷ vaṉpuṟik kētuvāyiṉa.  kaṟaṅkici viḻavi ṉuṟantik kuṇātu, neṭumperuṅ kuṉṟat tamaṉṟa kāntaḷ. teyvamali ākalāṉ ataṉuḷamaṉṟa kāntaḷit teyvap pūveṉak kūṟi, avi pōta viḻntāṟ pōla avar puṇarnta kālattup putumaṇaṅ kamaḻnta niṉ kittoṭikaḷ avi ariyavākip pirinta kālattup pōṉṟu vaṭivottu maṉaṅkuṟipaṭṭa tuṇiyēyāl avar pirintu ceyta taṉmi yiṉeṉa, ituvum vaṉpuṟikkē uṟup pāyiṟṟu (tol.cey.103 pērāciriyar uri.)
aṇiyilakkaṇam: aṇiyi muṟkālat tamiḻar yāppiṉiṉṟum pirikka villi. uvamiyiṉ vēṟupāṭukaḷiyum, eḻuttilak kaṇattilum collilak kaṇattilumuḷḷa pala amitikaḷi yumē piṟkālattār aṇiyilakkaṇamākap pirittuk kāṭṭiṉar.
uvami yeṉṉun tavalaruṅ kūtti
palvakik kōlam pāṅkuṟap puṉintu
kāppiya  varaṅkiṟ kaviṉuṟat tōṉṟi
yāppaṟi pulava ritaya
nīppaṟu makiḻcci pūppa naṭikkumē  (māṟaṉalaṅkāram; varalāṟu.pak.10.)
iṉi, iv vōttiṉiṟ (uvamaviyal) kūṟukiṉṟa uvamaṅkaḷuṭ cilavaṟṟi yum, collatikārattiṉuḷḷuñ ceyyuḷiya luḷḷum collukiṉṟaṉa cila poruḷkaḷiyum vāṅkikkoṇṭu, maṟṟavi ceyyuṭkaṇṇē aṇiyāmeṉa ikkālattāciriyar nūl ceytārumuḷar.  avi oru taliyākac ceyyuṭku aṇi yeṉṟu ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟappaṭā; eṉṉi? vallār ceyyiṉ aṇiyākiyum allār ceyyiṉ aṇiyaṉṟākiyum varuntāṅ kāṭṭiya ilakkaṇattiṟ citiyā vaḻiyumeṉpatu....
iṉi, iraṇṭumporu ḷeṇṇiyavaṟṟi viṉippaṭukkuṅ kāl oruṅkeṉpatōr coṟpeytal ceyyuṭku aṇiyeṉpa. piṟavum iṉṉōraṉṉa palavuñ ceyyuṭkaṇiyā meṉpatu avar karuttu. oruṅkē yeṉpatēyaṉṟi mūṉṟu tāḻiciyuḷ mūṉṟu poruḷkūṟi, eṉavāṅku eṉpatoru collāṉ muṭintavaḻiyum eṉavāṅkueṉpatōr moḻi eṉaveṉpatu oralaṅkāra meṉal vēṇṭu mākalāṉ, avvāṟu variyaṟuttuk kūṟalami yāteṉpatu. piṟavum aṉṉa.
iṉi, avaṟṟip poruḷuṟuppeṉpatallatu aṇi yeṉpa vāyiṟ cāttaṉiyuñ cāttaṉā laṇiyappaṭṭa muṭiyuntoṭiyum mutalāyavaṟṟiyum vēṟu kaṇṭāṟpōla. av vaṇiyuñ ceyyuḷiṉ vēṟākal vēṇṭumeṉpatu.
iṉi, ceyyuṭku aṇiceyyum poruṭpaṭi ellāṅ kūṟātu
cilavē kūṟi oḻiyiṉ atu kuṉṟak kūṟalāmeṉpatu.  (tol.uva.37, pērā. uri.)
uvami tolkāppiyattiluḷḷa uvamaviyalil mika virivāka viḷakkap paṭṭuḷḷatu. ataṉ vakikaḷil uḷḷuṟiyuvamam eṉpatu mikac ciṟantatākum.  atit tamiḻar akapporuṭ ceyyuḷ kaḷil kiyāṇṭu vantaṉar. atu oru nāṭṭu varaṇaṉiyāy, allatu karupporuṭṭoḻilik kūṟuvatāyamintu, an nāṭṭaracaṉiṉ iyaliyum ceyaliyuṅ kuṟippāyt terivip patākum. itu maṉṉa ṉeppaṭi maṉṉuyi rappaṭi eṉṉum neṟi muṟi paṟṟiyatu. uḷḷuṟut tiyaṟṟappaṭum uvamam uḷḷuṟi yuvamam.
vīṅkunī raviḻnīlam pakarpavar vayiṟkoṇṭa
ñāṅkar palarcūḻtan tūrpukunta varivaṇ
ṭōṅkuya reḻilyāṉik kaṉikaṭaṅ kamaḻnāṟṟa
māṅkavi viruntāṟṟap pakalalkik kaṅkulāṉ
vīṅkiṟi vaṭukkoḷa vīḻunarp puṇarntavar
tēṅkamaḻ katuppiṉa ḷarumpaviḻ naṟumulli
pāyntūtip paṭartīrntu paṇṭutā marīiya
pūmpoyki maṟantuḷḷāp puṉalaṇi nallūra  (kalit.66)
itaṉuḷ, vīṅkunīr parattiyar cēriyākavum, ataṉ kaṇ  aviḻnta nīlappūk kāmaccevvi nikaḻum parattiyarākavum, pakarpavar, parattiyarit tērēṟṟik koṇṭuvarum pāṇaṉ mutaliya vāyilkaḷākavum, am malaric cūḻnta vaṇṭu tali vaṉākavum, yāṉiyiṉ kaṭātti āṇṭuṟinta vaṇṭukaḷ. vanta vaṇṭukku viruntāṟṟutal pakaṟpoḻutu puṇarkiṉṟa cērip parattiyar tamatu nalatti at talivaṉi nukarvittalākavum, kaṅkuliṉ vaṇṭu mulliyi ūtutal iṟparattiyaruṭaṉ iravu tuyiluṟutalākavum, paṇṭu maruviya poykiyi maṟattal taliviyi maṟattalākavum, poruḷ tantu, āṇṭup pulappaṭak kūṟiya karupporuḷkaḷ pulappaṭak kūṟāta marutattiṇip poruṭku uvamamāyk kēṭṭō ṉuḷḷattē viḷakki niṉṟavāṟu kāṇka.
ilakkiyam
tamiḻilakkiyatti iyal, ici, nāṭakam eṉa mūvakiyāka vakuttu, avaṟṟi muttamiḻ eṉṟaṉar muṉṉōr.
iyalpākavuḷḷatu iyaṟṟamiḻ; ataṉōṭu arākamum tāḷamum cērntatu icittamiḻ.  icittamiḻoṭu āṭṭañ cērntatu nāṭakattamiḻ.
muttamiḻil ovvoṉṟum ilakkaṇam, ilakkiyam eṉa iru pirivuṭiyatu. tolkāppiyam pōṉṟatu iyaṟṟamiḻilakkaṇam; maṇimēkali pōṉṟatu iyaṟṟamiḻ ilakkiyam. ici nuṇukkam pōṉṟatu icittamiḻ ilakkaṇam; tēvāram pōṉṟatu icittamiḻilakkiyam. ceyiṟṟiyam pōṉṟatu nāṭakattamiḻ ilakkaṇam; cīkāḻi aruṇācalakkavirāyar iyaṟṟiya irāmayāṇa nāṭakam pōṉṟatu (āṉāl uriyiṭi yiṭṭatu) nāṭaka ilakkiyam. akattiyam muttamiḻilakkaṇam; cilappatikāram muttamiḻ ilakkiyam eṉṟu collalām.
tamiḻnāṭaka vilakkiyattic cilar āriya moḻikaḷiluḷḷa nāṭakat toṭarnilic ceyyuḷ pōṉṟatākak karutukiṉṟaṉar.  icippāṭṭum uriyumāka amintu naṭittaṟkēṟṟa niliyiluḷḷatē tamiḻnāṭaka vilakkiyamākum.
ilakkaṇam ilakkiyam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ tūya tamiḻc coṟkaḷē. ivi ilakku eṉṉum pakutiyaṭiyāyp piṟantu muṟiyē, aṇam, iyam eṉṉum īṟu peṟṟaviyākum.  ilakku ili, ilakkittal eḻututal (cīvaka.180). ilakkaṇam, ilakkiyam eṉpavaṟṟiṉ tiripē, lakṣaṇam lakṣiyam eṉṉum vaṭacoṟkaḷ. vaṭamoḻiyil ilakkaṇattik kuṟikkum col viyākaraṇam eṉpatu. ilakkaṇattik kuṟikka ilakkaṇam eṉṟa collaṉṟi vēṟu col tamiḻil illi.  iṅṅaṉamē ilakkiyattiṟkum, ciṟanta ilakkaṇamum ilakkiyamu muṭiya teṉmoḻikku avaṟṟik kuṟikkum col illi yeṉṟāl, aṟiñar nampa muṭiyumā?
icittamiḻ - (Musical Literature)
iciyāvatu oci. atu pāṭṭiṟkuriya iṉṉiciyic ciṟappāyuṇarttiṟṟu. iṉṉici = caṅkītam. (iṉṉiciyaraṅku = caṅkītak kaccēri).
tamiḻ talikkaḻakak kālattil iyalici nāṭakameṉa muppirivā yiruntateṉpatu, akattiyam muttamiḻilakkaṇa nūl eṉpatāl aṟikiṉṟōm.  tolkāppiyam iyaṟṟamiḻ ilakkaṇa nūlāyiṉum, icittamiḻk kuṟippukaḷ āṅkāṅku cilavuḷa.
aviyāvaṉa:
aḷapiṟan tuyirttalum oṟṟici nīṭalum
uḷaveṉa moḻipa iciyoṭu civaṇiya
narampiṉ maṟiya eṉmaṉār pulavar  (eḻuttu.33)
…….ceyti yāḻiṉ pakutiyoṭutokiia
avvaki piṟavum karuveṉa moḻipa  (akat.20)
tuṟiyami nalyāḻt tuṇimiyōr iyalpē  (kaḷavu.1)
pāṇaṉ pāṭi....... ṉi vāyilkaḷ eṉpa  (kaṟpu.52)
pāṭṭiṉ iyala paṇṇattiyiyalg (cey. 173)
eṉpaṉa.
ici ellā nāṭṭiṟkum potuvēṉum, tamiḻar atil taliciṟantiruntami paṟṟi ati moḻippakutiyākkiṉar.  iḻavu vīṭṭil aḻuvatukūṭa arākattōṭu aḻuvatu tamiḻar vaḻakkam.  arākattōṭu aḻāviṭṭāl, aḻat teriyātavarāka eṇṇappaṭuvar.
icikku uṟuppāvaṉa arākam, tāḷam eṉa iraṇṭu. arākam ar eṉṉum olikkuṟippiṉiṉṟum piṟantu iciyik kuṟikkum pākupāṭu vēṟu moḻiyil illi. coṟkaḷiyum avi kuṟikkum poruḷkaḷpaṟṟi irutiṇiyāka vakuttaṉar.
tamiḻil. coṟkaḷ avi kuṟikkum poruḷkaḷ pataṟiyē pāluṇarttum āriya moḻikaḷilō, avi īṟupaṟṟiyēporuḷuṇarttum.
vaṭamoḻiyil maṉiviyik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷum. taram, pariyi, kaḷattiram eṉṉum mūṉṟum muṟiyē āṇ pālum, peṇpālum alippālumām
uyilkaḷi ōraṟivuyir mutalāka āṟaṟivuyi rīṟāka aṟuvakiyākap  pakuttaṉar tamiḻar.
oṉṟaṟi vatuvē uṟṟaṟi vatuvē
iraṇṭaṟi vatuvē ataṉoṭu nāvē
mūṉṟaṟi vatuvē avaṟṟoṭu mūkkē
nāṉkaṟi vatuvē avaṟṟoṭu kaṇṇē
aintaṟi vatuvē avaṟṟoṭu ceviyē
āṟaṟi vatuvē avaṟṟoṭu maṉaṉē
nēritiṉ uṇarntōr neṟippaṭut tiṉarē  (marapu.27)
eṉṟu muṉṉōr uyirkaḷip pakuttamiyik kūṟi, ataṟkaṭutta nūṟpākkaḷil, av vuyirkaṭkup pullum maraṉum, nantum muraḷum, cītalum eṟumpum naṇṭum tumpiyum māvu mākkaḷum, makkaḷum piṟavum eṉa muṟiyē kāṭṭut tantaṉar tolkāppiyar.
nilamtī nīrvaḷi vicumpō ṭintum
kalanta mayakkam ulakam ātaliṉ  (tol. marapu.86)
eṉpataṉāl, aimpūta vuṇarcciyum tamiḻarkkiruntami yaṟiyappaṭum.
maṟuvil ceyti ... aṟivaṉ tēyamum eṉṟu tolkāppiyar kūṟuvatiṉālum, tamiḻar meypporuḷuṇarcciyi oruvā ṟuṇaralām.
oḻukka nūl (Ethics)
tolkāppiyattil, puṟattiṇiyiyalil 44ām nūṟpāvil mūṉṟaṉ pakuti eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatāl, oḻukkanūl ak kālattil tamiḻiliruntami uṇarappaṭum. mūṉṟaṉ pakutiyāvatu aṟattiṉāl poruḷākki apporuḷāl iṉpam nukartal eṉṟal.  aṟamporu ḷiṉpam eṉpatu oḻukka nūṟpākupāṭu. aṟam eṉavē, illaṟam tuṟavaṟam iraṇṭum aṭaṅkum. tuṟavaṟam - vīṭṭuneṟi.
aṟamporu ḷiṉpam vīṭeṉṉum pākupāṭu tamiḻaratē. āriyarē avaṟṟit tarmam, arttam, kāmam, mōkṣam eṉa vaṭamoḻiyil moḻi peyarttuk koṇṭaṉar.
tamiḻar aṟattiṟ ciṟantiruntami, tolkāppiyattil āṅkāṅkuk kūṟappaṭum aṟavoḻukkaṅkaḷā laṟiyappaṭum.
poruḷkaḷi akam puṟam eṉṟu pakuppatē tamiḻa muṟi yeṉṟum, aṟamporu ḷiṉpam vīṭeṉṟu pakuppatu āriya muṟiyeṉṟum cilar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.  oru moḻiyilēyē nūlukkum kalikkum eṟṟapaṭi, vevvēṟu vakiyāyp poruḷkaḷ pakukkappaṭum. ilakkaṇanūl oṉṟilēyē, eḻuttatikārattil uyir, mey, uyirmey eṉṟu mūṉṟu vakiyākavum, collatikārattil poruḷ iṭaṅ kālam ciṉi kuṇam toḻil eṉṟu āṟu vakiyākavum, maṟṟōr muṟiyil uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi eṉṟu iruvakiyākavum poruḷkaḷ pakukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  tarukkanūlil ēḻākavum patiṉāṟākavum pakukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ākiyāl, akam puṟam eṉpatu  poruḷa tikārap pākupāṭeṉṟum, aṟm poru ḷiṉpam vīṭeṉpatu oḻukkanūṟ pākupāṭeṉṟum aṟintukoḷka.
coṟkaḷ ilakkaṇa muṟiyil, peyar viṉi yiṭi yuriyeṉ ṟum, colliyal muṟiyil, iyal tiri tici vaṭa coṟkaḷeṉ ṟum, oḻukkanūliṟ poy kuṟaḷi kaṭuñcol payaṉilcol leṉṟum, koṇmuṭipu(cittānta) nūlil vēṟuvakiyākavum pakukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  iṅṅaṉamē poruḷakaḷu meṉka.
aintiṇiyum taṉittaṉi kūṟappaṭumpōtu kuṟiñci, mulli, marutam, neytal, pāli yeṉṟu peyar peṟumeṉṟum, kōviyāyk kūṟappaṭum pōtu kaḷavu, kaṟpu eṉa iru ki kōḷkaḷākak kūṟappaṭumeṉṟum kōvi yilum,muṉṉiya nāṉkum muṉṉataṟ keṉpa eṉpatāl kikkiḷi muṉṉum, kūṭṭat tiṉāl aintiṇi iṭiyum, kūṭṭam nīṭikkātu maṭalēṟṟam nikaḻiṉ peruntiṇi piṉṉum amintirut talum, muṟiyē nikaḻkiṉṟa puṇartal pirital iruttal allatu iraṅkal, ūṭal eṉpavi muṟiyē kuṟiñci, pāli, mulli, neytal, marutam eṉṉum aintiṇikaḷā yamitalum aṟika.
uḷanūl (Psychology)
tolkāppiya meyppāṭṭiyalil,
eḷḷal iḷami pētimi maṭaṉeṉ
ṟuḷḷap paṭṭa nakināṉ keṉpa  (4)
iḷivē iḻavē acivē vaṟumiyeṉa
viḷivil koḷki aḻuki nāṉkē  (5)
mūppē piṇiyē varuttam meṉmiyō
ṭiyāppuṟa vanta iḷivaral nāṉkē  (6)
putumi perumi ciṟumi ākkamoṭu
matimi cālā maruṭki nāṉkē  (7)
aṇaṅkē vilaṅkē kalvartam iṟiyeṉap
piṇaṅkal cālā accam nāṉkē  (8)
calvi taṟukaṇ pukaḻmi koṭiyeṉac 
collap paṭ perumitam nāṉkē  (9)
uṟuppaṟi kuṭikōḷ alikoli eṉṟa
veṟuppa vanta vekuḷi nāṉkē  (10)

celvam pulavē puṇarvuviṉi yāṭṭeṉa
allal nītta uvaki nāṉkē  (11)
eṉṟum piṟavāṟu nuṭpamāy ivaṟṟi virittum kūṟiyirup patāl, tamiḻarkku uḷanūluṇarcci yiruntami yaṟiyappaṭum.
kaṇitanūl (Arithmetic)
kaṇ + itam = kaṇitam. kaṇ = karutu, mati, aḷa. kaṇ + iyam = kaṇṇiyam (matippu). kaṇ - kaṇi, to calculate.fÂ + ki = kaṇiki (tāḷaṅkaṇittāṭum kūtti). kaṇi + aṉ = kaṇiyaṉ (jōtiṭaṉ). kaṇ + akku = kaṇakku. akku eṉpatu ōr īṟu. kā. ilakku ili, patakku, kaṇi - kuṇi - aḷaviṭu. kaṇ, kaṇi eṉṉum teṉcoṟkaḷiyē gan, gani eṉa urappi yolittu vaṭacollākak kāṭṭuvar. kaṇṇutal eṉṉuñ col, akakkaṇṇiṉ toḻilik kuṟittalāl vaṭamoḻi vaṭivaṅkaḷ teṉcoṟkaḷiṉ tiripē eṉpatu teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkum.  kaṇpaṭi kaṇṇiya, kapili kaṇṇiya eṉṟu tolkāp piyattilēyē varutal kāṇka.
ari, kuṟi, kalam paṉi, kā, patakku, tūṇi, uri nāḻi eṉṉum aḷavup peyarkaḷum niṟippeyarkaḷum, tolkāppiyattil, tokimarapu, uyirmayaṅkiyal eṉṉum iyalkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
tokimarapil, 28ām nūṟpāvil, aḷavuppeyarkaḷum niṟippeyarkaḷum ka ca ta pa na ma a u eṉṉum oṉpateḻuttukaḷil toṭaṅkumeṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. nacciṉārkkiṉiyar ataṟku,
eṭuttukkāṭṭum: kalam cāṭi tūti pāṉi nāḻi maṇṭi vaṭṭi akal uḻakku ivi aḷavu. kaḻañcu cīrakam toṭi palam niṟi māvari anti iviniṟi....immioraṭi iṭā eṉa variyaṟi kūṟātaṉavuṅ koḷka.... tēyavaḻakkāy oruñār orutuvali eṉpaṉavuṅ koḷka eṉṟu uira kūṟiyuḷḷār. puḷḷimayaṅkiyal 98āmnūṟpāvil, ai,am,pal’ eṉamuṭiyum cila pēreṇṇup peyarkaḷ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷa.
avi tāmari kuvaḷi caṅkam veḷḷam āmpal mutaliyaṉa. paripāṭal 2ām pāṭṭilneytaluṅ kuvaḷiyum āmpalum caṅkamum eṉṟu cila mikap pēreṇkaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. caṅkam ilakkaṅ kōṭiyeṉṟum, tāmari kōṭākōṭi yeṉṟum collappaṭukiṉṟatu.
tolkāppiyattiṟ collappaṭṭuḷḷa tuṇmiyāyiṉ, paṇṭit tamiḻil āyirattukku mēṟpaṭṭa patiṟṟiṭap pēreṇkaṭkut taṉippeyarilli. itaṉāl, ataṟkumēl tamiḻarkku eṇṇat teriyāteṉṟu koḷḷaṟka. āyiram, nūṟu, pattu eṉṉum eṇṇuppeyarkaḷiyē uṟaḻntu, ilakkatti nūṟāyirameṉṟum pattilakkattip pattu nūṟāyirameṉṟum, kōṭiyi nūṟū nūṟāyira meṉṟum kūṟiṉar eṉṟē koḷḷap paṭum. tolkāppiyattil patiṉūṟāyiram vari kūṟappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. āṅkilattil, hundred thousand, two hundred million  eṉṟu iṉṟum kūṟutal kāṇka.
ilakkam (lakṣam, Skt,. lakh, Eng.), e kōṭi (kōṭṭi, Skt., crore, Eng.)  eṉṉum peyarkaḷ piṟkālattaṉavā yirukkalām. āṉāl, avi intiyāviṉiṉṟē tōṉṟiṉatākat terikiṉṟatu.  kōṭi eṉṟu tamiḻilum, kuṭōṭu eṉṟu intu tāṉiyilum, kurōr (crore) eṉṟu āṅkilattilum vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka.
ilakkam kōṭi yeṉpaṉa tamiḻp peyarkaḷāyiṉ, muṟiyē, periya ilakkam (eṇ) eṉṟum, kaṭiciyeṇ eṉṟum poruḷ paṭuvaṉavākum. ivaṟṟuḷ ilakkam muṉṉum kōṭi piṉṉum tōṉṟiṉaveṉpati, āyirappattu pattāyiram, pattāyirappattu nūṟāyiram, nūṟāyirappattu pattu nūṟāyiram, pattu nūṟāyirappattu pattilakkaṅkōṭi (pattilakkam allatu kōṭi) eṉṉum eṇcuvaṭi vāypāṭṭāl aṟiyalām.
paṇṭittamiḻar kaṇitattil mikac ciṟantavar eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟāka iraṇṭōr aḷavi vāypāṭukaḷi iṅkuk kāṇka.

makara vaḷavoṭu nikaralu murittē  (76)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyak kuṟṟiya lukarap puṇariyal nūṟpāvil mā eṉṉum aḷavu kūṟappaṭṭiruppatāl, kīḻvāyilakkattic cērnta ēṉiyaḷavukaḷum tolkāppiyar kālattil vaḻaṅkiṉa veṉṟa koḷḷappaṭum.

cilappatikārattil, paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟiṟkum kumariyāṟṟukku miṭi yilirunta cēymi eḻunūṟṟuk kāvatam eṉṟu aṭiyārkku nallār kūṟiyatu, iv vaḷavu paṟṟiyatāyiṉ, atu ēṟattāḻa 1600 milkaḷākum. paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟukkut teṟkum kumariyāṟṟukku vaṭakkum irunta nilattiyuñ cērttuk kaṇippiṉ, aḻintu pōṉa tamiḻnāṭu evvakiyiṉum 2000 milukkuk kuṟiyā teṉṉalām.  aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ, iṟṟi yintiyā aḷavu oru perunilam teṉkaṭalil mūḻkiyatākum.
cōḻa irācciyam muḻumiyum, mutal irācarāca cōḻaṉatu āṭcikkālattil orumuṟiyum, mutal kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉatu āṭcikkālattil orumuṟiyum, mūṉṟām kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉatu āṭcikkālattil orumuṟiyum aḷakkappaṭṭatu. ivi muṟiyē, ki.pi. 1001, ki.pi. 1086, ki.pi. 1216ām āṇṭukaḷil nikaḻntavikaḷākum.
nilam aḷanta kōli ulakaḷanta kōl eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvar. ikkōl patiṉāṟu cāṇ nīḷamuṭiyatu... nilaṅkaḷ nūṟu kuḻi koṇṭatu oru māvākavum irupatu mākoṇṭatu oru vēli yākavum aḷakkappeṟṟaṉa.... annāḷil nilattiṉ ettuṇic ciṟu pakutiyum viṭāmal nuṭpamāka aḷakkap peṟṟuḷḷatu eṉpatu iṟiyili nīṅku nilam mukkālē iraṇṭu mākāṇi arikkāṇi muntirikikkīḻ ariyē iraṇṭumā mukkāṇik kīḻ mukkālē nāṉkumā arikkāṇi muntirikik kīḻ nāṉku māviṉāl iṟikaṭṭiṉa kāṇikkaṭaṉ eṉpataṉāl viḷaṅkum.  (mutaṟ kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ, pak.84,85. cōḻavamica carittirac curukkam, pak. 58) iṅkuk kuṟikkappaṭṭa aḷavu 1/52428500000 veḷiyākum.
vāṉanūl (Astronomy) 
nāḷkōḷ (puṟat.11) eṉavē nāṇmīṉum, (Lunar asterism), ñāyiṟu tiṅkaḷ (kiḷavi.58) eṉavē, eḻukōḷum (Planets) avaṟṟāṟ peyar peṟum ēḻu kiḻamikaḷum (days), ōriyum (Signs of the Zodiac) (kaḷa.45) eṉavē irāciyum, kārum māli yum (akat.6.). kūtir yāmam (akat.7). paṉiyetir paruvamum (akat. 8),vikaṟi viṭiyal (akat.9.9), eṟpāṭu 9akat.10), naṇpakal vēṉiloṭu (akat.10). piṉpaṉi (akat.12) eṉavē perumpoḻutum  (seasons) ciṟupoḻutum (Divisions of the day)  tolkāppapiyar kālat tamiḻarkkut terintum vaḻakkilu miruntami yaṟiyappaṭum.
(acuviṉi mutal rēvatiyīṟāṉa) irupattēḻu nāḷmīṉ (naṭcattiram) kaṭkum paṇṭikkālattil vaḻaṅkivanta tamiḻp peyarkaḷ, muṟiyē puravi, aṭuppu, āral, cakaṭu, māṉṟali, mūtiri, kaḻi, kāṟkuḷam, kaṭcevi, koṭunukam, kaṇi, uttaram, ki, aṟuvi, viḷakku, muṟam, paṉi, tuḷaṅkoḷi, kuruku, uṭi kuḷam, kaṭikkuḷam, mukkōl, kākki, cekku, nāḻi, muracu, tōṇi eṉpaṉa. ivaṟṟukku vēṟu peyarkaḷu muṇṭu. avaṟṟi nikaṇṭukaḷiṟ kaṇṭukoḷka. veḷḷi, uṭu, viṇmīṉ eṉpavi potuvāṉa naṭcattirap peyarkaḷākum.
kōḷkaḷ ēḻeṉṟē tamiḻarkoṇṭu, avaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷāl ēḻunāṭ koṇṭa vārameṉṉum aḷavi ēṟpaṭuttiṉar.  āṉāl, āriyar kōḷkaḷiṉ tokiyi mikuttuk kāṭṭa vēṇṭumeṉṟu, katiravaṉ maṟiyum tiṅkaḷ maṟivu mākiyavaṟṟit tam tiripuṇarcciyāl irukōḷkaḷākak karuti avaṟṟukku rāku kētu eṉṟu peyariṭṭu, avaṟṟi eḻukōḷkaḷōṭu kūṭṭi navakkirakameṉṟaṉar.
putaṉ caṉi eṉṉum iru peyarkaṭkum, paṇṭikkālattil vaḻaṅkivanta tamiḻp peyarkaḷ aṟivaṉ kāri eṉpaṉa.
paṉṉiru mātaṅkaṭkum ippōtu vaḻaṅkivarum vaṭamoḻip peyarkaṭkup patilāka, paṉṉīr ōri(rāci)yiṉ peyarkaḷē maliyāḷa nāṭṭiṟpōlap paṇṭit tamiḻnāṭṭilum vaḻaṅkivantaṉa. paṉṉīr ōrippeyarkaḷil mēṭam, iṭapam, kaṭakam, kaṉṉi, tulām, kumpam, mīṉam, eṉṉum ēḻum tamiḻc coṟkaḷē.  ivaṟṟuḷ mutalāṟukku muṟiyē, takar, kuṇṭi, alavaṉ, maṭanti, tūkku, kuṭam eṉṟu peyaruṇṭu. ēṉiya mituṉam, ciṅkam, viruccikam, taṉucu, makaram eṉṉum aintum tamiḻil, muṟiyē, iraṭṭi, āḷi, tēḷ, vil, cuṟā eṉappaṭum.
paṉṉiru mātaṅkaḷum orippeyar peṟumāṟu
1. cittiri - mēṭam		2. vikāci - iṭapam   
3. āṉi - iraṭṭi   		4. āṭi - kaṭakam
5. āvaṇi - āḷi		6. puraṭṭāci - kaṉṉi
7. aippaci - tuli		8. kārttiki - tēḷ
9. mārkaḻi - vil		10. ti - makaram
11. māci - kumpam		12. paṅkuṉi – mīṉam
mātam eṉpatu mati eṉpatiṉiṉṟum piṟanta tamiḻc collē. mati -mātam(ta.)māsam (va.) mati =cantiraṉ. kālatti matippataṟkuk karuviyāyiruppatu mati. matittal aḷaviṭutal. o.nō: moon - month. mati eṉpatiṉiṉṟu mātam eṉṉum vaṭivam tōṉṟu mēyaṉṟi māsam eṉṉum vaṭivan tōṉṟātu. ta-sa, pōli. tiṅkaḷ eṉṉum col oru kiḻamip peyarātaliṉ, mātam eṉṉum collē mātattik kuṟippatākum. oru vaḷarpiṟiyum oru tēypiṟiyum cērntatu oru mātam.  matiyāluṇṭākum kāla aḷavu mātameṉappaṭṭatu.  mati eṉṉum peyarum mātattik kuṟikkum. vaḷarpiṟikkālatti oḷippakkameṉṟum, tēypiṟik kālatti iruṭpakkameṉṟum (cilap.23:133) muṉṉōr kūṟiṉar.  ivaṟṟiyē, muṟiyē, cukkilapakṣameṉṟum  kiruṣṇapakṣa meṉṟam, nilimoḻikaḷi moḻipeyarttum varumoḻikaḷi eḻuttup peyarttuṅ koṇṭaṉar āriyar.
ōrippeyarkaḷiṉ mūlaṅkaḷāvaṉa:
mēḻakam - mēṭakam - mēṭam - mēṣam (va. ) o.nō: puḻali - puṭali, nāḻuri - nāṭuri, viṭi - viṭapam (vruṣapam, va.) - iṭapam, kum - kumpu - kumpam. kumpu + am = kumpam. kumputal - kuvital. miṉ - mīṉ - mīṉam. o.nō: tiṉ - tīṉ.
kaṭakam (naṇṭu) eṉpatu vaṭṭamāṉatu eṉṉum poruḷatu. tōṭkaṭakam, kaṭakappeṭṭi eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷi nōkkuka. nāḻiki vaṭṭilik kuṟikkum kaṭiki eṉṉuñ collum kaṭakam eṉpataṉmaṟuvaṭivamē.  vaṭṭamāṉatu vaṭṭil. kaṭiki + āram = kaṭikiyāram - kaṭikāram. tōṭkaṭakam tōḷvaḷivi eṉṟum kaṭiki eṉṟum vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka. kaṭakam oru parumip peyarumākum (Augmentative)
tuli eṉṉum coṟkup pakuti, tōl eṉṟu intiyil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. vaṭanāṭṭil, āriyar varumuṉ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷē vaḻaṅki vantamiyāl cinti, inti, vaṅkam mutaliya vaṭanāṭṭu moḻikaḷil pala tirāviṭac coṟkaḷ kalantuḷḷaṉa. 
mēlum, oru moḻiyiṉ tāyvaḻakkil, oru poruṭkuriya pala coṟkaḷil, vaḻakkaṟṟaviyellām kiḷivaḻakkukaḷiltāṉ vaḻaṅkum. ituvē vaḻak kaṟṟacol vaḻaṅkal itu oru tāyvīṭṭiluḷḷa vaḻaṅkāta kalaṅkaḷiyellām makkaḷ koṇṭupōy vaḻaṅkutal pōṉṟatu.
tamiḻiluḷḷa nōṭṭam eṉṉum toḻiṟpeyariṉ vērāṉa nōṭu eṉpatu, kaṉṉaṭattil vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka.
tullipam, tuli, tulinā, tulā, tulām, tulāṉ, tulākkōl, kittulā, āḷēṟuntulā eṉṟu iruvaḻakkilum, tuli eṉṉum collum ataṉ piṟa vaṭivaṅkaḷum toṉṟutoṭṭut tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟaṉa.
iḷavēṉil, mutuvēṉil, kār, kūtir, muṉpaṉi, piṉpaṉi eṉṉum aṟuḷavakip paruvaṅkaḷiyum, muṟiyē, vacantam kirīṣmam, varṣam, carat, hēmantam, ciciram eṉṟu moḻi peyarttuk koṇṭaṉar āriyar. vacantam (Sáing), kōṭi (Summer), vaṟaḷi (Autumn), māri (Winter) eṉṉum nālvakip paruvaṅkaḷtām āriyanāṭu eṉṉum vaṭanāṭṭiṟkēṟkum. iḷavēṉil mutaliya aṟuvakip paruvaṅkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭiṟkē yuriyaṉa.  itaṉāl, mūlam eviyeṉṟum moḻipeyarppu eviyeṉṟum aṟintukoḷḷalām.  iṅṅaṉamē āriya moḻi peyarppukaḷ cilaviṭaṅkaḷil maṟintiruppiṉum cilaviṭaṅkaḷil veḷiyāy viṭumeṉka.
nāḷum puḷḷum piṟavaṟṟi ṉimittamum eṉṟu  tolkāppiyattil (puṟat.30) kūṟiyiruppatāl kuṟi nūlum (Astrology)  tolkāppiyar kālattilirunta miyaṟiyappaṭum. 
vāṉanūlaṟiñaṉukkut tamiḻil kaṇiyaṉ eṉṟu peyar. kaṇiyaṉ kālattik kaṇippavaṉ. paṇṭit tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḷḷuvarē perumpālum kaṇiyarāyiruntaṉar. tamiḻar kaṇitattil vallavarāyiruntatiṉāl vāṉa nūlilum vallavarāyiruntaṉar.
ippōtu vaḻaṅkum 60 āṇṭup peyarkaḷ cāli vākaṉaṉ eṉpavaṉāl, allatu kaṉiṣkaṉāl (The Hindu, March 10, 1940, p. 7.) ki.pi. 78-il ēṟpaṭṭavi. ivi vaṭanāṭṭaracaṉāl ēṟpaṭṭaviyātaliṉ vaṭamoḻip peyarkaḷāyuḷḷaṉa. 60 āṇṭuc cakkaram cuṟṟic cuṟṟi varuvatiṉālum, mikak kuṟukiyatiṉālum, carittira nūṟkup payaṉpaṭātu. rutrōtkāri eṉṟu kūṟiṉāl, enta rutrōtkāri yeṉṟu aṟiyappaṭāmiyāl, iv vaḷaviṉmipaṟṟit tamiḻukkoru kuṟiyumilli yeṉka.

tolkāppiyattil,

eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta collā kummē  (ecca. 5)
eṉṟum, aṟuvakit tiripukaḷum (ec. 7), mūvakik kuṟikaḷum (ecca.57) viṉi payaṉ mey uru eṉṟa nālvaki yuvamaṅkaṭku vevvē ṟurupukaḷum (uva. 12-6) kūṟiyiruppatālum, piṟavaṟṟālum, paṇṭit tamiḻarkkuc colliyaluṇarcci yiruntamiyaṟiyap paṭum. oru moḻipaṟṟiyatu colliyalum pala moḻipaṟṟiyatu moḻinūlumāku meṉṟaṟika.
tarukkanūl (Logic)
tarukkutal oruvaṉ taṉṉi mikuttukkāṭṭutal, taṉṉoṭum avaḷoṭum tarukkiya puṇarttu eṉṟu tolkāppiyat tilēyē (akat.53) vantiruttal kāṇka. tarukku - cerukku. ta - ca, a - e, pōlikaḷ. (o.nō: ōti - oci, cattāṉ - cettāṉ.)
tarukku = am = tarukkam. tarukkattiṟku ēraṇam eṉṟum peyaruṇṭu.  ēr + aṇam = ēraṇam. ēraṇam eḻucci. tarukkam eṉṉum teṉcolliyē, tarkkam eṉṟu eḻuti vaṭacol lākak kāṭṭuvar āriyar.
tarukkam eṉpatu oru coṟpōr. oruvaṉ taṉ koḷkiyi nāṭṭi etiriyi velvatē tarukkam. velvatellām vāki. vākip potuvilakkaṇamāṉatu,
tāvil koḷkit tattam kūṟṟip 
pākupaṭa mikutip paṭuttal  (tol. puṟat.15)
akattiyat tarukka nūṟpākkaḷ
kīḻvarum nūṟpākkaḷ tarukkap pulavar vaṭivēlceṭṭiyār patippitta tarkka paripāṣi eṉṉum nūliṟutiyiṟ kaṇṭavi. ivi uṇmiyil akattiyar iyaṟṟiyaviyāyiṉ, tol kāppiyar kālattil tarukkanūlun tamiḻiliruntami yaṟiyappaṭum.
	1.	poruḷkuṇaṅ karumam potucciṟap poṟṟumi
	yiṉmi yuṭaṉ poru ḷēḻeṉa moḻipa.
2.	maṇpuṉa laṉalkāl veḷipoḻu tāci
	yāṉmā maṉatō ṭoṉpatum poruḷē.
3. 	vaṭivañ cuviyiru nāṟṟa mūṟeṇ
	ṇaḷavu vēṟṟumi puṇarcci pirivu
	muṉmi piṉmi tiṇmi nekiḻcci
	cikkeṉa lōci yuṇarcci yiṉpan 
	tuṉpam viruppam veṟuppu muyaṟci
	yaṟamaṟam vātaṉiyoṭu kuṇamaṟu nāṉkē.
4.	eḻumpal viḻutal vaḷita ṉimirta
	ṉaṭatta luṭaṉē karumami vakittē.
5. 	potumi mēlkī ḻeṉaviru vakiyē.
6. 	maṉṉiya poruḷiṉ kaṇṇavā yavaṟṟiṉ
	vēṟṟumi terippaṉa palavāñ ciṟappē
7. 	oṟṟumi yāppaḵ toṉṟē yeṉka.
8. 	muṉṉiṉmi piṉṉiṉmi muṟṟu miṉmi
	yoṉṟiṉoṉ ṟiṉmiyeṉ ṟiṉmi nāṉkē
9. 	maṇṇī raṉalkāṉ muṟiyē nāṟṟan
	taṭpam veppa mūṟṟa māki
	meypporu ḷaḻiporuṇ mēvu meṉka.
10. 	aṇukkaṇ meypporuḷ kāriya maḻiporuḷ
	pirutivi nittiya vanittiya vaṇampeṟum
	niliyaṇup poruṇili yillātu kāriyam
11. 	atuvē:
	uṭalpoṟi viṭaya mūvakip paṭumē
	tammaṉōr yākki maṇkūṟ ṟuṭampu
	nāṟṟaṅ kavarvatu nāciyi ṉuṉiyē
	maṇkal mutaliya viṭaya mākum.
12. 	nīriṟi varippiṟ kaṭṭunī ruṭampu
	cuvitiṟaṅ kavarvatu nāvi ṉuṉiyē
	kaṭalyā ṟāti viṭaya mākum.
13. 	katirōṉ varippiṟ kaṭṭaṉa luṭampē
	yuruvaṅ kavarvatu karumaṇi viḻiyē
	maṇviṇ vayiṟā karanāl viṭayam.
14. 	vaḷiyiṟi varippiṟ kaṭṭukā luṭampē
	yūṟṟaṅ kavarvatu mīntō leṉka
	viṭaya maramuta lacitaṟ kētuvē
	pirāṇa ṉuṭalakat tiyaṅkuṅ kāṟṟē.
15. 	vicumpē kālan ticiyō ṭāṉmā
	maṉamivi yintu nittiyap poruḷē.
16. 	ocip paṇpiṟ ṟākā yammē.
17. 	iṟappumutal vaḻakkiṟ kētuk kālam.
18. 	kiḻakkumutal vaḻakkiṟ kētut ticiyē.
19. 	aṟivup paṇpiṟ ṟāṉmā veṉka
	viṟiyē yīcaṉmuṟ ṟaṟivaṉōr mutalē
	yuyirtā ṉuṭaṟoṟum vevvē ṟākum.
20. 	maṉamaṇu vaṭivāy varumiṉ pāti
	yaṟitaṟ kiṉṟi yamiyāk karuvi
	yākip palavā yaḻiviṉ ṟuṟumē.
in nūṟpākkaḷiyē, nuṇṇaṟivuḷḷa oruvar oru nūlāka virittuviṭalām.
in nūṟpākkaḷ ciṟappika muṟiyic cērntavi. ciṟappikam (vicēṭikam), muṟiyikam (niyāyam) eṉṉum iruvakit tarukka muṟikaḷuḷ, muṉṉatē ciṟantateṉka.
	toḻiṟkalikaḷ (Arts)
ciṟpam (Architecture)
muḻumutal araṇam (puṟat.8), āreyil (puṟat.10), matiṟkuṭumi (puṟat.10) mutaliya kuṟippukaḷāl, tolkāppiyar kālattil ciṟpattoḻil tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ ciṟantiruntami yuṇarappaṭum.
cuvar, matil, pataṇam, iñci, akappā, purici, eyil, cō mutaliya matilvakikaḷum, ñāyil, cūṭṭu, ēppuḻi mutaliya matiluṟuppukaḷum, eḻunili māṭamum paṇṭit tamiḻariṉ ciṟpakkalic ciṟappi uṇarttuvaṉavākum. ivaṟṟiṉ virivic cilappatikārattuṭ kaṇṭukoḷka.
paṇṭit tamiḻar nāṭakavaraṅku amitta nuṭpam (cilap.113-3:6) mika viyakkattakkatu.
vaḷiviṟpoṟi, karuviralukam, kallumiḻkavaṇ, pākaṭu kuḻici, kāypoṉṉuli, kalliṭukūṭi, tūṇṭil, toṭakku, āṇ ṭaliyaṭuppu, kavi, kaḻu, aiyavittulām, kipeyarūci, ceṉṟeṟi ciral, paṉṟi, paṉi, nūṟṟu varik kolli, taḷḷiveṭṭi, kaḷiṟṟuppoṟi, viḻuṅkumpāmpu, kaḻukupoṟi, pulippoṟi, kuṭappāmpu, cakaṭappoṟi, takarppoṟi, arinūṟpoṟi (cilap. pak. 413-415) mutaliya matiṟpoṟi vakikaḷ, paṇṭit tamiḻariṉ paṭimikkaliyiyum (Sculpture) cūḻcciyak kaliyiyum (Engi-neering) terivippaṉavākum. paṭimi - patumi, pāviviḷakku kantiṟpāvi mutaliya peyarkaḷiyum nōkkuka.
ciṟpam paṭimikkali mutaliyavaṟṟālum, nāṭakavaraṅkiṉ eḻiṉi yālum ōviyanūlum (Drawing) paṇṭit tamiḻarkkut terin tiruntami collāmē yuṇarappaṭum. ciṟpanūl oviyanūl eṉṟu irunūlkaḷ kaṭikkaḻakak kālattiliruntaṉavāka, aṭi yārkku nallār araṅkēṟṟu kātiyuriyiṟ kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār.
uḻavu (Agriculture)
uḻavu paṇṭit tamiḻarkkut terintiruntamiyi oruvarum aiyuṟār. āyiṉum, atippaṟṟiya cila kuṟippukaḷ aṟiyat takkaṉa.
ērōr kaḷavaḻi eṉpatu tolkāppiyam (puṟat.16). Agriculture eṉṉum āṅkilappeyar tamiḻ mūlattatākavē terikiṉṟatu.
Agriculture, L. agricultura - ager, a field, cultura, cultivation - colo, to cultivate.
akaram marutanilattūr. marutanilam vayaṟpāṅku. pārppaṉar marutanilattil taṅkiṉatiṉālēyē, avar kuṭiyiruppu ak kirākāram eṉappaṭṭatu. akkira + akaram = akkirākaram. akkiram = nuṉi, mutal, talimi.
akara māyiram antaṇark kīyileṉ
eṉṟār tirumūlar.
E. acre, A.S. aceer, Ger. acker, Gk. agros, L. ager, Skt. ajra.
kal = tōṇṭu. kal + vi = kalvi. kalvi eṉṉum peyar mutalāvatu tōṇṭutal eṉṉum poruḷil uḻavik kuṟittatu. piṉpu, nūṟkalvikkuc ciṟappup peyarāyiṟṟu. māntaṉ mutaṉmutaṟ kaṟṟa kalvi uḻavē. kal - colo, to till.
toḻil eṉṉum peyarum mutalāvatu uḻaviyē kuṟittatu. toḷ + il = toḷil - toḻil.
o.nō: poḷ + il = poḷil - poḻil. poḷ - poḷi. poḷital veṭṭutal. (poḷ - poḻ) poḷḷa (veṭṭa)p paṭuvatu toḻil. poḻil = cōli. paṇṭit tamiḻakam muḻuvatum peruñcōliyāy allatu kāṭāyiruntatu. kāṭu veṭṭi nāṭākkappaṭṭatu. akattiyar kāṭukeṭuttu nāṭākkip potiyiṉkaṇirun tami nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟutal kāṇka.
tamiḻakam muḻuvatum oru peruñcōliyā yiruntatiṉālēyē poḻil eṉṉum peyar ulakam allatu nāṭu eṉṟu poruḷ paṭuvatāyiṟṟu. nāvalan taṇpoḻil vaṇpukaḻ mūvar taṇ poḻil, ēḻpoḻil eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷi nōkkuka.
toḷ = tōṇṭu. toḷ - toṭu = tōṇṭu. toḷ - till, to cultivate.bjhŸStJ toḻil.
māntaṉ mutalāvatu ceyta peruntoḻil uḻavē. uḻaviṉ peyarāṉa toḻil eṉpatu, piṟkālattil ellāt toḻil kaṭkum potuppeyarāyiṟṟu.
cey = vayal. ceyvatu cey. naṉcey, puṉcey eṉṉum peyar kaḷi nōkkuka. ceyki = vayalvēli. paṇ = cey. paṇṇuvatu paṇṇi. paṇṇi = vayal.
mutalāvatu kiyiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa toḻil uḻavu eṉṉuṅ karuttuppaṟṟiyē, uḻavarik kiceytūṇ māliyavar eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
mutalāvatu tōṉṟiya oru poruḷiṉ allatu toḻiliṉ peyar, piṟkālattil ataṉ iṉattiṟkup potuppeyarāy vaḻaṅkutal iyalpē. tōṭu eṉṉum ōlip peyar, piṟkālattil ataṟkup patilāyaṇippaṭum kammal (kamalam) vakikkup potup peyarāy vaḻaṅkuvatiyum; villēruḻavar, col lēruḻavar, tērōr kaḷavaḻi yeṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷiyum nōkkuka.
paḻa nārvījiyam (Old Norse), āṅkilō cāksaṉ eṉṟa moḻi kaḷil, muṟiyē, erfidhi, earfodh eṉṟa coṟkaḷ mutalāvatu uḻavik kuṟittup piṉpu uḻippik kuṟittaṉa veṉṟum. lattīṉil ars, artis eṉṟa coṟkaḷ mutalāvatu uḻavik kuṟittup piṉpu kalip potuppeya rāyiṟṟeṉṟum, ac coṟkaṭku mūlam ar(to plough) eṉpatu eṉṟum mutalāvatu kaṟpikkappaṭṭa kali uḻavēyeṉṟum mākkacu mullar kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka. (L.S.L. p.294)
ēr = kalappi. ērttoḻil = uḻavu.
E. ear(V®.), to plough. A.S. erian, L. aro, Gk. aroo - root ar. to plough. E. arable, adj.
E. share - koḻu. M.E. schar, A.S. scear, Ger. schar, schaar.
kāṟu = koḻu.
ka-ca, pōli. o.nō: tiki - tici (tēvā. 308:1), ikal - ical. cēmpar akarātiyil shear (to cut) eṉpatu share eṉpataṉ mūlamākak kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. aṅṅaṉamāyiṉum, shear  eṉpatu ciṟi eṉṉum tamiḻc colli ottiruttal kāṇka.
kittoḻil (Industries)
tāḻ, vāḷ, vēl, kaḻal, tōl (kēṭakam), kuṭi, kaṇi, tēr, yāḻ, iḻi, tuṭi, poṉ mutaliya peyarkaḷum, ūḻaṇiti varal (mey.14) eṉṉun toṭarum tolkāppiyattil kūṟappaṭ ṭiruppatāl, atu iyaṟṟappaṭṭa kālattil, tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟ kittoḻil ciṟantiruntati yuṇarappaṭum.
ak kittoḻil pala tiṟattaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ necavu paṟṟik kāṇka.
necavu (Weaving)
uṭipeyart tuṭuttal  eṉpatu tolkāppiyam (mey. 14). parutti necavu mutaṉmutal intiyāviltāṉ ceyyappaṭṭa teṉṟum, aṅkiruntē mēṉāṭukaḷukkupa paraviyateṉṟum vayavar cāṇ mārcal kūṟukiṟār.
paṇṭikkālac carittirāciriyarellām, mēṉāṭukaḷ intiyā viṉiṉṟum iṟakkumati ceyta poruḷkaḷuḷ, tuṇi oṉṟākat tavaṟātu kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉar.
18ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakutivari, intiyāviṉiṉṟē mēṉāṭukaṭkut tuṇi ēṟṟumatiyāṉami, piṉvaruṅ kuṟippiṉā laṟiyalām.
taṟkāla intiyāviṉ vaṟumikku maṟṟoru peruṅkāraṇam, airōppāvil poṟiviṉit tōṟṟattiṉālum, tiruntiya muṟic ceyporuḷ viṉiyiṉ paravaliṉālum ēṟpaṭṭa kit toḻil vēvikkuṟivē. airōppā, intiyariṉ contat tuṟikaḷil, iṉṉum, eṇṇukkeṭṭāta toṉṟu toṭṭu evaṟṟukku avarkaḷic cārntiruntōmō aviyum, avar kaḷukkē vitappā yuriyavumāṉa kittoḻilkaḷilum cey poruḷ viṉikaḷilum, avarkaḷi vellak kaṟṟuk 
koṇṭa tiṉāl, uṇmiyil iṉi yorupōtum etaṟkum intiyāvic cāntiruppatilli.  uṇmiyil, kāriyaṅkaḷ talikīḻāy māṟi viṭṭaṉa. iv veḷippāṭu intiyāvi muṟṟilum keṭuttu viṭuvatāka accuṟuttukiṉṟatu.
airōppāviṟkut tirumpuvataṟkuc caṟṟu muṉpē, kittoḻil ceyyum kūṟṟaṅkaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟiṉūṭu vaḻic ceṉṟēṉ. aṅkup paraviyirunta pāḻnilikku ētum oppākātu. vēliyaṟi kaḷellām cāttappaṭṭiruntaṉa. aṅkē vāḻum ilakkak kaṇakkāṉa necavuttoḻiṉ makkaḷ paciyāl maṭintu koṇ ṭiruntaṉar. nāṭṭiluḷḷa muṉṟavaṟṟeṇṇam (prejudice) paṟṟi, avarkaḷ avamāṉappaṭṭataṉṟip piṟa toḻiliyum mēṟ koḷḷa muṭiyātu. muntiya kālattil nūlnūṟṟut taṅkaḷ kuṭum pattik kāttuvanta, kaṇakkaṟṟa kimpeṇkaḷum piṟa peṇṭi rum vēliyiḻantu piḻippaṟṟiruntatik kaṇṭēṉa. yāṉ eṅkuc ceṉṟālum orē tuṉpattōṟṟam eṉakku etirē niṉṟatu (Hindu Manners, Customs and Ceremonies, Ch.VI.) eṉṟu 18ām nūṟṟāṇṭil micūr nāṭṭil viṭiyūḻiyam ceyta appē ṭūpāy (Abbe Dubois) kūṟukiṉṟār.
paṇṭik kālattiṟ peṇṭir nūl nūṟṟamiyi nakkīrar, kapilar, pavaṇanti mutaliyōruṅ kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar. paruttip peṇṭu eṉṟu puṟanāṉūṟu (125,326) kūṟum.
nūliṉu mayiriṉu nuḻinūṟ paṭṭiṉum 
pālvaki teriyāp paṉṉū ṟaṭukkattu
naṟumaṭi ceṟinta vaṟuki vītiyum  (cilap.14:205-7)
eṉṟu iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ kūṟuvatāl, paṇṭit tamiḻar necaviṉ perumi viḷaṅkum.
mayiriṉum eṉpataṟku elimayiriṉālum eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟiyuḷḷār aṭiyārkkunallār.
mayir niṟinta oruvaki maliyeli paṇṭit tamiḻ nāṭṭi lirunta teṉpatum, ataṉ mayirāl ciṟanta kampaḷam neyyap paṭṭa teṉpatum,
pukaḻvaric ceṉṉimēṟ pūciyiṟ periyaṉa
pavaḷamē yaṉiyaṉa paṉmayirp pēreli  (1898)
cenne ruppuṇuñ cevve limmayi
ranne ruppaḷa vāypoṟ kampalam  (2686)
eṉṟu cintāmaṇi kūṟuvatā laṟiyappaṭum. palavakit tuṇikaḷ paṇṭit tamiḻnāṭṭil neyyappaṭṭami,
tukil (āṭi): kōciyam, pītakam, paccili, arattam, nuṇṭu kil, cuṇṇam, vaṭakam, pañcu, iraṭṭu, pāṭakam, kōṅkalar, kōpam, cittirakkammi, kuruti, curiyal, pēṭakam, pariyaṭṭakkācu, vētaṅkam, puṅkar;kāḻakam, cilliki, tūriyam, paṅkam, tattiyam, vaṇṇaṭi, kavaṟṟumaṭi, nūlyāppu, tirukku, tēvāṅku, poṉṉeḻuttu, kuccari, tēvakiri, kāttūlam, iṟañci, veṇpotti, cempotti, paṇippotti (cilap.pak.379) eṉa 36 vakiyeṉṟu aṭiyārkkunallār kūṟuvatiṉiṉ ṟaṟiyalām.
āvi yaṉṉa vavirnūṟ kaliṅkam  (pat.4:169) eṉpatiṉālum, pālāṭikkum pañcāṭikkum āṭiyeṉṉum potup peyar iruttalālum, pāmpu kaḻaṟṟiya mīntōl caṭṭiyeṉap paṭutalālum, ikkālattiṟ cūḻcciyappoṟiyāl neyyum noyya āṭi vakikaḷ pōṉṟē, akkālattiṟ kittaṟiyāl tamiḻar neytu vantaṉar eṉpataṟiyappaṭum.
necavupaṟṟiya cila tamiḻccoṟkaḷ mēliyāriya moḻikaḷil vaḻaṅku kiṉṟaṉa.
paṉṉal = parutti. L. punnus, cotton, It. Panno, cloth.
koṭṭi = pañcu. Ar. qutun, It. cotone, Fr. coton, E. cotton.
koṭṭi nūṟṟal eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka. paruttik kāyiṉiṉṟu koṭṭuvatu koṭṭi.
vēṭṭi = āṭi. L. vestis, Skt. vatra, E. vesture.
veṭṭuvatu vēṭṭi. o.nō: tuṇippatu tuṇi. aṟuppatu aṟuvi. vēṭṭiyi vēṣṭi eṉṟu tamiḻarē tavaṟāy vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. itu iṉṉum vaṭamoḻikkuṭ pukavilli.
taṭukkap paḻiyavoru vēṭṭi yuṇṭu eṉṟu paṭṭiṉattār kūṟutal kāṇka. (tiruttilli,17)
cēli = (cīli) nīṇṭa allatu akaṉṟa tuṇi.
A.S.segel, E. sil.
taccu = L. texoï E. texture, anything woven. ti + cu = ticcu - taccu. taccu = tippu. taccu eṉṉum peyar cariyāṉapaṭi necavukkariya tēṉum, marakkolvēlikku vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟatu, atilum tipput toḻiluḷḷamiyiṉ. vaṇṭiyit tikka vēṇṭum eṉṟu taccar kūṟum vaḻakki nōkkuka. tittal pala vuṟuppu kaḷi oṉṟāyiṇittal. necavil nūlkaḷ oṉṟāyiṇikkap paṭalum, taccil vaṇṭiyāyiṉ kuṟaṭu āri cūṭṭu eṉṉum uṟuppukaḷum, peṭṭi nāṟkāliyāyiṉ palakikaḷum kālkaḷum oṉṟāyiṇikkappaṭalum kāṇka.
vāṇikam (Commerce)
vāṇi eṉṉum col vāynīr eṉpatiṉiṉṟu piṟantatākavum koḷḷa iṭamuṇṭu. vāynīri vāṇīr eṉpatu kocci vaḻakku. o.nō: kuṟunoy - kuṟuṇi. taṇṇīr taṇṇi eṉap paṭuvatupōla, vāṇīr vāṇi eṉappaṭum. vāynīrukku maṟupeyar coḷḷu eṉpatu. coḷucoḷu eṉṟu vaṭivatu koḷḷu. pēcat toṭaṅkum paruvattil kuḻantikaṭkuc coḷḷu vaṭiyum. coḷḷu mikutiyāy vaṭintāl pēccu mikutiyākum eṉṟoru koḷkiyuḷatu. coḷḷup peruttāl collup perukkum eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. coḷḷu - collu - col. vāynīr - vāṇīr - vāṇi = nīr, col. vāṇi - pāṇi.
kocci vaḻakkiliruntum cila coṟkaḷ koḷḷappaṭum. kā. koṇṭuvā - (koṇṭā) - koṇā - koṇar, paḻam - payam.
vāṇi eṉpataṟku vāc eṉṟu vaṭamoḻi mūlaṅkāṭṭiṉum, ataṟkum vāy eṉṉum tamiḻccollē mūlamātal kāṇka.
vāṇi + ikam = vāṇikam. vāṇi = col. collutal eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum vāṇikam eṉṉum col, vili collutal eṉṉum poruḷil paṇṭamāṟṟik kuṟippatākum. o.nō: pakartal = collutal, viṟṟal. noṭuttal = collutal, viṟṟal. noṭi = kati. noṭi = viṟpaṉi.
vāṇi eṉṉum col vaḻakkilillāmiyiṉālēyē vaṭacollākak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu, pāṇi eṉṉuñ col pōla, varttakam, viyāpāram, viciyam eṉṉum coṟkaḷē vaṭa coṟkaḷ. vāṉi (vāṉ + i) eṉṉuñ col vāṇiyeṉṟu tirintirukkalām. oliyi vāṉattiṉ paṇpāka muṉṉōr karutiṉar. vāṇikam eṉpatu vaṇikam eṉṟu kuṟukiyum vaḻaṅkum.
vāṇikam mutalāvatu uḷnāṭṭu vaṇikam ayalnāṭṭu vaṇikam eṉa irutiṟappaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ ayalnāṭṭu vaṇikamē ciṟantatu. atuvum nilavāṇikam nīrvāṇikam eṉa irutiṟappaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ nīrvāṇikamē ciṟantatu.
tamiḻar paṇṭikkālattil nīrvāṇikattiṟ ciṟantiruntamikku akaccāṉṟum puṟaccāṉṟum nirampavuḷa.
